### [1] [The exceptional set of the Goldbach problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27282)
*Lasse Grimmelt,Gautami Bhowmik*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究整数表示为至多两个素数之和的例外数估计，大部分内容为综述，涵盖Hardy-Littlewood圆法、Montgomery-Vaughan (1975)与Pintz (2018)的改进，并给出一个新的显式大弧公式以及稀疏版Hardy-Littlewood猜想下例外零点不存在的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索整数表示为至多两个素数之和时例外数的上界估计，这是数论中经典问题（涉及Goldbach相关猜想），旨在梳理已有成果并给出新的显式结果。

Method: 以Hardy-Littlewood圆法为起点，综述其发展历程；引入大弧部分的完全显式公式这一新结果；并证明在稀疏版Hardy-Littlewood猜想下不存在例外零点。

Result: 给出了大弧的完全显式公式；证明了在稀疏Hardy-Littlewood猜想下例外零点不存在；同时对Montgomery-Vaughan与Pintz的幂节省结果进行了系统概述。

Conclusion: 本文兼具综述性与创新性，既为初学者提供了圆法及相关技术的入门导引，又在扩大弧公式显式化和例外零点非存在性方面做出了新贡献。

Abstract: We study the estimates for the number of exceptions to the representation of integers as the sum of at most two prime numbers. Most of this article is a survey that gives an overview of existing results. We begin with the legendary Hardy-Littlewood circle method and show how it paved the way to a power saving by Montgomery-Vaughan in 1975 and Pintz in 2018. We conclude with a new result that is a fully explicit formula for the major arcs. Another new observation is the non-existence of exceptional zeros under a sparse version of the Hardy-Littlewood conjecture. The survey part of this article aims to be accessible to an audience that has not encountered these techniques before.

</details>


### [2] [Multivariate counting of wild abelian extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27364)
*Fabian Gundlach*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在特征为p的全局函数域K上，有限阿贝尔群G（指数为p^e）的次G-扩张的多元生成函数（针对e个特殊高度函数）是有理函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究全局函数域上有限阿贝尔群扩张的计数问题，特别是通过捕获高分歧群指数逐级下降的高度函数来理解扩张的分布规律，并揭示生成函数的代数性质（有理函数）。

Method: 通过引入e个特定的高度函数（编码高分歧群指数的逐级下降），构造多元生成函数，并证明该多元生成函数为有理函数。

Result: 证明了在特征为p的全局函数域上，计数次G-扩张的多元生成函数（关于e个特定高度函数）是有理函数。

Conclusion: 该结果为全局函数域上有限阿贝尔群扩张的计数问题提供了更精细的高度函数下的有理生成函数结论，深化了对高分歧结构与扩张计数之间关系的理解。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a global function field of characteristic~$p$ and let $G$ be a finite abelian group of exponent $p^e$. We show that the multivariate generating function counting sub-$G$-extensions of $K$ with respect to $e$ specific height functions (encoding successive drops in the exponents of the higher ramification groups) is rational.

</details>


### [3] [On some Diophantine and Ergodic properties of the Schneider map over function fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27437)
*Matias Alvarado,Nicolás Arévalo-Hurtado,Claudio Bravo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究由Schneider-like映射定义的多项式环上的连分数，证明了Laurant级数域中每个元素展开的存在唯一性、收敛子的丢番图逼近性质、Haar测度的不变性与遍历性，以及最差逼近集的分形维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的连分数理论推广到多项式环上的Schneider-like映射框架，系统探讨其算术与动力学性质，填补该领域中展开性质、遍历性和分形维数等问题的研究空白。

Method: 通过构造与固定多项式（任意次数）相关联的Schneider-like映射，利用动力学系统方法证明展开的存在唯一性，借助测度论（Haar测度）分析不变性与遍历性，并使用丢番图逼近工具研究收敛子性质和最差逼近集合，结合分形几何计算Hausdorff维数。

Result: 证明了Laurant级数域中每个元素的连分数展开存在且唯一；建立了收敛子的精确丢番图逼近性质；证明了Haar测度是映射的不变和遍历测度；得到几乎所有元素展开数字之和的渐近估计；确定最差逼近元素集合并计算其Hausdorff维数为正。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个完整的多项式环上Schneider型连分数理论，涵盖从展开存在唯一性到遍历性和分形几何的多个层面，表明最差逼近集是一个具有正Hausdorff维数的分形集合，为后续研究提供了新框架。

Abstract: We introduce and study continued fractions defined by Schneider-like maps over polynomial rings, where the maps are associated with a fixed polynomial of arbitrary degree. In particular, we prove the existence and uniqueness of the continued fraction expansion for every element of the field of Laurent series. We then establish precise Diophantine approximation properties of the corresponding convergents. We also study the dynamical aspects of the underlying map, proving that the Haar measure is invariant and ergodic. As an arithmetic consequence, we determine the asymptotic sum of the digits of the expansion for almost every element. Finally, we identify the set of elements that are worst approximable in this framework and compute its Hausdorff dimension, showing that it is a fractal set of positive dimension.

</details>


### [4] [On the greatest common divisor of $n, \lfloor α_1n\rfloor, \lfloor α_2n^2\rfloor, ..., \lfloor α_kn^k\rfloor$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27533)
*Jérémy Champagne*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文回答了Bergelson和Richter提出的一个关于gcd概率的问题：对于固定无理数α₁,...,αₖ，当n∈ℕ时，gcd(n,⌊α₁n⌋,...,⌊αₖn^k⌋)=1的概率。作者避免了使用指数和（因为当某个α具有高效有理逼近时难以控制），转而采用类似Erdős-Lorentz的初等方法，并结合关于n与⌊α₁n⌋共享大素因子的通用界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Bergelson和Richter提出的开问题，即关于n与其floor函数值⌊αᵢn^i⌋的gcd为1的概率。传统指数和方法在无理数具有极佳有理逼近时难以处理，需要探索新的途径。

Method: 作者不使用指数和，而是采用与Erdős和Lorentz相似的基础性方法。该方法结合了对那些与⌊α₁n⌋共享大素数因子的n的一些通用界。

Result: 给出了该gcd等于1的概率的精确结果，避免了指数和方法的局限性，适用于任意固定无理数α₁,...,αₖ。

Conclusion: 通过初等方式解决了Bergelson-Richter问题，证明无需指数和即可处理这类gcd概率问题，为相关数论概率问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We answer a question of Bergelson and Richter about the probability of the relation $\gcd(n,\lfloorα_1n\rfloor,\lfloorα_2n^2\rfloor,...,\lfloorα_kn^k\rfloor)=1$ for $n\in\mathbb{N}$ when $α_1,...,α_k$ are fixed irrational numbers. In particular, we avoid the use of exponential sums, as they are difficult to control when one of $α_1,...,α_k$ admits very efficient rational approximations. Instead, we use an elementary method similar to that of Erdős and Lorentz, together with some general bounds on those $n$'s which share a large prime divisor with $\lfloorα_1n\rfloor$.

</details>


### [5] [An Asymptotic Bound for Non-covering Congruence Systems over Fq[x]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27538)
*Rongyin Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上多项式环中非覆盖同余类族所省略多项式的最小次数问题，证明了最大可能最小次数 D_q(n) = n/(q-1) + O_q(1)，其中上界通过极小反例约化到不可约模数并结合截断容斥（Brun筛法）得到，下界通过嵌套模数构造实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有限域上多项式环 F_q[x] 中非覆盖同余类族的最小被省略多项式次数问题，在已知"每个非覆盖族都省略次数小于 n 的多项式"这一定理的基础上，希望精确刻画最大可能的最小省略次数 D_q(n) 的渐近行为。

Method: 上界证明采用极小反例约化到不可约模数的方法，结合截断容斥原理（Brun筛法）进行计数论证；下界构造采用嵌套模数（nested-modulus construction）的方式来构造非覆盖同余类族。

Result: 证明了 D_q(n) = n/(q-1) + O_q(1)，即最大可能的最小被省略多项式次数主项为 n/(q-1)，误差项仅依赖于 q 的常数。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了非覆盖同余类族最小省略多项式次数的精确渐近公式，是对作者2025年工作的后续推进，完整刻画了这类问题的量级。

Abstract: Fix a prime power $q$. Let $D_q(n)$ be the largest possible least degree of a polynomial omitted by a non-covering family of $n$ congruence classes in $\mathbb F_q[x]$. Assuming the known theorem that every non-covering family of $n$ classes omits a polynomial of degree less than $n$, we prove \[
  D_q(n)=\frac{n}{q-1}+O_q(1). \] The upper bound combines a minimal-counterexample reduction to irreducible moduli with a truncated inclusion--exclusion (Brun sieve) argument. A nested-modulus construction gives the matching lower bound. This is a follow-up to the author's 2025 work.

</details>


### [6] [Perfect $2$-codes over arbitrary alphabets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27555)
*Michael A. Bennett*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究任意大小字母表上完美e-码的分类问题。对于e>2的情形，分类已经完成；对于非素数幂的q，猜想不存在完美2-码。本文在多种情况下证实了这一猜想，包括q=2^α p^β（p为素数，α、β为正整数），且α≤20或α充分大的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完美e-码在任意大小字母表上的分类是一个重要的组合学问题。虽然e>2的情形已被完全解决，但非素数幂字母表上完美2-码的存在性问题仍是开放猜想，需要进一步证实。

Method: 本文通过分析q=2^α p^β形式的非素数幂，结合数值验证（α≤20）和渐近分析（α充分大）两种途径，对不存在完美2-码的猜想进行证明。

Result: 证实了在q=2^α p^β（p为素数，α、β为正整数）且α≤20或α充分大的情况下，不存在完美2-码，从而在多种情形下确认了该猜想。

Conclusion: 本文在非素数幂字母表上对完美2-码不存在的猜想提供了新的支持证据，覆盖了形如2的幂与奇素数幂乘积的多种情况，为最终完全解决该猜想奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: The classification of perfect $e$-codes over an arbitrary alphabet of size $q$ is complete for $e > 2$. In the case of non prime power $q$, it is conjectured that no perfect $2$-codes exist. We confirm this conjecture in a number of situations, including the case where $q=2^αp^β$ with $p$ prime, $α$ and $β$ positive integers, and either $α\leq 20$, or $α$ sufficiently large.

</details>


### [7] [Schur Eisenstein series and Schur MacMahon series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27702)
*Henrik Bachmann,Jinbo Yu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究了两族以分拆为索引的拟模形式：Schur Eisenstein级数和Schur MacMahon级数，它们由Schur函数构造而来。两者之间的显式转换可解释为对称函数Faà di Bruno Hopf代数中的卷积。论文证明了分拆各部分大小不超过3的Schur Eisenstein级数构成拟模形式的一组基，并猜想Schur MacMahon级数张成所有具有整数系数的拟模形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟模形式是模形式的自然推广，在数论、组合数学和数学物理中有广泛应用。作者希望通过Schur函数这一对称函数论中的基本工具，构造新的拟模形式族，并探索其中的代数结构及其在拟模形式空间中的基底性质。

Method: 利用Schur函数构造两类以分拆为指标集的级数（Schur Eisenstein级数和Schur MacMahon级数）；通过Faà di Bruno Hopf代数中的卷积揭示两者的转换关系；借助经典sl2作用分析其代数性质；针对分拆各部分不超过3的情形证明基底性质。

Result: 证明了分拆各部分大小至多为3的Schur Eisenstein级数构成拟模形式的一组基；并给出了一个猜想：Schur MacMahon级数张成所有整数系数拟模形式的空间。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Schur函数与拟模形式之间新的深刻联系，为拟模形式的研究提供了新的组合构造工具，并通过Hopf代数结构揭示了Schur Eisenstein级数与Schur MacMahon级数之间的内在关系。

Abstract: We introduce and study two partition-indexed families of quasimodular forms obtained from Schur functions: Schur Eisenstein series and Schur MacMahon series. An explicit transition between them can be interpreted as a convolution in a Faà di Bruno Hopf algebra of symmetric functions. We discuss the classical sl2-action and prove that Schur Eisenstein series for partitions with parts of size at most 3 give a basis for quasimodular forms. Further, we conjecture that the Schur MacMahon series span all quasimodular forms with integral coefficients.

</details>


### [8] [Irrationality of finite logarithms in a congruence-class adèle ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27774)
*Daniel Evans*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究"穷人的adele环"中非零有理数的有限对数（通过Fermat商定义），将已有结果从素数扩展到形如p≡1 mod m的算术级数中的素数，并利用分圆多项式及其对数导数建立联系，在abc猜想下证明有限对数不能是二次无理数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的Matsusaka和Seki证明了在非平凡情形下有限对数不能取非零有理值，Silverman在abc猜想下证明了它们非零。本文旨在将这些结果推广到更一般的素数集合（算术级数中的素数），并探索有限对数取值的进一步约束（如二次无理数）。

Method: 将Fermat商与分圆多项式及其对数导数的值联系起来，通过对p≡1 mod m型的素数（即包含m次单位根的原根的素数）进行讨论，建立有限对数的性质，从而扩展已有结论。

Result: 将Matsusaka-Seki和Silverman的结果从所有素数推广到限制于p≡1 mod m的算术级数中的素数；并在假设abc猜想的前提下，证明了有限对数不能在适当意义下等于二次无理数。

Conclusion: 有限对数在穷人的adele环中的取值受到强烈约束：既不能取非零有理值（非平凡情形），在abc猜想下也不能取二次无理值，且相关结论对算术级数限制的素数仍然成立。

Abstract: Finite logarithms of non-zero rational numbers can be defined in the "poor man's adèle ring" ${\mathcal A}$ by Fermat quotients modulo sufficiently large primes. This ring contains $\mathbb{Q}$ and outside trivial cases, Matsusaka and Seki have shown that finite logarithms cannot take non-zero rational values in ${\mathcal A}$. Furthermore, a theorem of Silverman shows they are not zero, assuming the $abc$-conjecture. We extend these results to primes restricted to arithmetic progressions of the form $p\equiv 1\bmod m$ by relating Fermat quotients to values of cyclotomic polynomials and their logarithmic derivatives. As an application we show that, subject to the $abc$-conjecture, finite logarithms cannot be quadratic irrational in ${\mathcal A}$ in an appropriate sense.

</details>


### [9] [On a question of Gowers related to Littlewood's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27780)
*Frederik Broucke,Máté Matolcsi,Szilárd Gy. Révész*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过显式构造回答了Gowers在2009年提出的关于Littlewood猜想的一个可能路径所引出的问题，证明了该路径若不加以进一步改进则无法证明Littlewood猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gowers在2009年的博客文章中提出了一个证明Diophantine逼近中Littlewood猜想的可能方法，该方法归结为一个关于单位立方体中是否存在足够多两两"双曲距离"足够大的点的问题。验证这个问题是证明该路径可行性的关键。

Method: 通过显式构造的方式回答问题，在单位立方体中构造出满足特定"双曲距离"条件的点集，从而检验Gowers提出方法的前提假设是否成立。

Result: 显式构造表明Gowers所提出的问题被否定地回答，即不存在足够多的满足条件的两两"双曲距离"足够大的点。因此，该方法路径在不进一步改进的情况下无法证明Littlewood猜想。

Conclusion: 本文的结论指出了Gowers提出的证明Littlewood猜想的路径存在根本性障碍，除非添加进一步的细化条件，否则该途径不能奏效。这为Littlewood猜想的研究提供了重要的否定性进展。

Abstract: In a blogpost in 2009, Gowers raised a possible approach to Littlewood's conjecture in Diophantine approximation, leading to a question about the existence of sufficiently many points in the unit cube such that "hyperbolic distance" between any two of them is large. In this note we answer this question by an explicit construction. This shows that this approach to prove Littlewood's conjecture cannot work, unless some further refinements are added.

</details>


### [10] [On the Gow--McGuire Conjecture for Primitive Quadratic Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28052)
*Juncheng Zhou,Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上一类二次多项式族 x²+μx+λ-α 的本原多项式存在性问题，通过根参数化、特征和估计、素数筛法及有限计算，证明了 Gow 和 McGuire 的猜想3对所有大于 204931 的奇素数幂成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gow 和 McGuire 针对特定形式的二次多项式族提出了三个猜想，涉及本原多项式的存在性。本研究旨在证明这些猜想，特别是猜想3，以解决有限域上本原多项式构造的相关理论问题。

Method: 首先通过根参数化将问题归结为在一个有理函数在 q+1 个点上取本原值的问题；然后结合特征和估计（character-sum estimates）与改进的素数筛法（refined prime sieves）；最后辅以精确的有限计算（finite computation）来覆盖小范围情形，从而完成对所有 q>204931 的证明。

Result: 证明了 Gow 和 McGuire 的猜想3对所有奇素数幂 q>204931 成立；在此范围内猜想1和猜想2也作为推论成立。该结果首次在如此大的统一范围内验证了这三个猜想。

Conclusion: 通过结合解析数论工具与有限计算，本文完整证明了 Gow 和 McGuire 三个猜想在 q>204931 范围内的有效性，为该领域本原多项式的研究提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Let \(q\) be an odd prime power, let \(μ\in\F_q^\times\), and let \(α\in\F_{q^2}\setminus\F_q\). We study primitive polynomials in the family \(x^2+μx+λ-α\), where \(λ\in\F_q\). A root parametrization reduces the problem to finding primitive values of a rational function on \(q+1\) points. Combining character-sum estimates, refined prime sieves, and an exact finite computation, we prove Conjecture~3 of Gow and McGuire for every odd prime power \(q>204931\). Their Conjectures~1 and~2 follow in the same range.

</details>


### [11] [Random linear configurations in dense sets and primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28091)
*Lasse Grimmelt,Joni Teräväinen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了：对于$[N]$中任何多对数稠密的子集，以及素数集中任何多对数相对稠密的子集，在几乎所有的系数向量$(b_1,\ldots,b_k)$和很宽的尺度范围内，都包含非平凡配置$x+b_1m,\ldots,x+b_km$。


<details>

Motivation: Koenigsmann在2016年证明了$\mathbb Q\setminus\mathbb Z$在$\mathbb Q$上是丢番图的，即存在一个整系数多项式使得非整数有理数恰好对应多项式的有理零点。后续研究中，最小化所需变量个数n是一个自然且重要的优化问题。Daans在2024年将记录改进到n=10，本文旨在进一步降低该记录。

Method: 本文通过构造更精细的丢番图表示，将非整数的刻画所需的变量数从10个降到7个。具体方法涉及对整数环的丢番图性质的深入研究，并且文章中特别指出该构造可以推广到任意全局域（包括数域和函数域）。

Result: 证明了存在整数系数的多项式$P(t,x_1,\ldots,x_7)$使得$t\not\in\mathbb Z$当且仅当存在有理点满足该多项式为零，即n=7给出了$\mathbb Q\setminus\mathbb Z$的丢番图表示，优于Daans在2024年得到的n=10。该结果还推广到了任意全局域。由此结合孙智伟的结果，推出一个涉及16个变量的多项式在特定量词组合下无法判定其可满足性。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了$\mathbb Q\setminus\mathbb Z$丢番图表示的变量数量记录（从10降到7），并将结论推广到全局域，进而推导出一个更强的算法不可判定性结果。

Abstract: In 2016 J. Koenigsmann proved that $\mathbb Q\setminus\mathbb Z$ is diophantine over $\mathbb Q$, i.e., there is a polynomial $P(t,x_1,\ldots,x_{n})\in\mathbb Z[t,x_1,\ldots,x_{n}]$ such that for any rational number $t$ we have $$t\not\in\mathbb Z\iff \exists x_1,\ldots,x_{n}\in\mathbb Q\,[P(t,x_1,\ldots,x_{n})=0].$$ In this paper we show that we may take $n=7$ which improves the previous record $n=10$ obtained by Daans in 2024. (Actually we even extend this to any global field.) This, together with a previous result of Z.-W. Sun, implies that there is no algorithm to decide for any $F(x_1,\ldots,x_{16})\in\mathbb Z[x_1,\ldots,x_{16}]$ whether $$\forall x_1,\ldots,x_9\in\mathbb Q\exists y_1,\ldots,y_{7}\in\mathbb Q\,[F(x_1,\ldots,x_9,y_1,\ldots,y_{7})=0].$$

</details>

Conclusion: 基于微分包含算法的TV去噪后处理是一种有效的振荡抑制工具，适用于守恒律的高阶数值方法，且具有无需调参、保持质量等优良性质，可推广到多维度场景。

Abstract: High-order numerical methods for conservation laws can generate spurious oscillations near discontinuities. We propose to suppress these oscillations by post-processing the numerical solution with a total variation (TV) denoising step that reduces the total variation of the nodal values. For a general analysis operator, the resulting discrete minimization problem need not satisfy the submodularity property that existing exact max-flow algorithms require, so we instead compute the TV minimizer exactly using a differential inclusion algorithm. In one dimension, the differential inclusion algorithm computes the TV minimizer in finitely many steps, requires no tuning of algorithmic parameters, and preserves the total mass. In two dimensions, we apply the 1D algorithm dimension by dimension. We test the method as a post-processing limiter for Fourier pseudospectral methods and a fifth-order finite difference scheme applied to scalar conservation laws, compressible Euler equations, and the two-dimensional incompressible Euler equations.

</details>

Abstract: We propose dynamical low-rank (DLR) type filters for data-assimilation problems based on stochastic differential equations (SDEs). In detail, first we derive a DLRA filter for minimizing jointly the mean and covariance error, as well as a strategy to efficiently include the relevant orthogonal directions. This last approach allows the main subspace to evolve also according to the observation operator. Those procedures naturally extend to a Kalman-Bucy type filter when dealing with linear drift, and to ensemble methods, too, resulting also suitable for problems described by nonlinear drift and possible non-Gaussian distribution. Moreover, we further propose a preliminary particle-type DLRA filter that shows potentiality in nonlinear settings. Numerical simulations show the efficacy of these procedures in relevant applications, opening up to further studies in these filtering directions.

Abstract: This work presents a new adaptive finite-element framework for level-set transport, achieving high accuracy in kinematic interface transport problems relevant to interface-capturing methods for two-phase flows while reducing computational cost. Abstract: Computing numerical approximations of matrix functions frequently relies on the efficient evaluation of high-degree matrix polynomials. Although computational bounds are historically governed by the Paterson--Stockmeyer (PS) method, recent theoretical developments have demonstrated the viability of evaluation schemes that eliminate two matrix products ($2M$). Existing literature documents stable instances of this $2M$ reduction only for isolated cases, such as specific degrees of Taylor approximations for the matrix exponential and the matrix logarithm. However, a generalized approach for arbitrary polynomials remains unestablished. To address this limitation, this work presents a software-driven procedure that extends these computational savings to polynomials of degrees $m \in \{18, 21, 24, 26, 27, 28\}$ and all $m \ge 30$, requiring primarily a non-zero leading coefficient. Numerical experiments involving various matrix functions verify that the developed implementation preserves or, in some instances, enhances the numerical accuracy of the classic PS method, while systematically achieving the theoretical reduction of $2M$.

Abstract: Solving large, sparse linear systems is a core task in scientific computing, and efficient iterative solvers rely critically on effective and robust preconditioning. While classical methods such as algebraic multigrid (AMG) are highly scalable, their robustness can degrade on indefinite or nonsymmetric systems where heuristics originally developed for elliptic PDEs are less reliable. Recently, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as data-driven preconditioners; yet, the practical impact of imposing an AMG-style hierarchy remains underexplored for general sparse matrices. In this work, we propose a Graph Neural Multilevel Preconditioner (GMP) that adopts an AMG hierarchy as a structural prior and learns smoothing, restriction, and interpolation operators in a unified framework. Our method targets general sparse systems and is instantiated as a drop-in preconditioner for standard Krylov solvers. On a benchmark of over 800 sparse matrices, we compare against classical AMG, single-level ILUT, and state-of-the-art GNN preconditioners, and characterize the regimes where multilevel graph neural preconditioning improves convergence or, conversely, introduces overhead relative to strong single-level baselines. These results highlight both the promise and the limitations of enforcing AMG-style multilevel structure in learned preconditioners for large-scale scientific simulations.

Abstract: We investigate the stationary Dirac equation associated with a generalized Kronig-Penney model in (N+1)-dimensional Clifford analysis. Away from the interaction sites, the free Dirac system is reformulated by introducing suitable generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators, leading to a coupled first-order system for two Clifford-valued fields. By means of characteristic hypercomplex variables, this system is reduced to a pair of decoupled generalized Helmholtz equations. To construct explicit solutions, we introduce a bivariate Clifford-Appell polynomial basis adapted to the generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators. The Appell expansions transform the differential problem into an algebraic recurrence for the expansion coefficients, yielding explicit series representations of the solutions. The proposed framework provides a unified Clifford-analytic formulation of the free propagation problem and establishes an explicit connection between generalized Helmholtz equations, and Appell polynomial systems in hypercomplex function theory.

Abstract: Climate change reshapes food webs by altering species distributions and interactions, making it essential to identify structural backbones of interactions that persist across ecosystems. Deterministic alignment methods are computationally slow and restricted to one-to-one correspondences. We introduce a scalable, non-deterministic alignment framework inspired by optimal transport that captures overlapping species roles via many-to-many mappings. Framed via motif-role profiles as a Gromov-Wasserstein transport problem, our method is both efficient and interpretable. We apply the proposed method to a large continental-scale data set of 129 mammal food webs in Sub-Saharan Africa. Pairwise alignments are identified, and we uncover robust backbones with greater connectivity and transitivity than those expected under null models. The proposed approach provides a formal, reproducible tool for forecasting ecosystem reorganization and conservation efforts.

Abstract: A Thouless pump describes a system in which a quantized amount of charge is transported by one lattice spacing per cycle when the parameters of a Hamiltonian are varied slowly and periodically. In the standard case, this quantization requires the system to remain in its ground state with vanishing thermodynamic entropy throughout the pumping process. Here, we introduce a class of Mott-Thouless pumps, which operate in highly entropic, spin-incoherent Mott states. We show analytically that these states exhibit exponentially protected quantized transport despite their extensive entropy, because the pumped charge resides in a gapped sector that remains dynamically decoupled from the hot spin degrees of freedom. By contrast, motivated by a recent experimental realization, we identify other classes of Mott-Thouless pumps that lack this protection. In these systems, spin excitations can efficiently generate charge excitations, leading to a rapid breakdown of quantized transport. Our analytical results are supported by numerically exact real-time simulations of finite systems.

Abstract: Using the recently introduced ensemble variational Monte Carlo (VMC), we study optimized wave functions for strongly correlated point defects, including nitrogen-vacancy and silicon-vacancy centers in diamond and substitutional iron and chromium impurities in aluminum nitride. We study the effects of fully optimized determinant expansion parameters, orbitals, and Jastrow correlation factors on these systems. We find that orbitals from the hybrid functional PBE0 are much better (have lower objective functional) than semilocal PBE, which results in changes in the excitation energies up to 0.5 eV. Further improvements can be made by directly optimizing the objective functional, resulting in changes in excitation energies up to 0.2 eV. The most important parameter varies from defect to defect, reinforcing the necessity of optimizing all parameters to obtain accurate excited states in strongly correlated defects.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1) 对浴进行对数离散化，将能量空间划分为高能和低能两个扇区；2) 计算Fermi液体固定点（Anderson模型、完全屏蔽和欠屏蔽Kondo模型）的低能纠缠熵；3) 将能量空间固定点波函数的尺度不变性映射为一维链上的有效平移不变性；4) 利用一维拓扑带理论对固定点进行分类，并关联手征对称性与拓扑边缘模；5) 以双轨道Anderson模型中的局域单重态-Kondo单重态转变为例，研究不同Fermi液体固定点之间的转变及其拓扑性质；6) 研究非Fermi液体临界点的纠缠熵及其Λ依赖性，与过屏蔽双通道Kondo模型进行对比。

Result: Fermi液体固定点的低能纠缠熵流到常数，为ln2的整数倍加上仅依赖对数离散化参数Λ的修正。每个ln2贡献对应一个拓扑边缘模（在手征对称性成立时）。局域单重态相的纠缠熵几乎为零（杂质有效解耦），而Kondo单重态相纠缠熵大于每自旋每轨道ln2。在手征对称性成立时，这一区别对应有效浴链的拓扑转变。非Fermi液体临界点的纠缠熵呈现不稳定平台，其Λ依赖性类似于过屏蔽双通道Kondo模型，支持同一非Fermi液体普适类的普适性。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy (EE) is commonly studied using real-space bipartitions. We show that, in quantum impurity models, an energy-space bipartition, equivalent to the momentum-space bipartition of the bath, can display universal behavior. Motivated by poor man's scaling, we logarithmically discretize the bath and partition it into high- and low-energy sectors. For models with Fermi-liquid fixed points, including the Anderson model and fully screened or underscreened Kondo models, the low-energy EE flows to constants independent of model parameters. These constants are integer multiples of $\ln 2$ plus corrections that depend only on the logarithmic discretization parameter $Λ$. We show that scale invariance of the fixed-point wavefunction in energy space maps to effective translation invariance along a one-dimensional chain, allowing the fixed points to be classified by one-dimensional topological band theory. With low-energy chiral symmetry, each $\ln 2$ contribution originates from a topological edge mode. We also study transitions between distinct Fermi-liquid fixed points using the local-singlet--Kondo-singlet transition in a two-orbital Anderson model driven by an inter-orbital antiferromagnetic coupling. The local-singlet phase has an effectively decoupled impurity and nearly vanishing EE, whereas the Kondo-singlet phase has finite EE larger than $\ln 2$ per spin and orbital. When chiral symmetry holds at low energies, this distinction corresponds to a topological transition of the effective bath chain. At the non-Fermi-liquid critical point, the EE develops an unstable plateau. Its $Λ$ dependence resembles that of the overscreened two-channel Kondo model, supporting universality within the same non-Fermi-liquid universality class.

</details>

Abstract: Reduced density matrix functional theory (RDMFT) offers a route beyond Kohn-Sham density functional theory for strongly correlated systems, yet practical calculations for periodic solids are still out of reach. This crossover originates from the competition between kinetic-energy-driven fermionic itinerancy and interaction-driven onsite pair formation and exhibits behavior consistent with the BCS--BEC crossover in the thermodynamic limit. PCA reveals a systematic redistribution of correlation variance whereas UMAP identifies a clear separation between weak- and strong-pairing regimes. Both machine-learning-based approaches independently identify the same crossover region inferred from conventional observables while providing an order-parameter-independent characterization of the underlying reorganization of many-body correlations. Finite-size scaling analyses of the pairing structure factor and the leading PCA variance ratio demonstrate that these signatures remain robust with increasing system size.

At lower fields, bond-orientational disordering nearly coincides with topological reconstruction, eliminating the skyrmion-glass window and contracting the buffer. Method: 采用统一的数值方法论：对L=8,14,20使用精确对角化，对L=8~100使用DMRG，所有尺寸使用相同的场网格和收敛判据（每个点独立运行chi=100和chi=200两次），明确展示精确对角化与DMRG需精确一致而非近似一致的区域。通过两条路径提取中心荷：中链熵的领头对数有限尺寸标度，以及对整个系统和所有键同时应用完整的Calabrese-Cardy公式。

Result: 系统排除了短距离晶格修正后，得到中心荷c_eff≈0.51-0.52，与二维Ising普适类的精确值c=1/2一致。同时解析并解决了先前数据集中一个由键维收敛伪影引起的虚假不连续性。

Conclusion: 论文既提供了TFIM纠缠结构的物理结果，也是一个认真的有限尺寸数值方法的示范案例，证明了统一的数值手段配合严格的收敛诊断可以提取精确的临界信息，并验证了量子系统中中心荷与经典Ising普适类的一致性。

Abstract: We present a self-contained numerical study of the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model (TFIM), tracing its ground-state entanglement structure from exact diagonalization at small system size (L=8,14,20) through density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations up to L=100. A single, consistent methodology - identical field grid, identical convergence diagnostic (independent runs at bond dimensions chi=100 and chi=200 at every point) - is used across all seven system sizes, and we show explicitly where exact diagonalization and DMRG must agree exactly rather than merely approximately. Along the way we document and resolve a bond-dimension convergence artifact that produced a spurious discontinuity in an earlier, less systematic dataset, as a worked cautionary example for practitioners. Using the resolved dataset we extract the central charge of the transition via two complementary routes - the leading logarithmic finite-size scaling of the mid-chain entropy, and the full Calabrese-Cardy formula applied to every bond of every system size simultaneously - obtaining c_eff to 0.51-0.52 as short-distance lattice corrections are systematically excluded, consistent with the exact two-dimensional Ising value c=1/2. We review the quantum-classical (Suzuki-Trotter) correspondence that underlies this agreement and discuss how the Widom-Kadanoff scaling hypothesis transplants from the classical to the quantum problem. The manuscript is intended as both a physics result and a worked methodological example of careful finite-size numerics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 学习到的力场能够准确复现电子计算得到的自旋力矩，并且产生的非平衡自旋动力学与直接电子结构模拟结果高度一致，涵盖共线、非共线和非共面三类磁序的金属磁性系统。

Conclusion: 研究确立了图神经网络作为机器学习磁力场的强大框架，为跨多长度和时间尺度的非平衡磁性大规模预测模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Metallic magnets exhibit complex spin dynamics governed by electronically generated interactions. Predictive simulations of such dynamics typically require repeated solutions of an underlying electronic problem throughout the time evolution, creating a major computational bottleneck. Here we introduce a graph neural network (GNN) magnetic force-field framework that learns the effective magnetic energy functional governing itinerant spin dynamics directly from electronic calculations. Conceptually analogous to machine-learned interatomic potentials, the proposed framework enables efficient evaluation of spin torques while capturing the nonlinear and spatially extended interactions generated by itinerant electrons. We benchmark the method on representative metallic magnetic systems exhibiting collinear, noncollinear, and noncoplanar magnetic order. The learned force fields accurately reproduce electronically generated spin torques and yield nonequilibrium spin dynamics in excellent agreement with direct electronic simulations. Our results establish graph neural networks as a powerful framework for machine-learned magnetic force fields, providing a pathway toward predictive large-scale simulations of nonequilibrium magnetism across multiple length and time scales.

</details>

*Pavel A. Nosov,Zhengyan Darius Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究晶格复合费米液体中的输运性质，发现反演对称性破缺会暴露复合费米子的奇异动力学响应——包括非解析的光学纵向电阻率（|ω|^{4/3}），有限波矢下的非互易输运和霍尔电导的|q|非解析依赖，以及通过2k_F散射重整化导致的奇异直流输运，这些特征在量子霍尔连续统对应物中不存在，可在扭曲MoTe₂和菱方石墨烯中直接验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解晶格复合费米液体与连续统量子霍尔复合费米液体之间的本质区别，寻找能够区分两者的唯一定位输运特征，并为近期在扭曲MoTe₂和菱方石墨烯中观测到的零场复合费米液体提供可检验的理论预测。

Method: 通过分析半填充Chern带中反演对称性破缺的晶格复合费米液体，研究规范场涨落和费米面散射对输运系数的影响。在零波矢下分析光学电阻率的非解析频率依赖，在有限波矢下考察非互易输运和霍尔电导的动量依赖，并分析2k_F散射对Umklapp弛豫的增强效应。

Result: 发现在门屏蔽库仑相互作用下，反演破缺使规范场涨落产生Reρ^{xx}(ω)∼|ω|^{4/3}的非解析纵向光学电阻率；有限波矢q下出现非互易输运和霍尔电导的∼|q|非解析依赖，可通过表面声波传播探测；2k_F散射重整化增强Umklapp弛豫，导致电阻率的非解析温度依赖。

Conclusion: 晶格复合费米液体表现出不同于量子霍尔连续统对应物的独特输运签名，这些特征为在扭曲MoTe₂和菱方石墨烯等实验平台中识别和验证零场复合费米液体提供了明确的实验可测判据。

Abstract: We study transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids realized in half-filled Chern bands with broken inversion symmetry. We show that reduced crystalline symmetry exposes intrinsic singular dynamical responses of composite fermions that are otherwise hidden in the conventional Landau-level setting. At zero wave vector, inversion breaking allows gauge-field fluctuations to generate a non-analytic longitudinal optical resistivity, with $\operatorname{Re}ρ^{xx}(ω)\sim |ω|^{4/3}$ for gate-screened Coulomb interactions. At finite wave vector $\mathbf{q}$, inversion breaking leads to nonreciprocal transport and a non-analytic $\sim |\mathbf{q}|$ dependence of the Hall conductivity, both of which can be probed through surface acoustic wave propagation. We also discuss a distinct mechanism for singular DC transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids: renormalization of $2k_F$ scattering at the composite Fermi surface enhances Umklapp relaxation and can lead to a non-analytic temperature dependence of the resistivity. Taken together, our results identify transport signatures of lattice composite Fermi liquids that are absent in their continuum quantum Hall counterparts and can be directly tested in ongoing experiments on twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene, where evidence for zero-field composite Fermi liquids has recently been reported.

</details>

### [60] [Minimum-Width Drawing of Trees with Sized Vertices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27445)
*Markus Wallinger,Oscar Navarro,Stephen G. Kobourov*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究在有子节点重排约束下最小化树形分层布局宽度的问题（MWD）。研究表明该问题即使对单位宽度顶点的二叉树也是NP完全的。作者提出了精确求解中等规模实例的混合整数线性规划（MILP）和快速启发式算法，实验表明重排可将绘图宽度中位数减少约20%，最多减少约55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，树的顶点需要表示为具有给定宽度和高度的矩形而非点。当子节点顺序没有语义含义时，合理的选择子节点顺序可以显著减少绘图宽度。然而现有算法大多假设子节点顺序已给定。

Method: （1）证明问题MWD在单位宽度顶点的二叉树上也是NP完全的；（2）提出精确求解的混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，适用于中等规模实例；（3）提出一个快速启发式算法用于大规模实例的近似求解；（4）在合成和真实数据集上与基线方法进行对比评估。

Result: 重排子节点顺序可将绘图宽度中位数减少约20%，单个实例减少最多约55%。启发式算法在一秒内完成布局计算；当MILP证明最优性时，启发式算法在四分之三的实例中与最优宽度的差距不超过25%。

Conclusion: MWD问题在计算复杂性上是不易处理的（NP完全），但通过精确的MILP方法适用于小规模实例，而提出的启发式算法能在实际场景中快速产生良好结果，显著优于不进行重排的基线方法。

Abstract: Trees arise in many applications and computing nice tree layouts is a classical problem in information visualization. In many practical settings, vertices need to be represented as rectangles with a given width and height rather than as points. When an order over the children of each vertex is given, polynomial-time algorithms are known that produce drawings adhering to various drawing conventions. However, in many applications, the order of children carries no semantic meaning, and choosing it well can significantly reduce the drawing's width. In this paper, we study the problem \textsc{Min-Width Tree Drawing with Reordering} (\textsc{MWD}): given a rooted tree whose vertices have prescribed widths, find a sibling order at each internal vertex that minimizes the width of the resulting layered drawing. We show that the problem is \textsf{NP}-complete, even on binary trees with unit-width vertices. We present a mixed integer linear program that solves \textsc{MWD} exactly on moderately sized instances, and a heuristic that is fast and delivers good results in practice. We evaluate both approaches against a baseline on synthetic and real-world datasets, where reordering reduces drawing width by a median of $\approx20\%$ and by up to $\approx55\%$ on individual instances. The heuristic computes its layouts in under a second and, when the MILP proves optimality, it stays within $25\%$ of the optimal width in three-quarters of all instances.

</details>


### [61] [Tight UGC Thresholds for Geometric Stabbing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28062)
*Khaled Elbassioni,Rishikesh Gajjala,Saurabh Ray*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个转移定理：任何固定有限、有界分支数的几何刺穿（stabbing）整数间隙（integrality gap）实例，在UGC假设下都能得到匹配的硬度比。通过严格CSP框架和随机舍入结合全支撑扰动构造连通局部分布，获得了三个紧的UGC阈值：任意大小轴平行d维立方体的坐标超平面刺穿阈值为d；水平线段刺穿的阈值为e/(e-1)；分离d区间横穿的阈值为d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多几何刺穿问题可以自然地表示为覆盖线性规划（LP），其中每个约束是候选集上连续迹的并集。已有的近似算法与积分间隙之间存在差距，但缺乏在UGC假设下匹配的硬度结果。作者旨在建立这类几何覆盖LP的积分间隙与UGC硬度之间的通用对应关系，从而获得紧的阈值。

Method: 作者证明了转移定理：每个固定有限、有界分支数的该形式积分间隙实例在UGC下产生匹配硬度比。技术上，利用Kumar等人（SODA 2011）的严格CSP框架，通过随机舍入和全支撑扰动构造所需的连通局部分布——给定块上的分数向量x，舍入以边际概率x_i选择候选i，并以概率min{1,x(T)}击中每个连续迹T。

Result: 获得三个紧的UGC阈值：(1) 对每个固定d≥2，任意大小轴平行d立方体的坐标超平面刺穿阈值为d；d=2时涵盖矩形和正方形刺穿，匹配Gaur等人2-逼近。(2) 水平线段被水平和垂直线刺穿的阈值为e/(e-1)，匹配Kovaleva和Spieksma的逼近比。(3) 分离d区间横穿阈值为d，在UGC下闭合了Ben-David等人d-逼近留下的间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个通用的转移框架，将一类几何覆盖LP的积分间隙转化为UGC下的硬度结果，并获得了三个不同几何刺穿问题的紧阈值，表明这些问题的近似算法在UGC下是最优的。

Abstract: Many geometric stabbing problems admit natural covering LPs in which each constraint is a union of consecutive traces on ordered candidate sets. We prove a transfer theorem showing that every fixed finite, bounded-arity integrality-gap instance of this form yields a matching hardness ratio under the Unique Games Conjecture. Using the strict-CSP framework of Kumar, Manokaran, Tulsiani, and Vishnoi [SODA 2011], we construct the required connected local distributions by randomized rounding and a full-support perturbation. Given a fractional vector $x$ on a block, the rounding selects candidate $i$ with marginal probability $x_i$ and hits each consecutive trace $T$ with probability $\min\{1,x(T)\}$.
  We obtain three tight UGC thresholds. First, for every fixed $d\ge 2$, stabbing arbitrary-size axis-parallel $d$-cubes with coordinate hyperplanes has threshold $d$. For $d=2$, the hardness holds for arbitrary-size squares and establishes threshold $2$ for rectangle and square stabbing, matching the $2$-approximation of Gaur, Ibaraki, and Krishnamurti [ESA 2000]. Second, stabbing horizontal segments with horizontal and vertical lines has threshold $e/(e-1)$, matching the $e/(e-1)$-approximation of Kovaleva and Spieksma [ESA 2004]. Third, separated $d$-interval transversal has threshold $d$ for every fixed $d\ge 2$, closing under UGC the gap left by the $d$-approximation of Ben-David, Grant, Ma, and Sharpe [CCCG 2012].

</details>
