### [95] [Estimating Treatment Effects for Depression in Longitudinal Therapy Switching Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27214)
*Xinyu Qin,Martin Katzman,Alexandria Greifenberger,Elssa Toumeh,Sachinthya Lokuge,Tia Sternat,Ruiheng Yu,Lu Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用纵向重度抑郁症（MDD）临床试验数据，构建了下一访视的反事实预测任务，估计汉密尔顿抑郁量表（HAMD-17）总分在替代治疗方案下的个体化治疗效果。基准测试了8种估计器（包括meta-learners、残差方法和基于树的方法），发现Causal Forest在所有评估标准上表现最佳且最稳定。分析表明症状获益集中在特定换药方向上，剂量强化通常有益，但存在一个反直觉的例外——在特定患者子集中，较低强度的方案优于较高强度的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症治疗常因应答不足或不良反应需要更换药物，但在该场景下估计个体化治疗效果面临三大挑战：治疗分配受患者特征混淆、换药导致时变选择偏差、以及随访数据中无法观察到反事实结果。作者旨在解决这些技术难题，为换药决策提供更可靠的因果推断支持。

Method: 使用专有的纵向MDD临床试验数据集，构建下一访视反事实预测任务来估计不同治疗方案下的HAMD-17总分。基准测试了8种估计器，涵盖meta-learners、基于残差的方法和基于树的方法，并对其进行多标准评估。

Result: Causal Forest在全部评估标准上表现最优且最一致。症状获益集中在特定换药方向，剂量强化总体有益；但发现一个反直觉例外——特定患者子集中较低强度方案优于较高强度替代方案。此外，未调整的观测比较显著高估治疗获益，而经混淆调整后的估计产生适中且可操作的效应量。

Conclusion: 研究发现为抑郁症治疗提供可前瞻性检验的临床决策支持候选方案，强调了混淆调整在估计个体化治疗效果中的必要性，并展示了Causal Forest在该复杂时序选择场景中的优势。

Abstract: Depression treatment often requires switching medications due to inadequate response or adverse effects. Estimating individualized treatment effects in this setting is challenging because treatment assignment is confounded by patient characteristics, switching induces time-varying selection, and counterfactual outcomes are not observed in follow-up data. Using a proprietary longitudinal major depressive disorder (MDD) clinical trial dataset, we formulate a next-visit counterfactual prediction task to estimate Hamilton Depression Rating Scale (HAMD-17) total scores under alternative treatments. We benchmark 8 estimators, including meta-learners, residual-based methods, and tree-based approaches. Causal Forest (CF) demonstrates the most favorable and consistent performance across all criteria. Our analysis shows that symptom benefits concentrate in specific switch directions, with dose intensification being generally beneficial. Notably, we identify a counterintuitive exception where a lower-intensity regimen outperforms a higher-intensity alternative for specific patient subsets. While crude observational comparisons substantially overstate gains, confounding-adjusted estimates yield modest, actionable magnitudes. These findings provide prospectively testable candidates for clinical decision support in depression care.

</details>


### [96] [Foundation-Model Earth Representations Enable Regional-Scale Forest Aboveground Biomass Monitoring Across the Northeastern United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27217)
*Shashika Lamahewage,Chandi Witharana*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究评估了Google卫星嵌入（GSE）在地表生物量估算中的应用，结合机载LiDAR和连续森林清查数据，在美国东北部温带森林中实现了R²=0.79的估算精度，通过年度GSE观测扩充训练数据后提升至R²=0.82，并减少了超过70%的模型偏差，展示了基础模型地球观测在区域碳监测中的规模化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林地上生物量是生态系统生产力和陆地碳储存的关键指标，但区域碳监测受限于野外清查和机载结构测量的稀疏时空覆盖。需要一种可扩展的方法来实现大规模生物量监测。

Method: 将AlphaEarth基础模型生成的Google卫星嵌入（GSE）与机载LiDAR、NEFIN连续森林清查数据整合到机器学习框架中；利用年度GSE观测通过时间生长调整将训练集扩展十倍以上；采用空间自相关分析评估残差空间依赖性；使用蒙特卡洛模拟评估超参数优化的性能变异性。

Result: LiDAR-GSE联合模型实现R²=0.79；通过年度GSE扩增训练数据后性能提升至R²=0.82，模型偏差降低>70%；整合基础模型表征和结构预测因子显著减少残差空间依赖性；超参数优化使模型性能变异性降低27.9%。

Conclusion: 基础模型地球表征能够捕捉与森林生物量相关的生态学信息，为机载LiDAR覆盖不完全地区提供可扩展的年度碳监测框架，建立了基于全球可用基础模型地球观测的下一代森林碳评估路径。

Abstract: Forest aboveground biomass (AGB) is a critical indicator of ecosystem productivity and terrestrial carbon storage, yet regional carbon monitoring remains constrained by the sparse spatial and temporal availability of field inventories and airborne structural measurements. Recent Earth observation foundation models provide globally consistent geospatial representations derived from diverse multimodal datasets, offering a potential pathway toward scalable biomass monitoring. Here, we evaluate Google Satellite Embeddings (GSE), generated by the AplphaEarth Foundation Model, for regional-scale AGB estimation across diverse temperate forest ecosystems in the northeastern United States. We integrated annual GSE observations, airborne LiDAR, and continuous forest inventory measurements from the Northeastern Forest Inventory Network (NEFIN) within a machine-learning framework. Combined LiDAR-GSE models achieved an R^2 of 0.79 for AGB estimation. Capitalizing on annual GSE observations expanded the training dataset by more than tenfold through temporal growth adjustment, increasing predictive performance to R^2 = 0.82 while reducing model bias by over 70%. Spatial autocorrelation analyses showed that integrating foundation-model representations and structural predictors substantially reduced residual spatial dependence. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrated that hyperparameter optimization reduced model-performance variability by 27.9%. Our findings demonstrate that foundation-model Earth representations capture ecologically meaningful information relevant to forest biomass and provide a scalable framework for annual carbon monitoring in regions with incomplete airborne LiDAR coverage. Our fundings establish a pathway toward next-generation forest carbon assessment based on globally available foundation-model Earth observations.

</details>


### [97] [Psych-ECA: A Reproducible Semi-Synthetic Benchmark for Synthetic Control Arms in Longitudinal Psychiatry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27224)
*Aakash Bhagat,Shashank Choudhary*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文发布Psych-ECA，一个可完全复现的纵向抑郁症（PHQ-9）、焦虑症（HAM-A）和精神分裂症（PANSS）症状轨迹半合成基准生成器，并评估八种外部对照臂估计方法，发现轨迹与灵活机器学习方法精度最优，Scribe方法在精度、校准性和假阳性率控制上全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部/合成对照臂（ECA）正进入精神科药物研发领域，但缺乏统一基准来评估监管机构关注的属性：轨迹重建准确性、不确定性校准性、对精神健康记录中信息性观测时间的稳健性（病情更重的患者就诊更频繁），以及go/no-go试验决策中的假阳性率。真实的临床试验数据（如STAR-D）需要授权访问且缺乏反事实真值。

Method: 遵循因果推断中成熟的半合成基准范式（IHDP、ACIC、PK-PD肿瘤生长模拟器），构建Psych-ECA生成器：可复现地生成抑郁症（PHQ-9）、焦虑症（HAM-A）、精神病（PANSS）的纵向症状轨迹，具有已知反事实对照组、信息性就诊和经过验证的量表测量噪声。基准评测八种估计器：携带前向填充、合并真实世界数据平均值、最近邻匹配、线性混合模型、梯度提升、Scribe轨迹桥接方法等。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）轨迹方法和灵活机器学习方法在反事实精度上最优（约2.3 PHQ-9 RMSE），优于横截面基线；（2）仅Scribe方法同时具备准确性和校准性，对名义90%预测区间达到93-96%的经验覆盖率，而梯度提升为87-88%，未校准SDE模型仅62-75%；（3）逆强度校正可降低信息性抽样下的偏差，而Scribe的校准区间是唯一在信息性增强时仍保持名义假阳性率的轨迹方法。

Conclusion: Psych-ECA为精神科外部对照臂方法提供了首个面向监管关注属性的标准化评估基准，揭示轨迹方法相比横截面基线的优势，并强调校准不确定性对决策正确性的关键价值。所有代码、数据生成脚本和随机种子均已公开，支持完全可复现的评测。

Abstract: External and synthetic control arms (ECAs) are entering psychiatric drug development, but the field lacks a benchmark that evaluates the properties regulators care about: not only how accurately a method reconstructs untreated trajectories, but whether its uncertainty is calibrated, whether it is robust to the informative observation times common in mental-health records (sicker patients are seen more often), and what false-positive rate it induces in go/no-go trial decisions. Real psychiatric trial data (e.g. STAR-D and registry cohorts) require credentialed access and lack ground-truth counterfactuals, so, following established semi-synthetic benchmarks in causal inference (IHDP, ACIC, and the PK-PD tumor-growth simulator), we release Psych-ECA, a fully reproducible generator of longitudinal symptom trajectories for depression (PHQ-9), anxiety (HAM-A), and psychosis (PANSS) with known counterfactual control arms, informative visits, and validated-scale measurement noise. We benchmark eight estimators spanning carry-forward, pooled real-world-data averages, nearest-neighbour matching, linear mixed models, gradient boosting, and the Scribe trajectory-bridge method. Three findings emerge. First, trajectory and flexible machine learning methods achieve the best counterfactual accuracy (about 2.3 PHQ-9 RMSE), outperforming cross-sectional baselines. Second, only Scribe is both accurate and calibrated, achieving 93-96% empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals, compared with 87-88% for gradient boosting and 62-75% for uncalibrated SDE models. Third, inverse-intensity correction reduces bias under informative sampling, while Scribe's calibrated intervals are the only trajectory method that maintains nominal false-positive rates as informativeness increases. We release all code, data-generation scripts, and random seeds to enable fully reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [98] [Who Moved? Ecological Estimates of Turnout & Coalition Support by Ethnicity and Age in Johor, Malaysia, 2022-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27864)
*Thevesh Thevananthan,Ong Kian Ming*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用2026年7月柔佛州选举中各投票站的族群构成地理变异与投票流年龄构成的行政变异，对2022年以来涉及柔佛的三场选举进行生态推断，估计不同族群和年龄层的投票率与联盟支持率，得出五项核心发现：投票率是结果的首要驱动因素；国阵（BN）的崛起几乎与国民联盟（PN）在马来选民中的崩塌一一对应；年轻马来选民是转向国阵的最大群体；希望联盟（PH）华裔票源的减少三分之二源于华裔投票率下降；BN 2026年支持率表观上的年龄梯度主要反映族群构成而非组内行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马来西亚选举中族群与年龄维度上的投票行为缺乏开放且可复现的生态估计。本文旨在填补这一空白，并厘清2026年柔佛州选举中BN大幅胜选背后的选民基础变化机制，特别是区分族群构成效应与组内行为变化。

Method: 利用投票站层面的族群构成地理变异和投票流层面的年龄构成行政变异，结合生态推断（ecological inference）方法，对2022年以来涉及柔佛的三场选举进行族群与年龄维度的投票率及联盟支持率估计，并通过留出行为数据对每个估计量提供特定于估计目标（estimand）的验证误差。

Result: 五项主要发现：(1) 投票率是结果的一阶驱动因素，同族群内跨选举投票率波动最高达26个百分点，且呈现生命周期形态；(2) BN的崛起与PN在马来选民中的崩塌近乎一比一对应，且对PN在56席中23席让选的情况稳健；(3) 最大幅度的转向来自年轻马来选民，他们此前是2022年"绿潮"的主要承载者；(4) PH在华裔选民中的衰退三分之二来自华裔投票率下降，转向BN的比例相对较小；(5) BN 2026年支持率看似存在的年龄梯度主要由族群构成驱动，而非组内行为差异——到2026年，马来选民对各联盟的支持率在各年龄段几乎持平。

Conclusion: 作者提供了马来西亚选举中首个开放且完全可复现的投票行为生态估计，并附有基于留出行为的估计算法特异性验证误差，为理解马来西亚选举政治中族群与年龄维度的动态变化提供了可靠的方法论基础与实证证据。

Abstract: At the July 2026 Johor state election, Barisan Nasional (BN) swept the state and nearly doubled its vote relative to 2022. We exploit geographic variation in the ethnic composition of polling stations and administration-induced variation in the age composition of polling streams to estimate turnout and coalition support by ethnicity and age for the three elections involving Johor since 2022, with five key findings. (1) Turnout is a first-order driver of outcomes, moving by up to 26 points within an ethnic group across elections and following a life-cycle profile. (2) BN's surge was a near one-for-one Perikatan Nasional (PN) collapse among Malay voters, robust to PN's stand-down in 23 of 56 seats. (3) The largest reversal towards BN was among young Malays, who had previously carried the `green wave' of 2022. (4) Two-thirds of Pakatan Harapan's (PH) decline among the Chinese electorate came through lower Chinese turnout; any crossover to BN was comparatively small. (5) The apparent youth gradient in BN's 2026 support is largely ethnic composition rather than within-group behaviour: by 2026, Malay support for every coalition was close to flat in age. More broadly, we provide what we believe are the first open and fully reproducible ecological estimates of voting behaviour for any Malaysian election, accompanied by estimand-specific validation errors measured against held-out behaviour.

</details>


### [99] [What exam scores can and cannot prove about unauthorized AI assistance: Evidence from a highly public classroom episode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27978)
*Chad M. Topaz,Utsav Bahl*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文分析了布朗大学2026年春季经济学期中、期末考试成绩数据（59名学生，期中均分95.7降至期末48.8，相关系数仅0.06），通过置换检验、模型复杂度和模拟实验证明：期中成绩对学生期末成绩不携带任何可检测的统计信息，数据无需用生成式AI作弊来解释，但同样无法区分AI作弊、考试内容变化等不同机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕学生在期中考试中未经授权使用生成式AI的指控，各方对成绩大幅下降提出了多种相互竞争的解释（AI作弊、考试焦虑、期末考试更难、学生退课、均值回归）。本文旨在通过严谨的统计方法评估这些竞争性解释的证据强度，并为未来关于生成式AI在学术评估中使用的争议提供分析框架。

Method: 采用多种统计方法：(1) 计算期中-期末成绩的相关系数（0.06）及个体分数变化范围（+4到-100）；(2) 置换检验检验期中与期末成绩之间的关联强度，并对比替代解释预测的关联强度能否被检测出；(3) 考虑模型复杂度后，比较允许关联的模型与无关联模型的拟合优度；(4) 在模拟课堂中生成学生两次成绩由同一能力特质关联的数据，检验"两个模式同时出现"的频率。

Result: 置换检验未发现期中与期末成绩之间有统计学上可检测的关联，而替代解释预测的关联强度在超过90%的实验中都能被检测到。考虑模型复杂度后，无关联模型与任何允许关联的模型拟合效果相当。在模拟数据中，当学生成绩通过自身能力关联时，两个显著模式（相关系数0.06和巨大分数变化范围）几乎从不共同出现；只有当这种关联被几乎完全切断时，它们才经常共同出现。但数据无法区分切断关联的具体机制（AI作弊或不涉及作弊的不同考试内容）。

Conclusion: 数据不支持期中成绩包含任何关于期末成绩的可检测信息，也不能将成绩下滑归因于生成式AI的未经授权使用。虽然不能排除弱关联存在，但数据不需要此类关联。研究提供了一个在涉及生成式AI使用的学术争议中评估统计证据的框架，强调数据本身无法区分AI作弊与其他切断成绩关联的机制。

Abstract: In spring 2026, an economics professor at Brown University gave a take-home midterm and, after unusually high scores, made the final exam proctored. Among the 59 students who completed the course, average scores fell from 95.7 out of 100 to 48.8. The instructor attributed the drop to unauthorized use of generative AI on the midterm; others proposed test anxiety, a harder final, student withdrawals, and regression to the mean. The publicly released scores, one midterm-final pair per student, show two striking patterns: the correlation between a student's two scores is only 0.06, and individual changes range from a 4-point gain to a 100-point loss. Permutation tests find no statistically detectable association between students' midterm and final scores, yet would detect association of the strength the alternative explanations predict more than 90 percent of the time. Once model complexity is accounted for, a model in which a student's midterm carries no information about that student's final describes the data as well as any model that permits an association. Neither result rules out a weak association, but together they show that the data do not require any. In simulated classes where each student's two scores remain linked through that student's own proficiency, as the alternative explanations imply, the two patterns almost never appear together; they appear together regularly only when that link is nearly severed. More than one mechanism could have severed it: midterm answers that were not the students' own work would have done so, and so would a final testing substantially different material, with no assistance involved. The scores cannot distinguish these possibilities or identify which students, if any, used unauthorized assistance. Our study offers a framework for evaluating statistical evidence in future disputes over generative AI use in academic assessment.

</details>


### [100] [Bridging Probabilistic LLMs and Deterministic Statistical Validation: The PROVE Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical Trial Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28218)
*Zhaohua Lu,Cheng Zheng,Yuanyuan Han*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出PROVE框架，一种可审计的临床TFL（表格、图表和清单）输出验证引擎，将LLM用于表格语义解释，而将数值和逻辑决策保留给程序化验证器。在模拟肿瘤学报告包的评估中，LLM辅助语义匹配将F1从0.735提升至0.996。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床TFL在监管报告中的准确性和一致性校验是重大挑战。传统人工审查容易遗漏结构、逻辑或算术差异，而LLM虽然能理解表格语言但不可靠地替代编程化统计检查。需要一个将LLM的语义理解能力与程序化数值校验相结合的框架。

Method: PROVE框架采用可选LLM和检索增强支持来解释表格语义，同时保留程序化验证器负责数值和逻辑决策。框架将发现结果链接到源证据，支持跨输出一致性检查，并允许根据研究需求启用或禁用LLM。评估使用十个复制的合成肿瘤学报告包（SDTM→ADaM→TFL），每个包含15个随机注入的差异，并测试了精确标签匹配和语义变体标签两种设置。

Result: 在精确标签设置下，所有自动化PROVE变体均达到完美分类。在标签变体设置中，LLM辅助语义匹配将整体召回率从0.588提升到0.993，整体F1从0.735提升到0.996，优于精确匹配、模糊词法和嵌入相似度变体。

Conclusion: LLM最适合用于解释TFL措辞和格式的真实世界变异性，而最终数值验证应由可执行的程序化检查负责。PROVE框架提供了一种结合两者优势的可审计方案，支持在监管报告中实现更可靠的TFL验证。

Abstract: Ensuring the accuracy and consistency of clinical trial Tables, Figures, and Listings (TFLs) remains a major challenge in regulatory reporting. Independent programming and manual review are essential quality-control practices, but cross-output verification still depends heavily on reviewer inspection and may miss structural, logical, or arithmetic discrepancies. Large language models (LLMs) can help interpret varied table language and navigate lengthy study documents, but they are not reliable substitutes for programmed statistical checks. We introduce PROVE (Programmatic Reporting and Output Verification Engine), an auditable framework that uses optional LLM and retrieval support for table interpretation while reserving numerical and logical decisions for programmed validators. PROVE links findings to source evidence, supports cross-output consistency checks, and allows LLM use to be enabled or disabled based on study requirements. We evaluated PROVE using ten replicated synthetic oncology reporting packages generated from raw data through SDTM, ADaM, and TFL outputs, with paired clean and discrepancy-injected packages; each replicate included 15 randomly injected discrepancies. We examined two table-label settings: exact labels matching the validator vocabulary and labels with similar clinical meaning but different wording. Within the implemented rule classes, all automated PROVE variants achieved perfect classification in the exact-label setting. In the label-variation setting, LLM-assisted semantic matching improved overall recall from 0.588 to 0.993 and overall F1 from 0.735 to 0.996 compared with exact-match, fuzzy lexical, and embedding-similarity variants. These findings suggest that LLMs are most useful for interpreting real-world variation in TFL wording and formatting, while executable checks should remain responsible for final numerical validation.

</details>


### [101] [Snapshot plots: displaying summary tables as parallel univariate plots with consistent color highlighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28302)
*Matthias Schonlau,Sandra Huang,Tiancheng Yang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出snapshot plots（快照图），一种并行单变量图，用于可视化实证研究中常见的"Table 1"（样本背景特征汇总统计表）。与传统的"Table 1"相比，快照图便于比较各组的背景特征，并提供更详细的数值变量信息。该图是hammock plots（混合分类/数值数据的平行坐标图）的边界情况，作者提供了Web应用和Python实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会与健康科学实证研究通常用"Table 1"汇总样本的背景特征，当存在处理组/对照组时，该表按组提供汇总统计量以检查各组背景特征是否有差异。虽然表格信息准确，但在直观比较多组、多变量时不够高效，尤其是数值变量的分布信息有限。作者希望提出一种可视化方案来弥补"Table 1"在组间比较和细节呈现方面的不足。

Method: 作者提出snapshot plots，即"并行单变量图+一致性高亮"的可视化方法。该方法将每个背景特征作为独立的小图（parallel univariate plots）并排展示，通过一致的高亮方式统一标记组别，便于跨特征、跨组的对比。数值变量采用更细粒度的分布呈现（如箱线图或密度估计），优于普通汇总统计。作者指出snapshot plots是hammock plots（混合分类/数值数据的平行坐标图）的边界情况，并在此基础上提供了Web应用和Python实现。

Result: 作者通过两个实际的"Table 1"示例展示了snapshot plots的有效性，验证了其能更直观地呈现组间背景特征的差异，并为数值变量提供了比传统汇总表更丰富的分布信息。此外，还交付了可供使用的Web应用和Python代码实现。

Conclusion: Snapshot plots作为一种"Table 1"的可视化替代方案，能有效促进背景特征的组间比较，并在数值变量上提供更细节的信息。该方法的实现工具（Web应用和Python包）降低了使用门槛，未来有望在实证研究中得到广泛应用。

Abstract: For empirical studies, social and health scientists give background characteristics of their sample and summarize them in the famous "Table 1". When treatment/ control groups are present, this table gives summary statistics by group to see whether the background characteristics differ by group. We propose snapshot plots --- parallel univariate plots with consistent highlighting --- to visualize such tables. Compared to "Table 1", such plots are designed to facilitate comparisons of background characteristics --- in particular among groups --- and give more detail on numerical variables. We provide a web app as well as a python implementation of snapshot plots. Snapshot plots arise as edge cases of hammock plots (parallel coordinate plots for mixed categorical/ numerical data). We demonstrate the usefulness of snapshot plots for two "Table 1"s.

</details>


### [102] [Stated, Realized, and Optimal Aiming Strategies for the Tennis Serve: Experimental Evidence from Collegiate Athletes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28500)
*Nathan Sandholtz,Ryan Hanson,Ron Hager,Stephanie Kovalchik,Gilbert Fellingham*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过实验方法研究网球发球的最佳瞄准位置，利用杨百翰大学网球队成员的实验数据估计球员特定的发球分布，并结合贝叶斯两期马尔可夫决策过程找到最优发球目标。研究发现第二发球的最优目标比第一发球更保守，且球员实际发球落点中心比其声称的目标更保守，更接近估计的最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网球比赛中，发球瞄准位置存在权衡：靠近发球区边界使回球更难，但增加发球失误概率。这一权衡取决于球员的执行误差，而普通比赛数据无法观察球员的意图目标，因此难以直接估计执行误差和最优瞄准策略。

Method: 开展受控实验：要求球员选定自认为最优的目标位置并标记在场上，然后反复向每个目标发球并精确记录落点。利用这些数据估计球员特定的发球分布（考虑被网拦截的审查数据），结合估计的得分奖励曲面，在具有连续空间行动空间的贝叶斯两期马尔可夫决策过程中求解最优目标。

Result: （1）每个球员-区域组合中，最优第二发球目标都比第一发球更保守，与既有理论一致；（2）球员声称的目标通常比最优目标更激进，但其实际发球中心比声称目标更保守，且明显更接近估计的最优值。

Conclusion: 实验数据能够弥补比赛数据无法提供执行误差信息的缺陷，为网球发球最优策略提供了实证估计。球员的实际行为（发球中心）比其主观声明的目标更接近理论最优，表明球员在实践中可能通过经验调整发球瞄准策略，使其趋于理性。

Abstract: We study where, within a chosen Wide or T region, a tennis player should aim a serve. Aiming near a service-box boundary makes a serve harder to return but raises the probability of a fault, creating a trade-off that depends on the player's execution error. Ordinary match data provide limited information about execution error because players' intended targets are unobserved. We therefore conducted an experiment with members of the Brigham Young University men's tennis team. Each player identified targets they believed to be optimal, which we marked on the court. Players then served repeatedly toward each target while we recorded precise bounce locations. From these data, we estimate player-specific serve distributions while accounting for serves censored by net contact, then combine them with an estimated point-win reward surface in a Bayesian, two-period Markov decision process with a continuous spatial action space. We find that the estimated optimal second-serve target is more conservative than its first-serve counterpart in every player-region combination, consistent with prior theory. Also, while players' stated targets were generally more aggressive than optimal, their realized serve centers were more conservative than those targets and markedly closer to the estimated optima.

</details>


### [103] [ABC methods for IoT Emitter Geolocalisation using LEO Satellite Doppler Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28585)
*B. Ristic,Y. Choi,D. Y. Kim,A. Hourani*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究利用低地球轨道（LEO）卫星在多普勒频率测量下对地面静止IoT无线电发射源进行被动定位的问题，采用近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）方法来处理因多种测量误差叠加导致的不可解似然函数，并通过数值实验比较了拒绝采样ABC和序贯蒙特卡洛ABC两种方法的统计与计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本IoT设备发射的无线电信号受到多种复合测量误差的影响，使得似然函数无法以闭式形式表达，传统的定位方法难以直接应用。因此需要一种不依赖显式似然函数的推断方法来解决这个被动定位问题。

Method: 采用近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）框架，具体实现两种算法：拒绝采样ABC（rejection sampling ABC）和序贯蒙特卡洛ABC（sequential Monte Carlo ABC），利用LEO卫星在观测窗口内收集的多普勒频率测量数据对地面静止发射源的位置进行被动定位。

Result: 数值结果展示了两种ABC方法在统计性能和计算性能上的表现，验证了它们在处理该定位问题时的有效性和可行性。

Conclusion: ABC方法为LEO卫星多普勒测量下的IoT发射源被动定位提供了一种可行的推断途径，其中两种ABC变体各有其统计与计算上的权衡，可在实际应用中选择合适的方案。

Abstract: We address the problem of passive localisation of a stationary, ground-level IoT radio emitter using Doppler frequency measurements collected by low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellites during an observation window. The problem is challenging because radio emission from low-cost IoT devices is affected by various compounding sources of measurement error, that collectively render the likelihood function intractable in a closed form. Hence, we apply and investigate the performance of Approximate Bayesian Computation (ABC) methods for this task. Numerical results demonstrate the statistical and computational performance of two ABC methods, rejection sampling ABC and sequential Monte Carlo ABC.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明在标准d-单纯形上，对于关于负熵L-光滑的凸函数类，任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，当d=Ω(T²)时。这表明镜像下降在此函数类中最优（仅差对数因子），并扩展到了量子设置（负von Neumann熵）中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Dragomir等人（2022）已证明在相对光滑性假设下加速可能不成立，但其使用的prox函数是病态的、与硬实例一起构造的。作者希望证明对于具有特别良好结构的特定prox函数（负熵），加速同样不可能，从而说明非加速现象并非病态构造的产物。

Method: 作者构造了关于负熵L-光滑的凸函数的硬实例，证明任何一阶方法在d=Ω(T²)维度条件下的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)。该下界与镜像下降的收敛率匹配（仅差对数因子）。作者还通过类似的技术将结果推广到量子设置，即关于负von Neumann熵L-光滑的函数类在谱面体上的情形。

Result: 在d-单纯形上，关于负熵L-光滑的凸函数类中，任何一阶方法的收敛率下界为Ω(L/T)，维度条件为d=Ω(T²)。类似下界也适用于量子设置中关于负von Neumann熵L-光滑的函数类。

Conclusion: 镜像下降在关于负熵相对光滑的凸函数类中是最优的（至多差对数因子），即便在此类函数具有良好结构的情况下，加速仍然不可能实现。这一结论同时适用于经典设置和量子设置。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [105] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预测（prognostics）的混合规划方法，用于制定预测性维护（PdM）策略。该方法结合决策选项、预测模型提供的系统未来状态信息以及更新-报酬过程的成本，生成仅由少量参数定义的策略，可通过理论推导或基于run-to-failure数据优化确定。在预防性更换和预防性订货两个决策场景中验证，数值结果表明所提策略在性能上与优化的基准策略相当，但效率显著更高且对过拟合更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性维护策略的制定需要考虑多种因素（决策选项、预测状态信息、底层过程的成本），而现有方法可能存在策略复杂、计算效率低或对过拟合敏感等问题，因此需要一种既能保证性能又能提高效率和鲁棒性的混合规划方法。

Method: 提出一种混合规划方法，将决策空间、预测模型输出的未来系统状态信息以及更新-报酬过程的成本纳入统一框架。所得策略由少量参数定义，参数可通过两种途径确定：基于理论推导，或通过对run-to-failure数据进行优化。在预防性更换和预防性订货两种维护决策情境中进行验证。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的参数化策略在与优化基准策略的对比中性能相当（rival），但具有显著更高的效率（significantly more efficient），并且对过拟合的鲁棒性更强（robust against overfitting）。

Conclusion: 该混合规划方法能够在保持与优化基准策略相当性能的同时，提供更简单、更高效且更抗过拟合的预测性维护策略，为实际预测性维护决策提供了一个实用性强的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [106] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在最小时间函数框架下研究具有集值目标的一般化多源Weber问题，建立了全局与局部最优解的存在性，分析了解集的闭性、紧性及有界性等定性性质，推导了目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性，并引入全局与局部解映射，从集值分析视角研究了其稳定性，为多源Weber问题提供了定量与定性的灵敏度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源Weber问题是设施选址领域的经典问题，但传统研究通常假设目标为单点集。当目标扩展为集值时，问题变得更加复杂且具有广泛的实际应用背景（如区域覆盖、不确定性下的选址）。作者希望在该广义设定下建立系统性的理论框架，填补最小时间函数框架下多源Weber问题在解存在性、解集定性性质及灵敏度分析方面的空白。

Method: 本文采用最小时间函数作为核心分析工具，将集值目标的多源Weber问题纳入该框架。方法上：（1）通过变分分析与优化理论证明全局和局部最优解的存在性；（2）利用集值映射和拓扑工具刻画解集的闭性、紧性、有界性/无界性条件；（3）运用Lipschitz分析推导目标函数和最优值函数对目标集扰动的显式连续性质；（4）借助集值分析（如Berge型连续性、Hausdorff距离等）研究全局与局部解映射的稳定性。

Result: 文章获得了以下主要结果：（1）建立了全局和局部最优解的存在性定理；（2）完整刻画了解集的定性属性，包括闭性、紧性以及有界/无界条件；（3）推导出目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz连续性估计；（4）证明了解映射在Hausdorff拓扑意义下的稳定性。这些结果构成了对广义多源Weber问题的定量和定性灵敏度分析体系。

Conclusion: 该研究将多源Weber问题从单点目标推广到集值目标，并在最小时间函数框架下建立了完整的理论分析体系，涵盖解的存在性、解集的定性性质以及目标函数和解映射的灵敏度分析。所得结果不仅理论上丰富了多源Weber问题的研究，也为设施选址、多目标优化等实际应用提供了具有扰动鲁棒性的理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [107] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: OptGraph是一个基于图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）的自动化进化优化智能体工作流，通过将可复用经验构建为类型化图，利用图邻域信息增强知识检索，实现自适应知识更新，无需LLM参数微调即可提升优化性能，平均精确度比最先进的基于提示的方法高8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化进化优化方法存在三个关键局限：模式复用不足、错误感知改进能力弱、跨多样任务的检索鲁棒性差。需要一种新框架来克服这些缺陷，实现更高效可靠的自动化优化。

Method: OptGraph首先将可复用经验构建为类型化图，捕获建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节和错误修正之间的关联关系。推理阶段利用图邻域信息丰富检索知识，提供结构化上下文以改进建模、验证和迭代精化。此外支持自适应知识更新，将执行轨迹和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用图知识，无需进行LLM参数调优。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，OptGraph的平均精确度比最先进的基于提示的自动化优化框架高出8.9%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: OptGraph通过引入GraphRAG成功解决了现有LLM自动化进化优化方法在模式复用、错误感知和检索鲁棒性方面的不足，显著提升了优化性能，且无需参数调优即可实现知识持续积累和更新。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [108] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了由一维粘性Burgers方程与局部内部控制驱动的二次最优跟踪问题的全局指数turnpike性质，证明所有有限时域最优解在周期跟踪目标足够小时逼近唯一的周期最优态；零目标情形下获得原点处全局稳态turnpike，且对初始数据无小性假设。这是粘性Burgers方程的首个全局指数turnpike结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: turnpike性质描述了有限时域最优控制问题中，当时间视域趋于无穷时，最优轨迹在大部分时间区间内接近某个稳态或周期最优解的现象。已有结果多限于线性系统或需要初始数据满足小性条件，对于非线性演化方程（特别是粘性Burgers方程）的全局（对大初值成立）指数turnpike性质此前尚无定论，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明方法结合了两条技术路线：(1) 利用严格凸性与周期Riccati理论获得局部指数turnpike性质；(2) 利用抛物耗散性论证，构造一个与时间视域无关的吸收时间（absorbing time），使系统的动态在有限时间内进入局部可处理的区域，从而将局部结果提升为全局结果。

Result: 对于每个初始数据，当周期跟踪目标充分小时，有限时域最优解指数逼近唯一的周期最优态；零跟踪目标情形下，无需对初始数据作小性假设，即可获得原点处的全局稳态turnpike。这是粘性Burgers方程情形下首次得到的全局指数turnpike结果。

Conclusion: 本文通过将局部指数turnpike（基于严格凸性与周期Riccati理论）与抛物耗散吸收论证相结合，成功地为粘性Burgers方程建立了全局指数turnpike性质，为非线性偏微分方程最优控制中的长时域行为分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [109] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维布朗运动和独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动的风险资产下的投资组合效用最大化问题，允许策略纳入跳跃信号。采用鞅最优性原理推导带跳跃的倒向随机微分方程（BSDE），刻画值函数和最优策略，证明解的存在唯一性，处理异质跳跃信号情形，并给出高斯信号下对数效用的数值示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将指数效用函数下一维情形的组合效用最大化方法推广到更一般的多维框架，并纳入跳跃信号，以处理更复杂的市场动态和投资者收到异质跳跃信号的实际场景。

Method: 首先将组合动态表示为半鞅过程，然后利用鞅最优性原理推导带跳跃的BSDE来刻画值函数和最优策略，接着证明该BSDE解的存在唯一性，并针对异质跳跃信号的特例进行分析。

Result: 成功推导出带跳跃的BSDE表征框架，证明了相关BSDE解的存在唯一性，处理了异质跳跃信号情形，并通过对数效用下的高斯信号数值模拟提供了算例验证。

Conclusion: 该工作将一维指数效用情形的框架推广至多维布朗运动驱动的资产模型并纳入跳跃信号，为含跳跃市场中的组合优化提供了理论基础和数值方法，同时也涵盖异质信号这一实际扩展情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [110] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套严格的数学框架，用于解决矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集选择的逆综合问题，以确保强结构可控性（SSC）。作者证明了结构不可控性仅源于维度特定的可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价，并据此设计了两阶段综合方法，包括可达性先决条件和三种不同的对称破缺算法，并通过大量数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵加权网络中的最小领导者集选择以实现强结构可控性（SSC）的逆综合问题已被证明是NP难的，且至今未有解决方案。这一挑战阻碍了大规模网络控制设计的实际应用，需要严格的数学框架来系统性地解决。

Method: 本文采用两阶段综合方法：(1) 可达性先决阶段，用于识别结构根节点；(2) 三种对称破缺算法，包括贪心Weisfeiler-Lehman选择算法、子模边界最大化算法和划分熵最大化算法。数学证明确保对不变子空间和结构膨胀具有免疫力。

Result: 通过严格的数学证明验证了方法的正确性，证明其能免疫于不变子空间和结构膨胀问题，并在多种异构拓扑上进行了广泛的数值评估，验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了解决矩阵加权网络SSC最小领导者集选择问题的系统性框架，通过将问题分解为可达性条件和对称破缺两个阶段，在保证理论严谨性的同时实现了实际可行性。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [111] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个有限时间范围内的劳动力规划问题，其中每期员工流失服从二项分布（参数取决于招聘后的劳动力水平），存在固定招聘成本（无论招聘人数多少都需支付）。目标是最小化期望总成本（含招聘、工资和缺员惩罚成本）。通过引入Binomial-K-凸性概念证明最优招聘策略具有(s, S)型结构，并提出了一种混合整数线性规划（MILP）的分段近似方法进行求解。数值实验表明该方法计算速度快且最优性差距小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际劳动力规划中，员工流失具有随机性且流失概率与当前劳动力水平相关（决策依赖的转移概率），同时招聘存在固定成本。这类问题使得传统的动态规划方法难以直接处理，需要建立新的理论工具（如K-凸性扩展）来刻画最优策略结构，并提供可计算的高效求解方法。

Method: (1) 建立有限期随机动态规划模型，其中状态转移概率（二项分布）依赖于招聘决策；(2) 证明变量单期成本（期望工资与惩罚成本之和）的离散凸性，并引入全新的Binomial-K-凸性概念，证明Bellman函数在二项传播下保持K-凸性；(3) 基于K-凸性推导出最优策略为(s, S)型结构（低于阈值s时招聘至S，否则不招聘）；(4) 开发分段线性近似方法，将原问题转化为MILP进行求解，并为每期计算(s, S)参数。

Result: 证明了在二项流失过程下最优招聘策略具有(s, S)型结构；提出的MILP分段近似方法能够高效求解问题并计算各期的(s, S)参数；数值实验表明该方法计算速度快且最优性间隙小。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入Binomial-K-凸性这一新概念，成功将经典的(s, S)策略理论扩展到决策依赖的二项流失场景，为存在固定招聘成本的随机劳动力规划问题提供了理论最优性保证和实际可计算的求解框架。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [112] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了测度空间中的优化问题，该问题以到给定先验测度的传输距离作为正则化项。通过弱-*二阶次微分理论推导无间隙类型的二阶最优性条件，并在目标函数光滑部分和Kantorovich势的附加假设下，证明其与二次增长的等价性。文中计算了弱-*二阶次微分并证明了弱-*上皮可微性，最后将结果应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测度空间上的优化问题中，以最优传输距离作为正则化项是近年来的热点方向。然而，这类问题的二阶最优性条件分析尚不充分，特别是缺乏无间隙（no-gap）类型的二阶条件。作者旨在通过弱-*二阶次微分理论填补这一空白，建立二阶必要与充分条件之间的等价性。

Method: 采用弱-*二阶次微分理论框架来分析测度空间中的正则化优化问题。关键步骤包括：(1) 利用弱-*二阶次微分推导无间隙型二阶最优性条件；(2) 在目标函数光滑部分及Kantorovich势（对偶传输问题的解）的附加假设下，建立与二次增长的等价性；(3) 具体计算弱-*二阶次微分表达式；(4) 证明弱-*上皮可微性。

Result: 成功推导出测度空间中带传输距离正则化的优化问题的无间隙型二阶最优性条件；证明了这些条件在适当假设下与二次增长条件等价；给出了弱-*二阶次微分的显式计算；证明了弱-*上皮可微性性质。

Conclusion: 建立了测度空间中以最优传输距离为惩罚项的优化问题的完整二阶变分分析框架，并将该理论成功应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题，为该类问题的数值求解和理论分析提供了坚实基础的二阶理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [113] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究实Hilbert空间中有限族真、下半连续、凸函数的邻近算子系统解的适定性问题，建立精确解与近似解存在的充要条件，提出逆邻近性质(IPP)作为逆最佳逼近性质(IBAP)的一般化推广，并将结果应用于可行性与信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典投影问题是凸分析中的基础工具，本文将其推广到邻近算子系统层面。投影问题作为邻近算子的特例，自然引导出对一般凸函数邻近算子方程组解的存在性与唯一性研究。这一推广不仅统一了若干已知结果，也为更广泛的优化与逆问题提供了理论框架。

Method: 首先将投影方程系统推广为邻近算子方程系统(每个方程形如prox_f_i(x)=p_i)，建立精确解存在的必要条件与充分条件；随后建立近似解存在的充要条件；在此基础上引入逆邻近性质(IPP)的概念，并给出其多种等价刻画，类似于IBAP的推广路径；最后将理论结果应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题进行分析验证。

Result: 给出了邻近算子方程组精确解存在性与唯一性的判定条件，建立了近似解存在的充要条件，引入了IPP性质并导出了若干等价特征刻画，证明了该框架在可行性与信号恢复问题中的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该研究将经典的投影问题系统系统地推广至一般邻近算子系统，通过IPP概念统一并扩展了IBAP理论，为Hilbert空间中的优化与逆问题提供了新的理论工具和分析框架。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策图（DD）的割平面生成方法用于二元多项式优化（BPO）。针对超图多线性集，该方法对任意选定的支撑结构，在局部多线性多胞形中分离出定义面的割，并将其提升回原问题。通过一种新的递归紧凑DD编码（仅显式表示顶点变量，超边变量隐式编码在状态中），还获得了多线性多胞形的扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（揭示了一类新的多项式可解超图），以及生成割面性的证书。多种支撑策略的实验表明，该方法在根节点处以更少割实现更大间隙收拢，并显著加速分支定界过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的强不等式（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）是针对固定支撑模式手工推导的，缺乏通用性。本文旨在提出一种系统化、自动化的割平面生成框架，能够对任意支撑结构生成定义面的割，从而突破传统手工推导的局限。

Method: 提出决策图（DD）方法：对任意选定的支撑，在局部多线性多胞形中分离定义面的割并提升回原问题。引入基于递归表述的新型紧凑DD编码，仅表示顶点变量而将超边变量隐式编码在状态表示中。由此DD编码进一步导出扩展公式、基于有效反链的宽度刻画（发现新的多项式可解超图类），以及割面性证书。探索三种支撑策略：复用、扩展或划分局部结构为section超图。

Result: 实验表明，DD-based方法在根节点处以更少的割数实现更大的gap closure，相比现有方法有显著提升，并大幅加速分支定界过程。此外，宽度刻画揭示了一类新的多项式可解超图。

Conclusion: 基于决策图的割平面生成方法为BPO提供了一种通用、自动化的强割生成框架，兼具理论保证（面性证书、宽度刻画）和计算优越性，是传统手工推导割平面方法的有力替代方案。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>

### [115] [Study of $t\bar{t}H$ and $tH$ production in the $H\toττ$ channel in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27276)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了在τ轻子对衰变道中，利用ATLAS探测器在质心能量13 TeV（140 fb⁻¹）和13.6 TeV（161 fb⁻¹）的质子-质子对撞数据，研究希格斯玻色子与顶夸克对（$t\bar{t}H$）或单个顶夸克（$tH$）联合产生过程。测量的信号强度为$μ_{t\bar{t}H}=1.51^{+0.71}_{-0.62}$和$μ_{tH}=-0.4^{+5.6}_{-5.1}$，并在简化模板截面框架下对$t\bar{t}H$截面按希格斯玻色子横动量进行了三个分箱的微分测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量希格斯玻色子与顶夸克联合产生过程是检验标准模型预言、探测希格斯玻色子与顶夸克汤川耦合强度的重要途径，尤其是$tH$过程对电弱耦合的异常敏感，可用于探测新物理信号。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器记录的$\sqrt{s}=$13 TeV和13.6 TeV pp对撞数据（光度分别为140 fb⁻¹和161 fb⁻¹），在顶夸克全强子衰变和τ轻子对（含全强子衰变）的末态中寻找$t\bar{t}H$和$tH$产生信号，并采用简化模板截面框架对$t\bar{t}H$截面进行微分测量。

Result: 测得$μ_{t\bar{t}H}=1.51^{+0.71}_{-0.62}$，与标准模型预言一致（约1.5σ偏离）；$μ_{tH}=-0.4^{+5.6}_{-5.1}$，不确定性较大，尚未对$tH$过程给出显著观测。同时给出了$t\bar{t}H$截面在希格斯玻色子$p_T$三个分箱中的微分测量结果。

Conclusion: $t\bar{t}H$过程的测量结果与标准模型吻合，$tH$过程由于统计量有限仍受较大不确定性限制。该研究为精确检验希格斯-顶夸克耦合提供了新的实验约束。

Abstract: A study of the production of the Higgs boson in association with either a top-quark pair ($t\bar{t}H$) or a single top quark ($tH$) in the $τ$-lepton-pair decay channel is presented. The analysis relies on final states featuring fully hadronic decays of the top quarks and the $τ$-leptons. It employs data samples of proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ and 13.6 TeV recorded with the ATLAS detector at the CERN Large Hadron Collider and corresponding to integrated luminosities of 140 fb$^{-1}$ and 161 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. The measured signal strength ($μ$), defined as the measured cross-section normalised to the Standard Model prediction, is $μ_{t\bar{t}H}=1.51^{+0.71}_{-0.62}$ for $t\bar{t}H$ and $μ_{tH}=-0.4^{+5.6}_{-5.1}$ for $tH$. Additionally, the $t\bar{t}H$ cross-section is measured differentially in three bins of the Higgs boson transverse momentum in the simplified template cross-section framework.

</details>


### [116] [Search for heavy Majorana neutrinos in vector boson scattering with $τ$-lepton final states with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27307)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了ATLAS探测器在LHC上利用140 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据（√s=13 TeV），通过同号ττ、eτ或μτ终态搜索与第三代轻子耦合的重马约拉纳中微子在矢量玻色子散射中的产生。未发现超出标准模型背景的显著偏差，给出了92 GeV至6.5 TeV质量范围内|V_{τN}|²的约束，并将排除极限扩展至1.5 TeV以上尚未探索的质量区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重马约拉纳中微子（HMN）是解释中微子质量和跷跷板机制的重要候选新物理对象，尤其是与第三代轻子（τ）的耦合在实验上探索较少。矢量玻色子散射（VBS）过程为产生HMN提供了独特而干净的探针，本研究旨在填补高质量区域（>1.5 TeV）的空白。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器记录的140 fb⁻¹高亮度数据，在√s=13 TeV的pp对撞中搜索VBS过程产生的HMN，最终态为同号ττ、eτ或μτ轻子对。通过排除标准模型背景后，基于信号期望对混合矩阵元|V_{τN}|²设置上限，并给出与|V_{eN}|²、|V_{μN}|²的二维联合约束。

Result: 未观察到显著超出标准模型背景的信号。给出了重马约拉纳中微子质量为92 GeV至6.5 TeV范围的对|V_{τN}|²的约束，以及二维混合参数约束。在质量大于1.0 TeV时改善了对第三代轻子耦合HMN的限制，并将排除极限扩展到1.5 TeV以上的新质量范围。

Conclusion: 该研究为与τ轻子耦合的重马约拉纳中微子提供了目前最强的直接搜索限制，尤其在高质量区间（>1.0 TeV）显著改善了此前的结果，并首次将排除范围延伸至1.5 TeV以上的未探索区域，对未来更高能对撞实验及合作模型具有重要约束意义。

Abstract: A search for heavy Majorana neutrinos coupling to third-generation leptons in vector boson scattering at the LHC is presented. The search is performed in the final state with a same-sign $ττ$, $eτ$ or $μτ$ pair using data of proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, recorded with the ATLAS detector and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 $\text{fb}^{-1}$. No significant deviation from the Standard Model background is observed. Constraints on the squared mixing matrix element between the $τ$-lepton and the heavy Majorana neutrino ($|V_{τN}|^2$) are derived for heavy Majorana neutrino masses between 92 GeV and 6.5 TeV. Additionally, two-dimensional constraints on ($|V_{eN}|^{2}, |V_{τN}|^{2}$) and ($|V_{μN}|^{2}, |V_{τN}|^{2}$) are set. These results improve the limits on heavy Majorana neutrinos coupling to third-generation leptons for heavy Majorana neutrino masses above 1.0 TeV, and extend the exclusion limits to an unexplored mass range above 1.5 TeV.

</details>


### [117] [First Experimental Bounds on Transverse Plasmon Solar Axions with ANAIS-112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27346)
*S. J. Hollick,M. Giannotti,J. Ruz,J. K. Vogel,R. M. Alkaddah,J. Amaré,J. Apilluelo,S. Bharat,S. Cebrián,D. Cintas,I. Coarasa,E. García,M. Martínez,Y. Ortigoza,A. Ortiz de Solórzano,T. Pardo,J. Puimedón,M. L. Sarsa,C. Seoane*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次实验搜索了通过太阳内部磁场中横等离激元共振转换产生的太阳类轴子粒子（ALPs），利用ANAIS-112探测器625.75 kg×yr的曝光数据，对由地球-太阳距离变化引起的年调制信号进行探测，最终在m_a≈150 eV处给出轴子-光子耦合强度的首个限制g_aγ < 1.32×10⁻⁹ GeV⁻¹（90% C.L.），并在35-280 eV质量范围内扩展了探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Primakoff过程是太阳轴子产生的主要机制，但太阳内部磁场中横等离激元可通过共振转换为轴子，这是一种此前未被实验探索的补充产生机制，可能显著增强特定质量范围内的轴子通量。

Method: 利用ANAIS-112探测器625.75 kg×yr曝光数据，搜索由地球-太阳距离变化引起的年调制信号；假设通过探测器中的逆Primakoff转换进行探测；在1-20 keV范围内以1 keV为间隔拟合调制振幅。

Result: 拟合结果与无调制假设一致，未发现显著调制信号。在m_a≈150 eV处给出首个包含共振横等离激元通量的轴子-光子耦合限制g_aγ < 1.32×10⁻⁹ GeV⁻¹（90% C.L.），覆盖35-280 eV质量范围。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实验探索了太阳横等离激元共振产生轴子的通道，在预期的太阳磁场模型下，该通道的通量在35-280 eV范围内比Primakoff过程高出两个数量级，从而显著扩展了ANAIS-112在该质量区间的探测灵敏度。

Abstract: Solar axion-like particles (ALPs) can be resonantly produced through the conversion of transverse plasmons in the magnetic field of the solar interior, introducing a production mechanism complementary to the conventional Primakoff process. In this Letter, we report the first experimental search for solar ALPs produced through this mechanism. Using an exposure of 625.75 kg$\times$yr from ANAIS-112, we search for the annual modulation of the flux induced by the variation of the Earth--Sun distance, assuming detection through inverse Primakoff conversion in the detector. Modulation amplitudes fitted in 1 keV bins between 1--20 keV are consistent with the absence of modulation, yielding the first constraints on the axion--photon coupling that include the resonant transverse-plasmon flux, $g_{aγ} < 1.32\times 10^{-9} \mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ (90\% C.L.) at $m_a \simeq 150$ eV. This previously unexplored production channel extends the sensitivity of ANAIS-112 in the mass range 35--280 eV, where, for the expected solar magnetic-field models, the transverse-plasmon flux exceeds the Primakoff flux by up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [118] [Search for $\textit{tH}$ production in the $ H \to ττ$ decay mode, and a combination with other searches, using $\textit{pp}$ collisions at 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27708)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了ATLAS探测器利用LHC上140 fb⁻¹的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，在包含τ轻子的多轻子末态中搜索单个顶夸克伴随希格斯玻色子（tH）产生过程。虽然未观察到显著超出背景的信号，但与之前不同末态的结果组合后，得到信号强度μ_tH = 3.3⁻¹.⁵⁺¹.⁷倍标准模型预言，观测（预期）显著性为2.3（0.8）个标准差，这是迄今为止对tH产生过程最精确的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tH产生过程是测量希格斯玻色子与顶夸克汤川耦合（Yukawa coupling）的直接探针，可用于检验标准模型预言，并搜索超越标准模型的新物理信号。该过程极为稀有，需要高统计量数据和多通道组合才能获得有意义的结果。

Method: 分析使用ATLAS探测器采集的140 fb⁻¹、√s=13 TeV数据，靶向H→ττ衰变通道，要求至少一个τ轻子强子衰变，在多轻子末态中搜索tH产生信号。结果与ATLAS合作组此前针对相同产生过程但不同末态（使用140 fb⁻¹@13 TeV及164 fb⁻¹@13.6 TeV数据）的测量进行统计组合。

Result: 单独分析中未观察到显著超出预期背景的过量，设置信号强度在95%置信水平上的观测（预期）上限为标准模型预言的8.6（9.3）倍。组合测量给出μ_tH = 3.3⁻¹.⁵⁺¹.⁷ = 3.3 ± 1.2（统计）⁻¹.⁰⁺¹.¹（系统）倍标准模型期望，对应观测（预期）显著性为2.3（0.8）个标准差，组合后的95%置信水平上限为6.1（2.7）倍标准模型预言。

Conclusion: 该组合测量代表了迄今为止对tH产生过程最精确的测量结果。虽然组合信号强度略高于标准模型预期且观测显著性仅为2.3个标准差，尚不足以确认tH产生的新物理信号，但为未来更高统计量的测量提供了重要参考基线。

Abstract: A search for the production of a Higgs boson in association with a single top quark in multi-lepton final states including $τ$-leptons is presented, using an integrated luminosity of $140\,\text{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13\,\text{TeV}$ collected by the ATLAS detector at the LHC. The analysis targets the $H \to ττ$ decay, where at least one $τ$-lepton decays hadronically, to indicate the presence of the Higgs boson. No significant excess over the expected background is observed, and an observed (expected) upper limit at the 95% confidence level on the signal strength is set at 8.6 (9.3) times the Standard Model prediction. This measurement is combined with previous publications of the ATLAS Collaboration targeting the same production process but in different final states, using $140\,\text{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s} = 13\,\text{TeV}$ and $164\,\text{fb}^{-1}$ at $13.6\,\text{TeV}$. The combined measurement of the signal strength yields $μ_{tH} = 3.3^{+1.7}_{-1.5} = 3.3 \pm 1.2\,(\text{stat.})^{+1.1}_{-1.0}\,(\text{sys.})$ times the Standard Model expectation, corresponding to an observed (expected) $\textit{tH}$ significance of 2.3 (0.8) standard deviations. The combined observed (expected) upper limit at the 95% confidence level on the signal strength is set at 6.1 (2.7) times the Standard Model prediction. This combination represents the most precise measurement of $tH$ production to date.

</details>


### [119] [Comment on the preprint: First Limits on Axion Dark Matter from a DALI Prototype arXiv:2603.21951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28095)
*Erika Garutti,Anton Ivanov,Béla Majorovits*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对DALI装置探测轴子样粒子（ALP）暗物质的灵敏度进行了独立评估，基于预印本信息和MADMAX合作组开发的 formalism，未发现12 GHz以下存在与轴子场显著耦合的模式来解释所报告的灵敏度，并提供了评估介质电介质haloscope实验中电磁模式轴子耦合的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对DALI装置所宣称的探测ALP暗物质灵敏度进行独立验证和批判性审查，确保实验设计的准确性和可信度。

Method: 基于预印本提供的信息，采用MADMAX合作组发展的形式主义框架，对DALI装置在12 GHz以下的电磁模式与轴子场的耦合强度进行独立分析和计算。

Result: 在12 GHz以下未识别出任何与轴子场具有显著耦合的电磁模式，无法支撑DALI所报告的灵敏度。

Conclusion: DALI预印本中报告的灵敏度可能存在问题，需要重新评估其对轴子暗物质的探测能力，并为介质haloscope实验中电磁模式轴子耦合的评估提供了改进建议。

Abstract: We report on an independent assessment of the sensitivity of the DALI setup to axion-like particle (ALP) dark matter, as described in [1]. Our analysys is based on the information provided in the preprint and using the formalism developed within the MADMAX collaboration. We do not identify any mode below 12 GHz with significant coupling to the axion field to account for the reported sensitivity. We provide recommendations for assessing the axion coupling of electromagnetic modes in dielectric haloscope experiments.

</details>


### [120] [Search for high-mass dilepton resonances in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV combined with 13 TeV results using the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28334)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文使用ATLAS探测器在LHC上记录的165 fb⁻¹（13.6 TeV）和140 fb⁻¹（13 TeV）质子对撞数据，搜索高能区自旋为1的ee和μμ谐振信号。未发现显著偏差，并在95%置信水平上给出自旋1谐振子的截面上限，同时为多种基准模型（如Z′_SSM、Z′_χ、Z′_ψ）提取了质量极限（5.5 TeV、5.1 TeV、4.8 TeV），是目前对该类Z'模型最严格的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索超出标准模型的高质量双轻子谐振，以检验是否存在新的中性规范玻色子（Z′），这是寻找新物理的重要探针。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器在13.6 TeV（2022-2024年，165 fb⁻¹）和13 TeV（2015-2018年，140 fb⁻¹）两个质子-质子碰撞数据集，对双轻子不变质量谱进行函数形式拟合以构建标准模型背景模型，使用通用的自旋1信号形状检验与背景预期的偏差，并统计组合两期数据结果。

Result: 未观察到显著的信号偏差。在95%置信水平上对自旋1谐振子的截面×分支比设置了上限（针对不同宽度假设，分别给出ee、μμ末态及其组合）。提取了多种基准模型的质量限制：Z′_SSM为5.5 TeV，Z′_χ为5.1 TeV，Z′_ψ为4.8 TeV。

Conclusion: 所考虑的高质量双轻子谐振Z′模型未出现，本研究给出了目前最严格的限制，显著排除了这些模型在相应质量阈值以下的区域。

Abstract: A search for spin-1 $ee$ and $μμ$ resonances in the high-mass range is presented. Two proton-proton ($pp$) collision datasets recorded by the ATLAS detector at CERN's Large Hadron Collider are utilised. A search is first performed using a dataset recorded at a center-of-mass energy of 13.6 TeV between 2022 and 2024, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 165 fb$^{-1}$. The results of this search are subsequently statistically combined with results using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of data recorded between 2015 and 2018 at 13 TeV. The dilepton invariant mass spectrum is fitted with a functional form to model the contribution from Standard Model background processes. A generic spin-1 signal shape is used to determine the significance of observed deviations from the background expectation. No significant deviation is observed, therefore upper limits are placed at the 95% confidence level on the fiducial cross-section times branching ratio for a spin-1 resonance with various width hypotheses, individually for $ee$, $μμ$ final states and for their combination. From the combined 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV results, mass limits are extracted for various benchmark model bosons, including a $Z^{\prime}_{\text{SSM}}$ (5.5 TeV), $Z^{\prime}_χ$ (5.1 TeV) and $Z^{\prime}_ψ$ (4.8 TeV). These results represent the most stringent limits to date on high-mass dilepton resonances on the considered $Z'$ models.

</details>


### [121] [Search for pair-production of vector-like $T$ quarks decaying into a top quark and a spin-0 particle in the diphoton final state in proton proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28381)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ATLAS实验利用2015-2018年采集的140 fb^-1 pp对撞数据（质心能量13 TeV），对成对产生的矢量类T夸克（VLQ）进行搜索。每个VLQ衰变为一个顶夸克和一个奇异（赝）标量粒子S，目标事件包含至少一个共振S→γγ衰变并伴有大的横向动量之和与缺失横向动量。这是首次在此末态进行VLQ搜索，未观察到超过标准模型背景的显著超出，结果以VLQ和自旋0粒子质量函数的基准截面×分支比上限形式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理，首次在包含共振双光子衰变的末态中搜索矢量类T夸克（VLQ），以检验VLQ通过级联衰变产生电弱规范玻色子或t\bar{t}末态的理论预言。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器2015-2018年记录的140 fb^-1 pp对撞数据，针对成对产生的VLQ事件，要求每个VLQ衰变为顶夸克加奇异（赝）标量S，选取至少一个共振S→γγ衰变以及大的横动量与缺失横动量作为触发条件，采用模型无关的搜索策略并定义基准（fiducial）区域。

Result: 未观察到超出标准模型背景的显著信号，设定VLQ与自旋0粒子质量函数的基准截面乘分支比的上限。

Conclusion: 这是首次在该末态中进行VLQ搜索，未发现新物理信号；所给出的上限为未来在更高能量或更多数据下探索VLQ信号提供了重要约束。

Abstract: A search for pair-produced vector-like T quarks (VLQs) is presented. Each VLQ is assumed to decay into a top quark and an exotic (pseudo)scalar S. The search targets events with at least one resonant $S \to γγ$ decay and large summed transverse momentum and missing transverse momentum from other final-state particles. The analysis uses data from $pp$ collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector from 2015 to 2018 and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. This is the first VLQ search performed in this final state. No significant excess over the Standard Model background is observed. The analysis is model-independent to a large extent but targets cascade decays into final states with electroweak bosons or $t\bar{t}$. Results are provided in the form of upper limits on the fiducial cross-section times branching ratio as a function of the masses of the VLQ and the spin-0 particle. The fiducial volume is defined by kinematic requirements on the photons from the spin-0 decay.

</details>


### [122] [A measurement of the Higgs boson mass in the diphoton decay channel in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28396)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（2016-2018年，积分亮度138 fb⁻¹）中，利用双光子衰变道测量希格斯玻色子质量的结果，测得m_H = 125.13 ± 0.15 GeV，并与7和8 TeV的测量结果组合，得到m_H = 125.06 ± 0.14 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过改进探测器校准和采用新的分析技术，提高希格斯玻色子质量在双光子衰变道中的测量精度，并与其他能级下的测量结果进行组合，以获得更精确的希格斯玻色子质量值。

Method: 使用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中收集的数据（2016-2018年，对应积分亮度138 fb⁻¹），通过双光子（H→γγ）衰变道进行希格斯玻色子质量测量，采用改进的探测器校准和新的分析技术以提高精度，并与7和8 TeV能级下的质量测量结果进行组合。

Result: 单独测量得到m_H = 125.13 ± 0.15 GeV，其中统计不确定度为±0.10 GeV，系统不确定度为±0.12 GeV；与7和8 TeV的测量结果组合后得到m_H = 125.06 ± 0.14 GeV，其中统计不确定度为±0.09 GeV，系统不确定度为±0.11 GeV。

Conclusion: 通过改进校准和分析技术，成功提高了希格斯玻色子质量在双光子衰变道的测量精度，组合测量获得了更高精度的希格斯玻色子质量值。

Abstract: A measurement of the Higgs boson mass in the diphoton decay channel is performed using proton-proton collision data at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. The data set recorded with the CMS detector between 2016 and 2018 is used, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. A refined detector calibration and new analysis techniques are employed to improve the precision of the results compared to earlier measurements. The Higgs boson mass is measured to be $m_\mathrm{H}$ = 125.13 $\pm$ 0.15 GeV = 125.13 $\pm$ 0.10 (stat) $\pm$ 0.12 (syst) GeV. In addition, a combination with the mass measurement at center-of-mass energies of 7 and 8 TeV in the diphoton final state is performed resulting in $m_\mathrm{H}$ = 125.06 $\pm$ 0.14 GeV = 125.06 $\pm$ 0.09 (stat) $\pm$ 0.11 (syst) GeV.

</details>


### [123] [Search for Lorentz-boosted di-$τ$ resonances produced in association with top quark pairs in $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV pp collisions with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28484)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文使用ATLAS探测器在√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分光度140 fb⁻¹）中，搜索与top-antitop夸克对联合产生、衰变为τ轻子对的Lorentz-boosted赝标量共振a（质量范围20-85 GeV）。两个τ轻子均在强子衰变通道重建，采用定制的高动量双τ候选物体。未观察到超出标准模型背景的显著偏差，在95%置信水平下对可见截面设置了0.19 fb的模型无关上限，并在双希格斯二重态模型场景下给出了σ(tt̄a)×BR(a→ττ)的限制，从m_a=20 GeV时的0.4 pb降至m_a=85 GeV时的0.05 pb。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越标准模型的新物理，特别是寻找可能的赝标量Higgs玻色子或相关的新共振态，通过tt̄a产生过程探测质量在20-85 GeV区间内的轻赝标量粒子，为研究Higgs玻色子性质及可能的扩展Higgs扇区提供实验约束。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器收集的√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（140 fb⁻¹），针对Lorentz-boosted产生机制，使用定制的重建、能量标定和鉴别程序构建高动量双τ（di-τ）候选物体。两个τ轻子均在强子衰变通道（τ→hadrons+ντ）重建，分析tt̄a伴随产生模式并拟合ττ不变质量分布以区分信号和标准模型背景。

Result: 未发现相对于标准模型预期背景的显著信号偏差。在95%置信水平下，对可见截面设置模型无关上限为0.19 fb。在双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）信号场景下，对σ(tt̄a)×BR(a→ττ)给出限制：m_a=20 GeV时为0.4 pb，m_a=85 GeV时降至0.05 pb。

Conclusion: 该搜索在质量区间20-85 GeV内未发现新的赝标量共振证据，所给出的模型无关可见截面上限及2HDM场景下的产额限制为未来更高灵敏度搜索提供了基准，并进一步收紧了轻质量赝标量在tt̄伴随产生过程中的参数空间约束。

Abstract: A search for a Lorentz-boosted pseudoscalar resonance $a$ in the mass range between 20 GeV and 85 GeV, produced in association with a top-antitop quark pair and decaying into a $τ$-lepton pair, is presented. Both $τ$-leptons are reconstructed in the hadronic decay mode. The boosted production of $a$ results in a small angular separation between its decay products, requiring the use of dedicated boosted di-$τ$ objects with custom reconstruction, energy calibration, and identification procedures. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data at a centre-of-mass-energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV collected with the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. No significant deviation is observed over the expected Standard Model background, and a model independent upper limit of 0.19 fb is placed at the 95% confidence level on the visible cross-section. Limits on $σ(t\bar{t}a) \times BR(a \to ττ)$ are also obtained for a Two-Higgs-Doublet Model signal scenario, ranging from 0.4 pb at $m_a=20$ GeV to 0.05 pb at $m_a=85$ GeV.

</details>


### [124] [Extraction of $σ_{TT}$ for Proton, Neutron, Deuteron and $^3$He from Quasi-real Photon Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28612)
*YiLei Li,B. Callahan,M. M. Dalton,A. Deur,O. Larson,A. Rask,D. W. Upton,X. Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究通过将电子散射数据外推到实光子点，提取了质子、氘核、中子和³He的极化光生截面，并与实光子数据进行比较。质子结果与实光子数据一致，但氘核和中子在Δ(1232)区域表现出更大的强度，且与质子结果相比更符合同位旋对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过虚光子外推获取实光子点的极化光生截面数据，以检验不同核子/原子核在Δ(1232)共振区域的行为，并验证同位旋对称性。

Method: 利用Jefferson Lab的E97-110(³He)和CLAS EG4(质子与氘核)实验的电子散射数据，通过外推到实光子点(Q²=0)提取极化光生截面。中子数据采用弱束缚近似从氘核或³He数据中提取。

Result: 质子结果与实光子数据一致；氘核和中子的结果在Δ(1232)区域显示出比实光子数据更强的截面强度，且与质子结果对比更符合同位旋对称性的预期。

Conclusion: 该外推方法在质子情况下验证可靠，但对氘核和中子揭示了Δ(1232)区域中与直接实光子测量不同的行为，为理解核内束缚效应和同位旋对称性提供了新的约束。

Abstract: We report on an extraction of the polarized photoproduction cross-section for the proton, deuteron, neutron and $^3$He, obtained by extrapolating electron scattering data to the real photon point. The data are from the Jefferson Lab E97-110 ($^3$He) and CLAS EG4 (proton and deuteron) experiments. Information on the neutron is extracted from the deuteron or $^3$He data using the weak binding approximation. Comparing with data obtained with real photons, we find that while the proton results agree, our results on the deuteron and the neutron exhibit a larger strength in the $Δ(1232)$ region, and are more consistent with isospin symmetry when compared with the proton results.

</details>

### [125] [Parametrized $\underline{\mathbb{F}}_2$-Cohomology of $B_{C_2}O(1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27398)
*Agnès Beaudry,Chloe Lewis,Clover May,Sabrina Pauli,Elizabeth Tatum*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了实$C_2$-线束分类空间$B_{C_2}O(1)$以常值Mackey函子$\underline{\mathbb{F}}_2$为系数的参数化（或扭曲）普通上同调。参数化上同调通过将所有局部系数的等变上同调组装成一个分次环，细化了$RO(G)$-分次Bredon上同调。该工作还编码了$B_{C_2}O(1)$上所有实$C_2$-向量丛的Thom空间的$RO(C_2)$-分次上同调的计算。此外，论文建立了多个可应用于一般基空间$B$上参数化上同调计算的通用结果，包括特征类、非齐次丛的定向定义、等变Steenrod运算的引入以及基变换相关的通用结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化上同调是$RO(G)$-分次Bredon上同调的推广，能更精细地刻画等变拓扑空间的上同调结构。作者的目标是计算$B_{C_2}O(1)$（实$C_2$-线束的分类空间）的参数化上同调，并且在此过程中发展一套可用于一般基空间的通用计算工具。

Method: 作者系统地计算了$B_{C_2}O(1)$上以常值Mackey函子为系数的参数化普通上同调。推导过程中引入了新的特征类体系，给出了非齐次丛的定向概念，将等变Steenrod运算引入该参数化框架，并建立了关于基变换的一般性定理。这些工具共同构成了完整的计算方法论。

Result: 成功计算了$B_{C_2}O(1)$的参数化普通上同调环结构，该结果同时编码了所有实$C_2$-向量丛的Thom空间的$RO(C_2)$-分次上同调。此外，论文还建立了一系列适用于一般基空间$B$的参数化上同调计算的一般性工具和定理。

Conclusion: 本文既完成了$B_{C_2}O(1)$这一具体对象的参数化上同调计算，又为参数化上同调的广泛计算提供了新的方法论工具（特征类、非齐次丛定向、等变Steenrod运算、基变换定理等），具有较强的推广价值。

Abstract: We compute the parametrized (or twisted) ordinary cohomology of the classifying space for real $C_2$-line bundles, $B_{C_2}O(1)$ with coefficients in the constant Mackey functor $\underline{\mathbb{F}}_2$. Parametrized cohomology refines $RO(G)$-graded Bredon cohomology by assembling equivariant cohomology for all local coefficients into a single graded ring. For this reason, our work also encodes a computation of the $RO(C_2)$-graded cohomology of all Thom spaces of real $C_2$-vector bundles over $B_{C_2}O(1)$.
  Along the way, we prove many general results that can be applied to computations of parametrized cohomology over general bases $B$. In particular, we introduce a collection of characteristic classes, give a definition of orientation for non-homogeneous bundles, import equivariant Steenrod operations to this context, and give general results relating to base change.

</details>


### [126] [Components in characteristic $p$ and Quillen's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27500)
*Kevin Ivan Piterman*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在温和的归纳假设下，若群G包含一个特征为p的Lie型单群作为其分量且O_p(G)=1，则G在p处的Quillen偏序集具有非零有理同调，从而排除了此类分量出现在Quillen猜想的极小反例中的可能性，尤其是在p=2（猜想仍开放）时具有特别意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quillen猜想是一个关于有限群的p-子群偏序集同调性质的著名未解猜想，尤其在p=2的情况下仍然开放。为了推进该猜想的证明，需要排除极小反例中可能出现的各种群分量结构。本文旨在处理其中一类关键情形——特征为p的Lie型单群分量。

Method: 采用归纳论证方法。作者在温和的归纳假设条件下，对包含特征p的Lie型简单群分量的群G进行分析，通过验证其Quillen偏序集具有非零有理同调来排除这类群作为极小反例的可能性。论证的核心是处理O_p(G)=1这一条件并利用Quillen偏序集的同调性质。

Result: 在温和的归纳假设下，证明了若群G包含一个特征为p的Lie型简单群作为分量且O_p(G)=1，则G在p处的Quillen偏序集具有非零有理同调。这意味着此类分量不会出现在Quillen猜想的极小反例中。

Conclusion: 该结果排除了Quillen猜想极小反例中一类重要的群分量，为p=2情形下Quillen猜想的最终解决提供了实质性进展。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to show that, under mild inductive assumptions, if a group $G$ contains a component that is a simple group of Lie type in characteristic $p$ and $O_p(G) = 1$, then the Quillen poset of $G$ at $p$ has nonzero rational homology. In particular, this shows that such components cannot arise in a minimal counterexample to Quillen's conjecture. This result is of particular interest at the prime $p=2$, where the conjecture is still open.

</details>


### [127] [Rational higher order Whitehead products of mapping spaces and defining systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27663)
*Takahito Naito*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文给出了映射空间中有理高阶Whitehead积的代数描述：给定基映射的Sullivan代表，在对应的导子复形中引入定义系统，证明了有理高阶Whitehead积恰好对应于导子复形上同调类所代表的导子括号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶Whitehead积是代数拓扑中重要的不变量，但此前对其在映射空间中有理情形的代数描述尚不完整，需要给出系统性的代数刻画。

Method: 利用Sullivan模型构造导子复形，在导子复形中引入定义系统（defining systems），通过导子括号（derivation brackets）在导子复形的上同调中表示有理高阶Whitehead积。

Result: 在映射空间有理同伦群与导子复形上同调之间的标准对应下，有理高阶Whitehead积恰好由相关的导子括号所代表的上同调类给出；示例展示了定义系统的不同选择如何产生非平凡元素及不确定性（indeterminacy）。

Conclusion: 该工作为映射空间中的有理高阶Whitehead积提供了完全代数的描述，并揭示了定义系统选择对结果的影响，深化了有理同伦论中高阶运算的理解。

Abstract: This paper gives an algebraic description of rational higher order Whitehead products in mapping spaces. Given a Sullivan representative of the base map, we introduce defining systems in the corresponding derivation complex. The main result shows that, under the standard identification between rational homotopy groups of mapping spaces and the cohomology of the derivation complex, rational higher order Whitehead products are given exactly by the cohomology classes represented by the associated derivation brackets. The examples show how choices of defining systems produce non-trivial elements and indeterminacy.

</details>


### [128] [The Euler Characteristic Transform from a Convex Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28021)
*Jesús Gacías Franco*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过建立凸几何的支撑函数与拓扑数据分析中的欧拉特征变换（ECT）之间的联系，提出了新的ECT诱导伪距离、推广的Steiner点定义、Steiner条形码等新工具，并给出了从ECT恢复平面形状周长的显式公式，最后在形状分类任务中验证了这些方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ECT管道在处理具有不同欧拉特征（Euler characteristic）的形状比较时需要截断参数（cutoff parameter），且缺乏对常见欧几里得等距变换的天然不变性。作者希望通过引入凸几何的工具来克服这些局限，发展更稳健、更通用的拓扑数据分析方法。

Method: 作者首先在凸几何的支撑函数与ECT之间建立理论联系，据此定义了新的ECT诱导伪距离，该距离对欧几里得等距变换不变且无需截断参数。接着将凸几何中Steiner点的概念推广到一般情形，定义为由ECT提供的特殊点。利用持久同调（persistent homology），进一步定义了Steiner条形码作为形状的等距不变特征，以及支撑函数和Steiner点的同调变体。同时给出了从ECT恢复平面形状周长的显式公式。

Result: 在形状分类任务中，所提出的轻量级特征在对齐和未对齐数据集上均表现良好，验证了这些新工具的实际效用。

Conclusion: 凸几何与拓扑数据分析的交叉为ECT管道提供了新的理论支撑和实践改进，包括等距不变的伪距离、无截断参数的优势，以及Steiner条形码等新型特征，为形状分析和分类提供了更优越的工具。

Abstract: By examining the relationship between the support function of convex geometry and the Euler Characteristic Transform (ECT) of topological data analysis, we develop new tools and suggest variations on some common ECT pipelines. Specifically, we put forward new definitions of ECT-induced pseudodistances, which have the advantage of being invariant under common euclidean isometries and require no cutoff parameter to compare shapes with distinct Euler characteristic. These definitions rely on a generalization of the convex geometric concept of the Steiner point, which we define in general as a distinguished point given by the ECT. We also show how convex geometry provides a path to recover interesting geometric information of a flat shape from its ECT, namely, its perimeter, for which we give an explicit formula. By building on these concepts and leveraging persistent homology, we define Steiner barcodes as an isometry invariant feature of shapes, as well as homological variants of the support function and Steiner point. Finally, we put these constructions to the test in shape classification tasks, providing lightweight features for aligned and misaligned datasets.

</details>


### [129] [Realizing additive monoids as mapping degree sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27993)
*Cristina Costoya,Vicente Muñoz,Bruno Valverde-Morales,Antonio Viruel*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明映射度集在乘以包含0的有限整数子集以及由加法子幺半群经有限次加减乘所得集合下具有稳定性，从而得到一大类无限映射度集（包括从0开始的有限个算术级数之并），推广了此前关于实现问题的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 映射度集的实现问题是几何拓扑中的经典问题，作者旨在推广已有结果，并回应Neofytidis、Wang和Wang提出的相关问题，探索何种整数集合可以作为两个流形之间的映射度集合。

Method: 通过证明映射度集在两种代数运算下的稳定性——即乘以包含0的有限整数子集，以及由加法子幺半群经有限次和与积运算所得的集合——来构造新的大类可行映射度集。

Result: 证明了映射度集的上述稳定性性质，并由此得出一个广泛的无限映射度集族，特别包括从0开始的有限个算术级数之并均可以作为映射度集出现。

Conclusion: 该结果将映射度集实现问题的可行性族大大扩展，部分解决了Neofytidis-Wang-Wang提出的问题，并为后续研究提供了新的框架。

Abstract: We prove that mapping degree sets are stable under multiplication by finite subsets of $\mathbb Z$ containing $0$ and by sets obtained from additive submonoids of $\mathbb Z$ through finitely many sums and products. In particular, every set of the latter type occurs as a mapping degree set. As a consequence, we obtain a broad family of infinite mapping degree sets, including finite unions of arithmetic progressions starting at $0$. These results extend previous work on the realization problem and are related to a question posed by Neofytidis, Wang, and Wang.

</details>


### [130] [Centralizers and classifying spaces for commutativity of $SL_2(\mathbb{Z}[1/p])$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28001)
*Omar Antolín-Camarena,Ramón Flores,Luis Jorge Sánchez Saldaña*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了群 SL₂(ℤ[1/p]) 的交换性分类空间（由 Adem、F. Cohen 和 Torres-Giese 引入）的同伦类型，分别考虑了离散拓扑群和从 SL₂(ℝ) 继承的子空间拓扑两种情形。这需要对 SL₂(ℤ[1/p]) 的极大阿贝尔子群进行详尽分类，结果表明这些子群恰好是非中心元素的正规化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交换性分类空间是研究群同伦性质与交换结构的重要工具。针对算术群 SL₂(ℤ[1/p]) 这一类具有丰富几何与数论结构的群，确定其交换性分类空间的同伦类型需要深入理解其阿贝尔子群结构，这本身是一个重要的数学问题。

Method: 作者首先利用几何群论的方法，对 SL₂(ℤ[1/p]) 中的极大阿贝尔子群进行了详尽分类，证明了这些子群恰好等于所有非中心元素的中心化子。基于这一分类结果，进一步计算了 Adem-Cohen-Torres-Giese 所定义的交换性分类空间的同伦类型，并分别处理了离散拓扑与 SL₂(ℝ) 子空间拓扑两种情形。

Result: 得到了 SL₂(ℤ[1/p]) 的交换性分类空间在两种拓扑下的同伦类型的完整刻画，其基础是对极大阿贝尔子群结构的完备分类。

Conclusion: 该工作将几何群论方法成功应用于计算算术群的交换性分类空间，揭示了极大阿贝尔子群与中心化子之间的精确对应关系，并为这类群同伦性质的研究提供了新的范例。

Abstract: We compute the homotopy type of the classifying space for commutativity introduced by Adem, F. Cohen and Torres-Giese for the group $\SL_2(\Z[1/p])$, both as a discrete topological group and with the subspace topology from $\SL_2(\R)$. Doing so requires compiling detailed information on the maximal abelian subgroups of $\SL_2(\Z[1/p])$, which turn out to be precisely the centralizers of the non-central elements of the group, for which we employ methods from geometric group theory.

</details>


### [131] [Interval Decompositions for Multipersistence Modules over Finite Posets and Robustness of Sheaf Data on Simplicial Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28134)
*Pablo Hernández-García,Daniel Hernández Serrano,Darío Sánchez Gómez*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限偏序集（非全序）上的多参数持久模块的结构定理。具体针对有限集非空子集偏序的对偶上的逐点有限维模块，给出了通过转移态射表达的充分条件，使得这些模块能分解为区间模块的直和。在一般情况下，区间和项及其重数可由有限个指标处的维数显式确定。此外，文章将厚度（thickness）和凝聚性（cohesion）从单纯上同调推广到细胞层（cellular sheaves），并利用抽象结构定理获得几何凝聚模块的区间分解，最后引入双参数持久构造来追踪层韧性（sheaf resilience）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数持久同调相比单参数情形要复杂得多，Zig-zag代数的结构困难使得区间模块分解通常不存在或不唯一。作者希望为非全序有限偏序上的多参数持久模块建立实用的结构定理，并探索这些看似代数上受限的假设背后的几何动机，从而将拓扑数据分析工具（如厚度、凝聚性）从单纯上同调扩展到更一般的细胞层框架。

Method: (1) 对有限集非空子集偏序的对偶上的逐点有限维模块，通过分析转移态射（transition morphisms）给出模块可分裂为区间模块直和的充分条件；(2) 在一般非分裂情形下，利用有限个指标处的维数信息显式确定区间和项及其重数；(3) 将厚度与凝聚性概念从单纯上同调推广至细胞层，证明抽象结构定理可应用于由此产生的几何凝聚模块；(4) 引入双参数持久构造，追踪结构退化过程中全局截面和上同调障碍在厚/凝聚子结构上的可检测性。

Result: 获得了有限偏序集上的多参数持久模块的区间分解充分条件（通过转移态射表达），并在一般情形下给出了区间和项与重数的显式确定方式。成功将厚度和凝聚性从单纯上同调推广到细胞层，利用结构定理得到几何凝聚模块的区间分解。提出了追踪层韧性的双参数持久构造。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多参数持久模块理论中代数结构定理与几何稳健性理论之间的桥梁。即使这些假设在代数上看起来具有限制性，作者证明了它们自然源自细胞层数据在单纯复形上的稳健性理论，为理解代数信息、相容性约束和上同调障碍在结构失效下的持续性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We prove structure theorems for multipersistence modules indexed by finite posets that are not totally ordered. Specifically, we consider pointwise finite-dimensional modules over the opposite of the poset of non-empty subsets of a finite set, and give sufficient conditions, expressed through transition morphisms, for such modules to split as direct sums of interval modules. In the general case, the interval summands and multiplicities are explicitly determined by dimensions at finitely many indices. Although the assumptions may look algebraically restrictive, we show that they have a natural geometric origin in a robustness theory of cellular sheaf data over simplicial complexes, where one studies how algebraic information, compatibility constraints, and cohomological obstructions persist under structural failures. We extend thickness and cohesion from simplicial cohomology to cellular sheaves: thickness detects the dependence of cohomology classes on high-dimensional support, while cohesion captures the influence of higher-order adjacencies on the cohomological features. We leverage our abstract structure theorems to obtain interval decompositions for the resulting geometric cohesion modules. Finally, we introduce biparameter persistence constructions for sheaf resilience, tracking whether global sections and cohomological obstructions remain detectable on thick or cohesive substructures during topological degradation.

</details>


### [132] [On small covers over Bier spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28141)
*Suyoung Choi,Younghan Yoon,Seonghyeon Yu*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了单纯复形的Bier球面，特别关注单形骨架的Bier球面。由于这些Bier球面是多胞形的，它们可以产生小覆盖（small covers）。作者对这些小覆盖进行了Davis–Januszkiewicz等价分类，并确定了若干具体情形的同胚类型及有理Betti数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bier球面是拓扑组合学中一类重要的对象，而小覆盖是实射影空间上的标准典范流形在一般单纯复形上的推广。对Bier球面的小覆盖进行分类有助于理解其拓扑结构。

Method: 利用Bier球面已知的多胞形性质构造小覆盖，并借助Davis–Januszkiewicz等价关系进行分类；通过组合和拓扑方法对特定骨架情形（r=0和r=m-3）确定同胚类型，对其余情形计算有理Betti数。

Result: 对所有m≥4，确定了0-骨架和(m-3)-骨架对应的Bier球面小覆盖的同胚类型；对0<r<m-3的中间情形，计算了其有理Betti数。

Conclusion: 本文给出了单形骨架Bier球面小覆盖的完整Davis–Januszkiewicz等价分类，并推广了对这些流形拓扑结构的理解。

Abstract: The Bier sphere of a simplicial complex $K$ is defined as the deleted join of $K$ and its combinatorial Alexander dual.
  We focus on the class of Bier spheres of the skeleta of a simplex.
  Since these Bier spheres are known to be polytopal, they give rise to small covers.
  We classify small covers over these Bier spheres up to Davis--Januszkiewicz equivalence.
  As applications, for all $m \geq 4$, we determine the homeomorphism types of small covers over the Bier spheres of the $0$-skeleton and the $(m-3)$-skeleton of an $(m-1)$-simplex.
  For the remaining cases $0<r<m-3$, we compute their rational Betti numbers.

</details>
