### [133] [Semi-analytic Inference of Satellite Densities in the Cold Dark Matter Model Part II. Implications for Dark Matter Indirect Detection Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27326)
*Kailash Raman,Dylan Folsom,Manoj Kaplinghat,Mariangela Lisanti,Benjamin R. Safdi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用半解析模型（SatGen）和最新的恒星运动学数据（Part I），计算了银河系39个矮椭球星系（dSph）的暗物质天体物理J因子概率分布，并基于此重新解读了Fermi-LAT对暗物质湮灭截面的现有限制。核心发现是：Jeans分析中J因子计算方式的不同会给截面限制引入2-4倍的不确定性；使用宇宙学信息先验时，热遗迹暗物质湮灭到b\bar{b}约为70 GeV以下被排除，而常用先验则排除130 GeV以下；最高J因子天体在天空中尺度约1度，挑战了点源近似的有效性；且最高J因子暗晕已被发现，未来超暗矮星系不太可能大幅增强暗物质湮灭限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮星系是搜索暗物质湮灭/衰变信号的理想目标，但J因子（即暗物质密度平方的线积分）的不确定性直接影响对Fermi-LAT等数据所得暗物质湮灭截面限制的可靠性。此前J因子的计算存在先验选择不统一、运动学数据更新滞后等问题，有必要用更新的数据和更合理的半解析模型重新校准J因子，以给出更可靠且物理上更自洽的暗物质湮灭限制。

Method: 基于SatGen半解析卫星模拟生成亚晕（subhalo）种群，将其分别条件化于矮星系由动力学方法确定的动力学质量或恒星质量，推断39个dSph的J因子概率分布。同时使用Jeans方程结合更新后的恒星运动学数据计算J因子，并引入不同程度的SatGen信息作为先验。最后用所得J因子重新解读Fermi-LAT对湮灭截面的限制，并检验点源近似及未来发现前景。

Result: (1) Jeans分析中不同先验导致的J因子变化给截面推断带来2-4倍不确定性；(2) 使用宇宙学信息先验时，可在约70 GeV以下排除热遗迹湮灭到b\bar{b}的截面，而常用先验在95%置信水平排除130 GeV以下；(3) 最高J因子dSph在天空上延展约1度，点源近似可能失效；(4) 最高J因子暗晕已被现有观测发现，未来超暗矮星系发现不太可能显著收紧暗物质湮灭限制。

Conclusion: J因子的计算对暗物质湮灭限制有显著影响（2-4倍不确定性），采用宇宙学启发的先验是更合理的选择；点源近似对最高J因子矮星系可能不成立，需要考虑空间延展效应；此外，现有矮星系样本中最高J因子的暗晕已被发现，说明未来凭借发现更多超弱矮星系来提升暗物质搜索灵敏度提升有限。

Abstract: Dwarf galaxies provide excellent targets to search for signals of dark matter annihilation or decay. Using a calibrated semi-analytic model and the latest stellar kinematic data (presented in Part I), this paper updates the astrophysical J-factors for the Milky Way's dwarf spheroidal galaxies. We infer the probability distributions for the J-factors of 39 dwarfs by conditioning a population of subhalos, generated with the SatGen semi-analytic satellite model, on either a dwarf's kinematically determined dynamical mass or its stellar mass. We also compute the J-factors using the Jeans equation with updated stellar kinematics and priors that incorporate varying degrees of SatGen information. We use the computed J-factors to recast existing limits from Fermi-LAT data on the annihilation cross section. Our main result is that variations in the J-factors computed using the Jeans analysis introduce a factor of 2-4 uncertainty into the inferred limits on the cross section. We argue that a cosmologically informed prior is a motivated choice that excludes thermal relic annihilation cross sections to $b\bar{b}$ below about 70 GeV. For comparison, the more commonly used priors, which can lead to unphysical halo parameters, exclude masses below 130 GeV at 95% confidence level. We also show that the highest-J-factor dwarfs are spatially extended, approximately one degree on the sky, which challenges the validity of the point-source approximation adopted in many analyses of Fermi data. Finally, based on the semi-analytic model and the kinematic data, the highest J-factor halos have already been discovered, suggesting future ultra-faint discoveries are unlikely to substantially strengthen limits on annihilating dark matter.

</details>


### [134] [Relativistic effects of PSR~J1856--0039 double neutron star system in a 2.36-hour compact orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27333)
*Z. L. Yang,J. L. Han,W. Q. Su,P. F. Wang,C. Wang,T. Wang,D. J. Zhou,Yi Yan,J. Xu,W. C. Jing,N. N. Cai,R. X. Xu,H. G. Wang,X. P. You*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: FAST望远镜发现了一个新的致密双中子星系统PSR J1856--0039，其周期为23.4ms，轨道周期2.36小时，偏心率0.106。该系统具有已知双中子星中最低的总质量（2.48841 M☉），测量了多种相对论效应（轨道周期衰减、近星点进动、爱因斯坦延迟），并验证了广义相对论对引力波辐射导致的轨道衰减预测（比值1.009±0.014）。该系统将在82百万年后合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密双中子星系统是检验引力理论和研究中子星并合事件的独特实验室。发现并精确测量新的DNS系统对于约束中子星质量分布、状态方程以及检验广义相对论具有重要意义。

Method: 利用FAST望远镜发现脉冲星PSR J1856--0039并进行后续跟踪观测，通过脉冲星计时技术精确测量了轨道周期导数、近星点进动速率和爱因斯坦延迟等相对论效应，从而推导出系统的几何参数（轨道倾角）和物理参数（脉冲星质量、伴星质量、总质量）。

Result: 测量得到轨道周期导数Ṗ_orb=-1.284×10^-12 s s^-1，近星点进动17.5859 deg/yr，爱因斯坦延迟0.445ms。系统总质量2.48841 M☉为已知DNS最低，脉冲星质量1.304 M☉和伴星质量1.185 M☉均为已知最低中子星质量之一。观测与广义相对论预言引力波辐射导致的轨道衰减比值为1.009±0.014，在68%置信度下一致。

Conclusion: PSR J1856--0039是一个具有极端低质量的致密双中子星系统，其观测结果与广义相对论预言高度一致。系统将在82 Myr后合并，可能形成稳定中子星或坍缩为黑洞，长期监测有望探测到Lense-Thirring进动效应。

Abstract: Compact double neutron star (DNS) systems are unique laboratories for testing gravitational theories and studying DNS mergers. Here we report the properties of a new DNS system, PSR J1856--0039, discovered in the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST). The pulsar is mildly recycled with a period of 23.4~ms in a compact eccentric orbit ($e=0.106$) with an orbital period of 2.36 hours. By following up FAST observations, we measured the relativistic effects, including the orbital period derivative $\dot{P}_{\rm orb}=-1.284\pm0.019\times10^{-12}$ s s$^{-1}$, periastron advance $\dotω=17.5859\pm0.0007$ deg yr$^{-1}$, and Einstein delay $γ=0.445\pm0.011$ ms. This DNS system has a low orbital inclination of $i=133^\circ.2\pm1^\circ.1$ and the lowest total mass of any known DNS, $M_{\rm tot}=2.48841\pm0.00015 M\odot$, with a determined pulsar mass of $1.304\pm0.022 M_\odot$ and a companion mass of $1.185\pm0.022 M_\odot$, one of the lowest neutron-star masses. The observed orbital decay due to gravitational-wave emission $\dot{P}^{\rm GW}_{\rm orb,obs}$ and the orbital decay predicted by general relativity $\dot{P}^{\rm GW}_{\rm orb,pred}$ are consistent at a level of $\dot{P}^{\rm GW}_{\rm orb,obs}/\dot{P}^{\rm GW}_{\rm orb,pred}=$1.009(14) (68% confidence). This DNS will merge after 82 Myr and may form a stable neutron star or collapse into a black hole after spin-down. Long-term monitoring could potentially probe the Lense-Thirring precession.

</details>


### [135] [Exploring cosmic magnetism with gamma-ray burst afterglow emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27336)
*Paolo Da Vela,Davide Miceli,Lara Nava,Giancarlo Ghirlanda*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用CTA观测伽马暴（GRB）的pair-echo信号来探测宇宙空隙中星系际磁场（IGMF）的可行性。通过模拟不同参数下的GRB余辉发射和预期pair-echo辐射，发现部分GRB子样本在所有测试的IGMF强度下均可产生可探测的信号，且余辉光变曲线的早斜坡（如喷流破裂）是提高探测概率的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学尺度磁场的本质和起源尚不清楚，而宇宙空隙中IGMF的存在可以通过高能光子（>100 GeV）产生的pair-echo时延发射来探测。近期GRB余辉在TeV能段的成功探测为利用GRB作为探针提供了新的机会，因此需要系统性评估CTA对GRB pair-echo信号的探测能力。

Method: 对GRB余辉发射进行模拟，变动三个关键参数：喷流动能（E_k,iso = 10^49 - 10^55 erg）、红移（z = 0.03 - 1）和光变曲线转折时间；对IGMF强度在B = 10^-19 至 10^-16 G范围内估计预期pair-echo辐射；评估CTA在数十GeV能段的探测能力。

Result: 在z - E_k,iso参数空间中的部分GRB子样本，对于所有测试的IGMF强度（10^-19 至 10^-16 G），均可产生CTA可探测的pair-echo成分。GRB余辉光变曲线在0.1-1天内发生早期喷流破裂（导致光变曲线变陡）是提高探测机会的关键因素。CTA在GRB触发后10-12小时至数天开始观测可获得相关结果。

Conclusion: CTA对GRB的pair-echo观测为探测宇宙空隙中的IGMF提供了可行的途径，早期喷流破裂的GRB是最有希望的候选源，观测窗口为触发后约10小时至数天。

Abstract: The nature and origin of magnetic fields on cosmological scales are still unclear. Magnetic fields detected in galaxies and galaxy clusters are typically interpreted as the result of the amplification of weak seed fields, but their nature remains largely unknown with two scenarios considered: the cosmological and the astrophysical origin. Signatures of magnetization in cosmic voids from observations of very high energy (VHE, E > 100 GeV) photons from extragalactic sources can provide crucial results. Indeed, if a non-negligible intergalactic magnetic field (IGMF) is present in the voids, a time-delayed emission known as pair-echo is expected. The timing and intensity of this signal encode information on the IGMF strength B and properties. Given the recent detection of GRB afterglows at TeV energies, in this article we perform a detectability study of pair-echo signatures from GRBs. We simulate afterglow emission for different values of the jet kinetic energy (E$_{k,iso}$ = 10$^{49}$ - 10$^{55}$ erg), redshift (z = 0.03 - 1), and lightcurve break times, and estimate the expected pair-echo radiation for IGMF strengths in the range B = 10$^{-19}$ - 10$^{-16}$ G. We investigate the capability of CTAO to detect the resulting emission at tens of GeV. We find that a subsample of GRBs in the z - E$_{k,iso}$ parameter space can produce a detectable pair-echo component for CTAO for all the tested IGMF strengths. A steepening of the GRB afterglow light curve, caused e.g. by an early (0.1 - 1 days) jet break, is a key factor to increase the chances of detection. CTAO observations starting from 10 - 12 hrs up to a few days after the GRB trigger time can provide relevant results to probe IGMF.

</details>


### [136] [Binary neutron star populations across cosmic time: the impact of binary stellar evolution uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27391)
*Mathieu Venet,Irina Dvorkin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用种群合成代码COSMIC系统探索了72个双星演化模型，研究二元恒星演化不确定性对双中子星（BNS）形成效率和宇宙并合率的影响，发现公共包层喷射效率和原生踢腿方案是主导不确定性来源，且不同参数组合可产生相似的并合率历史，存在显著简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定在广泛的金属丰度范围内，二元恒星演化能否产生可识别的稳健特征，以理解BNS系统的形成效率和宇宙学并合率。

Method: 使用种群合成代码COSMIC系统探索72个双星演化模型，变化四个关键参数：公共包层演化的触发时机和效率、原生踢腿方案、以及公共包层阶段的踢腿处理方式。对每个模型采用多种宇宙金属丰度-红移关系计算BNS形成效率和宇宙学并合率。

Result: 识别出BNS形成效率随金属丰度变化的特征性规律，包括低金属丰度下第一巨星分支跳跃的出现以及$Z=10^{-3}$到$7.5\times10^{-3}$之间的非单调演化。所有模型均预测BNS前身星质量呈双峰分布。公共包层喷射效率和原生踢腿方案导致形成效率和宇宙学并合率产生数个数量级的差异。不同参数组合可产生相似的并合率历史，存在显著简并性。当前引力波观测只能排除效率最低的形成场景。

Conclusion: 打破种群合成模型间的简并性需要依赖未来第三代引力波天文台预期获得的更大BNS并合样本，以及改进的宇宙时间金属丰度演化的观测约束。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of uncertainties in binary stellar evolution on the formation efficiency and cosmological merger rate of BNS systems. In particular, we aim to determine whether robust signatures of binary evolution can be identified across a broad range of metallicities. We perform a systematic exploration of 72 binary evolution models using the population synthesis code COSMIC. We vary four key parameters controlling the onset and efficiency of common-envelope evolution, the natal kick prescription, and the treatment of kicks during the common envelope phase. For each model, we compute the BNS formation efficiency and cosmological merger rate using several prescriptions for the cosmic metallicity-redshift relation. We identify characteristic features in the metallicity dependence of the BNS formation efficiency that arise from specific evolutionary processes. These include the onset of first-giant-branch skipping at low metallicity and a non-monotonic evolution between $Z=10^{-3}$ and $7.5\times 10^{-3}$. All models also predict a bimodal distribution of BNS progenitor masses associated with two distinct formation channels. The common envelope ejection efficiency and natal kick prescription remain the dominant sources of uncertainty, producing variations of several orders of magnitude in both the BNS formation efficiency and the cosmological merger rate. Finally, we show that different combinations of binary-evolution parameters can produce similar merger-rate histories, highlighting significant degeneracies among population-synthesis models. Current gravitational-wave observations can rule out only the least efficient formation scenarios. Breaking these degeneracies will require larger samples of BNS mergers expected from future third-generation gravitational-wave observatories, and improved observational constraints on the evolution of metallicity across cosmic time.

</details>


### [137] [Radio Follow-Up of Einstein Probe Fast X-Ray Transients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27439)
*Carmen Choza,Joe S. Bright,Francesco Carotenuto,Alex Pollak,Rob Fender,Andrew Siemion*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了对爱因斯坦探测器（EP）于2024年发现的20个快速X射线暂现源（FXT）的协同射电巡天观测。利用艾伦望远镜阵列（ATA）进行了59个观测时段，覆盖爆后1-65天，其中两个源（EP240315a和EP241021a）探测到射电对应体。EP241021a在15.5 GHz的光变曲线约30天达到峰值，等分分析表明其为温和相对论性喷流（Γ≈1.3，能量≈3×10^49 erg）；EP240315a的多频建模显示早期高度相对论性喷流（Γ≳3）并随时间减速。研究指出FXT是包含相对论性及非相对论性爆炸的异质性群体，其光度跨越最亮GRB和相对论性潮汐瓦解事件余辉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速X射线暂现源（FXT）是短暂的软X射线爆发事件，其物理起源尚不明确。爱因斯坦探测器（EP）的新能力使得这些事件的及时发现成为可能，为快速多波段后续观测提供了机会。本文旨在通过协调的射电巡天观测，对FXT的性质进行系统性的射电波段表征，以阐明其物理机制。

Method: 本文开展了一项针对EP在2024年探测到的20个FXT的协同射电观测计划。核心观测包含59个ATA观测时段，覆盖1-8 GHz频段，采样爆后约1-65天的时间尺度，典型3σ灵敏度为0.6-1.5 mJy。对探测到射电对应体的源（EP240315a和EP241021a）进行了多频段详细建模：利用AMI-LA在15.5 GHz监测EP241021a的光变曲线，并采用等分分析（equipartition analysis）推断物理参数；同时结合VLA新观测与已发表的MeerKAT、ATCA和eMERLIN数据对EP240315a进行多频建模。

Result: 在两个FXT中探测到射电对应体：EP241021a的光变曲线在约30天达到峰值（Fν≈1.0 mJy），等分分析得出牛顿等分能量约3×10^49 erg，温和相对论性on-axis解（Γ≈1.3）；EP240315a的多频建模表明其具有高度相对论性早期发射（Γ≳3）并随时间减速，与喷流外流一致。探测与非探测源的射电光度跨度与最亮GRB和相对论性TDE余辉重叠。在类似GRB的红移先验下，ATA的3σ极限对应观测者系比光度阈值约10^32-10^33 erg s^-1 Hz^-1。

Conclusion: FXT构成一个异质性天体物理群体，包含相对论性爆炸（如类GRB喷流）和非相对论性爆炸两类来源。计划的ATA升级以及下一代阵列（SKA、DSA、ngVLA）将实现灵敏度的群体级射电研究，有望进一步提升对FXT射电天空的理解。

Abstract: Fast X-ray transients (FXTs) are brief, luminous bursts of soft X-ray emission whose physical origins remain uncertain. The Einstein Probe (EP) mission has recently enabled prompt discovery of these events, providing opportunities for rapid multi-wavelength follow-up. We present a coordinated radio observing campaign targeting 20 FXTs detected by the EP in 2024. The core consists of 59 epochs with the Allen Telescope Array (ATA), sampling post-burst timescales from $\sim$1 to $\sim$65 days and reaching typical $3σ$ sensitivities of 0.6--1.5\,mJy across 1--8\,GHz. Two FXTs---EP240315a and EP241021a---have radio counterparts. For EP241021a, AMI-LA monitoring at 15.5\,GHz reveals a light curve peaking at $\sim$30 days with $F_ν\approx1.0$\,mJy; equipartition analysis implies a Newtonian equipartition energy of $\sim3\times10^{49}$\,erg and a mildly relativistic on-axis solution with $Γ_{\rm on}\approx1.3$. Multi-frequency modelling of EP240315a, combining new VLA measurements with published MeerKAT, ATCA, and eMERLIN data, indicates highly relativistic early emission ($Γ\gtrsim3$) that decelerates with time, consistent with jetted outflow. The detections and ATA non-detections span luminosities overlapping the brightest GRB and relativistic tidal disruption event (TDE) afterglows, suggesting FXTs comprise a heterogeneous population including both relativistic and non-relativistic explosions. Under a GRB-like redshift prior, our $3σ$ ATA limits correspond to observer-frame specific-luminosity thresholds of $\sim10^{32}$--$10^{33}\,\mathrm{erg\ s^{-1}\ Hz^{-1}}$; for nearby events ($z\lesssim0.2$), $L_ν\lesssim10^{30\text{--}31}\,\mathrm{erg\ s^{-1}\ Hz^{-1}}$. Planned ATA upgrades and next-generation arrays (SKA, DSA, ngVLA) will enable sensitive, population-level radio studies of the FXT radio sky.

</details>


### [138] [On the triple nature of the PSR J0435+3233 system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27462)
*Paulo C. C. Freire,Colin J. Clark,Cees G. Bassa,Guillaume Voisin,Rutger van Haasteren,Lars Nieder,Benjamin W. Stappers*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出PSR J0435+3233是一个层级三星系统，其异常高的自转减慢率由外三层的大质量伴星引起的加速度变化所导致，而非真实的固有自转减慢。通过脉冲星计时方法结合射电和伽马射线观测，发现该系统确实符合层级三星模型，并识别出光学对应体为一颗1.2太阳质量的F型主序星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSR J0435+3233的自转减慢率远高于其他毫秒脉冲星（MSP），这挑战了我们对MSP形成与演化的理解，需要一种新的机制来合理解释这一异常现象。

Method: 采用脉冲星计时方法，结合射电观测和费米大视场望远镜（LAT）自2008年以来的伽马射线观测数据，构建层级三星计时模型来拟合观测数据，并通过动力学分析确定系统各组成部分的性质。

Result: 层级三星计时模型能够很好地描述观测数据，并实现了自2008年LAT数据开始以来的伽马射线脉冲检测。固有自转减慢率仍不确定但排除了较大值，比观测值低至少两个数量级，与其他银道面MSP一致。识别出距离脉冲星11毫角秒处的一颗恒星为第三层伴星的光学对应体，推断其为1.2太阳质量、偏心约0.6、周期约70年、倾角较低的F型主序星。

Conclusion: PSR J0435+3233确认为层级三星系统。持续研究该系统有望在强等效原理违反约束方面提供比PSR J0337+1715更严格的限制，外星的后续光谱测量和天体测量将给出质量比和轨道倾角的估计。

Abstract: Context. The recent pulsar timing ephemeris of PSR J0435+3233 indicates that this millisecond pulsar (MSP) has a spin-down rate that is much higher than observed in other MSPs and challenges our understanding of the formation and evolution of MSPs. Aims. We propose that this system is a hierarchical triple, and that the high spin-down rate is caused by varying acceleration due to a tertiary in a wide orbit. Methods. We use pulsar timing methods with radio and gamma-ray observations of PSR J0435+3233 to determine the system properties. Results. We find that a hierarchical triple timing model describes the timing observations of PSR J0435+3233 and that this results in the detection of gamma-ray pulsations back to the beginning of the Fermi Large Area Telescope (LAT) data in 2008. The intrinsic spin-down rate remains uncertain as it correlates with the parameters of the outer orbit, but large spin-down rates are excluded and the intrinsic rate is at least two orders of magnitude lower than the observed rate, in line with other Galactic MSPs. We identify a star located 11 mas from the pulsar position as the optical counterpart to the tertiary companion. From the 1.5-2.5 kpc distance and colours, we infer that the tertiary is a 1.2 solar mass F-type main-sequence star. Along with the pulsar binary, it orbits the common centre of mass with an eccentric (e ~ 0.6), wide (~ 70 yr) orbit that is likely seen at a low orbital inclination. Conclusions. We conclude that PSR J0435+3233 is a hierarchical triple system. We discuss the motivation and prospects for the continued study of this system. Spectral measurements of the outer star in addition to continued astrometric measurements will yield mass ratio and inclination estimates, while continued pulsar timing may yield a tighter constraint on violations of the Strong Equivalence Principle than are currently obtained from PSR J0337+1715.

</details>


### [139] [JWST Spectra Indicate a Large Mass of Postshock Dust Formed by SN 2010jl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27468)
*Nathan Smith,Melissa Shahbandeh,Ori D. Fox,Thomas Moore,Jennifer E. Andrews,Thomas G. Brink,Eli Dwek,Michael Engesser,Alexei V. Filippenko,Bryony Nickson,Tea Temim,Yi Yang,WeiKang Zheng,Chris Ashall,Raphael Baer-Way,Geoffrey C. Clayton,Jacob E. Jencson,Joel Johansson,Zachary G. Lane,Dan Milisavljevic,Armin Rest,Arkaprabha Sarangi,Tamas Szalai,Schuyler D. Van Dyk,Brian J. Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用JWST中红外光谱和地面光学光谱，分析了超新星SN 2010jl爆炸13年后的剩余源。研究发现其存在大量暖尘埃（质量至少0.11 M☉，更可能≥0.2 M☉），这些尘埃形成于激波后区域，是迄今在不借助远红外/亚毫米数据情况下测量到的最大的超新星尘埃质量之一，表明强星周介质相互作用促进了高效尘埃形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SN 2010jl是一颗邻近的强相互作用超亮IIn型超新星，早期数据显示存在尘埃证据，但对尘埃的来源（预先存在的星周介质尘埃还是新形成的尘埃）及尘埃质量仍不明确。研究者希望通过长时间基线（爆炸后13年）的中红外和光学观测，明确尘埃的形成位置、质量及其对早期宇宙尘埃贡献的意义。

Method: 利用JWST中红外光谱获取爆炸后13年时剩余源的中红外超量与10-15 μm宽谱特征；结合地面光学光谱确认SN位置存在年轻大质量星团，并验证蓝移线轮廓持续存在。通过光谱分析推断尘埃温度、光学厚度和最小尘埃质量，并利用硅酸盐吸收特征判断尘埃的类型与分布位置。

Result: JWST中红外光谱显示来自暖尘埃的强中红外超量，尘埃质量下限为0.11 M☉，更可能的值为0.2 M☉以上（因尘埃光学厚）。该质量远超爆炸后2-3年时推断的尘埃质量，属于迄今未借助远红外/亚毫米数据测得的最大超新星尘埃质量之一。光学光谱确认了年轻大质量星团的存在，证实蓝移线轮廓源自激波后区域形成的尘埃，且最暖的中红外发射尘埃与造成蓝移的激波后尘埃为同一成分。光谱中的硅酸盐吸收可能来自视线方向上的冷超新星喷出物尘埃。

Conclusion: SN 2010jl中大量的激波后尘埃表明，强星周介质相互作用促进了高效的尘埃形成，且新形成的激波后尘埃能够存活。如果强相互作用超新星在早期宇宙中普遍存在，它们可能对婴儿星系中观测到的尘埃有重要贡献。

Abstract: We present new JWST mid-IR spectra and ground-based optical spectra of the lingering source at the position of SN 2010jl, which was a relatively nearby superluminous Type IIn supernova having strong interaction with circumstellar material (CSM). Early-time data showed evidence of dust, interpreted as either pre-existing CSM dust, or as newly formed dust in the SN ejecta and post-shock region. At 13 yr post explosion, JWST reveals a strong MIR excess from warm dust, with broad features at 10-15 $μ$m. Our analysis reveals a minimum dust mass of $>$0.11 $M_{\odot}$, and a more likely value of 0.2 $M_{\odot}$ or more because the dust is optically thick. This is among the largest masses of SN-produced dust yet measured without far-IR/submm data, and greatly exceeds SN 2010jl's dust mass inferred around 2-3 yr post-explosion. Ground-based optical spectra confirm the presence of a young massive cluster at the SN position, and confirm that blueshifted line profiles persisting until the latest epochs arise from dust formed in the post-shock region. The warmest dust emitting in the MIR is likely to be the same post-shock dust causing the blueshift. The JWST spectrum also reveals silicate absorption, which may arise from cool SN ejecta dust along the line of sight to the receding shock. The large mass of post-shock dust in SN 2010jl suggests that strong CSM interaction promotes efficient dust production, where the new post-shock dust will survive. If strongly interacting SNe are common in the early Universe, this may contribute significantly to dust seen in infant galaxies.

</details>


### [140] [Depolarization Induced by Rapid Polarization Angle Swings: A Common Feature of Pulsars and Fast Radio Bursts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27622)
*Yu-Chen Huang,Jie-Shuang Wang,Song-Bo Zhang,Zi-Gao Dai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出脉冲星和快速射电暴（FRBs）的线偏振度Π_L与偏振角时间导数dPA/dt之间可能存在反相关关系，通过几何框架分析其源于发射区域内不同偏振方向辐射的非相干叠加导致的退偏效应，并用射电脉冲星样本进行了部分验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏振角（PA）是诊断脉冲星和FRB发射区域磁场的重要工具，理解偏振特性对揭示FRB的辐射机制和旋转磁层起源至关重要。作者希望通过提出线偏振度与PA变化率之间的反相关作为共同特征，为区分FRB的不同起源模型提供新的观测判据。

Method: 在旋转中子星偏振发射的一般几何框架内建立分析模型，提出Π_L与dPA/dt反相关的理论预测；然后利用射电脉冲星样本对预判进行检验，寻找该反相关的观测证据，并讨论对FRB的适用性。

Result: 在脉冲星子样本中找到了与预期反相关一致的可能的观测证据；但在FRB中由于现有观测数据有限，该关系是否成立仍不确定。

Conclusion: 如果该反相关特征得以确认，不仅能支持FRB的旋转磁层起源，还能对驱动FRB的中子星的旋转周期和几何参数提供约束。

Abstract: The polarization angle (PA) of pulsars and fast radio bursts (FRBs) provides a useful diagnostic of the magnetic fields in their emission regions and is therefore crucial for understanding their radiation and origins. Within a general geometric framework for polarized emission from a rotating neutron star, we suggest a possible anti-correlation between the degree of linear polarization $Π_\text{L}$ and $d\text{PA}/dt$, the time derivative of the PA, as a common feature of pulsars and FRBs. The depolarization arises from the incoherent superposition of radiation with different polarization directions within the observable part of the emission region, and is detectable only when the PA swing is steep enough. We test this conjecture using a sample of radio pulsars and find possible evidence for the expected anti-correlation in a subset of pulsars. Whether this relation holds in FRBs remains uncertain due to limited observational data. Identification of this feature would not only provide insights into the rotating magnetospheric origin of FRBs, but also place constraints on the spin periods and geometric parameters of the neutron stars that power these mysterious bursts.

</details>


### [141] [An extra hard spectral component peaking at sub-GeV in the prompt emission of GRB 260226A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27650)
*Yi-Yun Huang,Hai-Ming Zhang,Ruo-Yu Liu,Xiang-Yu Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过GBM-LAT联合观测分析GRB 260226A，发现除传统Band函数的keV-MeV能谱之外，还存在一个额外的亚GeV硬成分。时间分辨分析表明该成分早期更适合截断幂律模型（可归因于γγ吸收），后期则倾向于（或至少同样适合）破折幂律模型。该成分的峰值能量与Band成分峰值能量之比较低，难以用单区域SSC模型解释，双区域发射模型可能提供合理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解伽马射线暴（GRB）在keV-MeV能段以上的高能辐射机制，特别是Fermi/LAT探测到的额外硬成分的本质，以揭示GRB的高能辐射物理过程。

Method: 对GRB 260226A（罕见的LAT种子触发GRB）进行联合GBM-LAT光谱分析，采用时间分辨光谱拟合，比较截断幂律、破折幂律等不同模型对额外亚GeV成分的描述能力，并结合γγ吸收解释早期截断，评估单区域SSC与双区域发射模型的适用性。

Result: 发现GRB 260226A存在额外的亚GeV成分。早期该成分由截断幂律模型拟合最佳，后期破折幂律模型更优（或至少同样好）。早期截断若归因于γγ吸收，表明洛伦兹因子随时间增加。额外成分与Band成分的峰值能量比过低，排除了单区域SSC场景。

Conclusion: GRB 260226A的额外亚GeV成分不能用单区域SSC模型解释，双区域发射模型（如内激波加速的相对论电子对光球层辐射的外部逆康普顿散射）可能是更合理的解释方案。

Abstract: The prompt emission spectra of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) have long been empirically described by the Band function over the keV--MeV range, whereas several \textit{Fermi}/LAT-detected GRBs show evidence for an extra hard component at higher energies. Here we present a joint GBM--LAT study of GRB~260226A, a rare LAT seeded onboard trigger GRB, and find that an extra sub-GeV component is present. In the time-resolved analysis, we find that a cutoff power-law model fits the spectral data of the extra component better than other models at earlier times, while at later times, the broken power-law model is preferred (or at least equally good). Interpreting the early cutoff as the $γγ$ absorption implies that the Lorentz factor at early time is lower and increases with time. The low ratio between the peak energy of the extra component and that of the Band component is difficult to explain with a one-zone synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) scenario. Two-zone emission models, such as external inverse Compton scattering of the photosphere emission by relativistic electrons accelerated in internal shocks, could provide a possible explanation.

</details>


### [142] [On the Blueprint of Active Galaxies Producing Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27658)
*Chen Li,Francis Halzen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于对活跃星系NGC 1068和TXS 0506+056的观测以及来自IceCube数据的额外证据，提出高能中微子产生于被稠密等离子体（辐射X射线）包围的超大质量黑洞的几个引力半径内。中微子由质子与0.1~1 keV X射线在Δ共振上通过光致产生反应p+γ→Δ→n+π⁺产生，且特征中微子通量与黑洞~10个引力半径内的X射线通量线性相关。文章推测这类γ射线遮蔽源可能是河外弥散中微子通量和宇宙射线的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的活动星系核中微子产生机制通常将发射区定位于喷流或较远区域，但基于IceCube对多个活动星系的观测证据，作者希望论证高能中微子实际上产生于靠近超大质量黑洞（仅几个引力半径内）的致密等离子体区域，其中X射线既作为光致产生反应的目标光子，又吸收中性π介子衰变产生的伽马射线。

Method: 作者通过对多个活动星系核（NGC 1068、TXS 0506+056、NGC 4151、CGCG 420-015、NGC 7469、Circinus Galaxy）的多信使数据（中微子与X射线数据）进行综合分析，论证中微子产生于黑洞邻近区域。具体机制为质子与0.1~1 keV的X射线光子通过Δ共振进行光致产生反应（p+γ→Δ→n+π⁺），产生数十TeV及以上的中微子。X射线同时作为目标光子并吸收同源π⁰衰变产生的伽马射线，从而解释这些源在伽马射线波段的表现。

Result: 分析结果表明，这些伽马射线遮蔽源的特征中微子通量与黑洞~10个引力半径内的X射线通量呈线性比例关系，且更小的产生半径（更靠近视界）更受数据支持。这些源有望解释河外弥散中微子通量和宇宙射线的来源。

Conclusion: 作者推测活动星系核的中微子产生区域位于超大质量黑洞附近的致密X射线辐射等离子体中，这类伽马射线遮蔽源可能是河外弥散中微子通量及宇宙射线的贡献者，为理解活动星系核的高能中微子产生机制提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Based on the observation of the active galaxies NGC 1068 and TXS 0506+056, and on additional evidence for the sources NGC 4151, CGCG 420-015, NGC 7469, and the Circinus Galaxy emerging from IceCube data, we make the case for the production of high-energy neutrinos within a few gravitational radii of supermassive black holes surrounded by a dense plasma radiating X-rays. X-rays represents the target for the production of neutrinos by protons accelerated near the black hole; they also absorb the gamma rays from the decay of neutral pions produced in the same interactions. Neutrinos with energies of tens of TeV and above originate in photoproduction interactions with X-rays of $0.1 \sim 1$\,keV energy on the $Δ$ resonance, $p + γ\rightarrow Δ\rightarrow n + π^+$. Our analysis of the multimessenger data points to gamma-ray-obscured sources with a characteristic neutrino flux linearly proportional to the X-ray flux originating within $\sim 10$ gravitational radii of the black holes, with lower values preferred. We speculate on such sources producing the diffuse flux of neutrinos and cosmic rays of extragalactic origin.

</details>


### [143] [Distinct Jet Properties in the X-Ray-Obscured State of GRS 1915+105](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27688)
*Xi Yan,Lang Cui,Sergei Trushkin,Shuangjing Xu,Wu Jiang,Zhen Yan,Sándor Frey,Timur Mufakharov,Ruchika Dhaka*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文报告了GRS 1915+105在2019年转入X射线遮蔽状态后，于2025年通过东亚VLBI网（EAVN）6.7 GHz观测到的两次射电耀发事件。观测揭示了两种截然不同的喷流形态：一次显示亮核伴随延伸喷流结构，另一次显示两个对称分离的喷流团块且无可探测核。未检测到显著喷流运动，推导的喷流速度$βΓ\lesssim0.40$远低于遮蔽前状态下的相对论性喷流速度（$βΓ\gtrsim1$），支持X射线双星在遮蔽和未遮蔽状态下喷流可能具有不同传播特性的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRS 1915+105是已知的微类星体，2019年从长期未遮蔽状态转入X射线遮蔽状态后，其喷流行为是否发生根本性变化尚不清楚。本文旨在通过高分辨率VLBI观测获取遮蔽状态下的喷流运动学和形态学信息，检验遮蔽态喷流是否与历史未遮蔽态喷流具有相同的相对论性传播特性。

Method: 使用东亚VLBI网络（EAVN）在6.7 GHz进行两次高分辨率射电干涉成像观测，结合RATAN-600射电望远镜在2.3-11.2 GHz的频谱监测以描述耀发的光变和光谱演化；通过多历元成像分析喷流形态和角位移，并推导喷流速度约束。两次观测间隔约5小时，若存在历史观测到的视超光速运动，应能检测到显著角位移。

Result: 两次观测分别捕捉到两种截然不同的喷流形态：第一次耀发从光学厚演化到光学薄谱，显示亮核+延伸喷流结构；第二次耀发在低频率显示光学薄发射，显示两个对称、明亮、分离良好的喷流团块但无可探测核。两次观测均未检测到显著喷流运动，推导出喷流速度$βΓ\lesssim0.40$，明显低于遮蔽前状态的$βΓ\gtrsim1$。

Conclusion: GRS 1915+105在遮蔽状态下发射的喷流速度显著低于其未遮蔽状态下的相对论性喷流速度，结合近期报道的喷流取向大变化，为X射线双星在遮蔽和未遮蔽状态下喷流具有不同传播特性的新兴范式提供了有力支持。

Abstract: GRS 1915+105 has remained in an X-ray-obscured state since its transition from a long-lasting unobscured state in 2019. We report on 6.7-GHz East Asia VLBI Network observations of GRS 1915+105 obtained during strong radio flares detected at 2.3--11.2 GHz with the RATAN-600 radio telescope in 2025. Our images reveal two contrasting jet morphologies. The first epoch, associated with a flare evolving from an optically thick to an optically thin spectrum, shows a bright radio core accompanied by an extended jet structure. By contrast, the second epoch, observed near the peak of another flare displaying optically thin emission at lower frequencies, is dominated by two bright, symmetric, well-separated jet blobs and shows no detectable radio core. If these jets exhibited the apparent superluminal motions commonly observed prior to 2019, measurable angular shifts would be expected over the five-hour observations. However, no significant jet motion is detected. Combined with our derived jet speed of $βΓ\lesssim 0.40$, these results suggest that the jets launched during the current obscured state are slower than the relativistic jets ($βΓ\gtrsim 1$) observed earlier during the unobscured state. Together with the recently reported large variations in jet orientation, our findings in GRS 1915+105 provide robust support for the emerging paradigm that X-ray binary jets launched in obscured and unobscured states likely exhibit distinct propagation properties.

</details>


### [144] [eRASSU J043115.8-711730: The first pulsating symbiotic super-soft X-ray source in the Magellanic Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27730)
*Tathagata Saha,Chandreyee Maitra,Philip Charles,Frank Haberl,David Kaltenbrunner,Andrej Udalski,David Buckley,Itumenleng Monageng,Lee Townsend,Manami Sasaki,Sara Saeedi,Mara Salvato*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对麦哲伦桥中发现的首个共生源候选体J0431-71进行了深入研究，利用XMM-Newton和SALT光谱、长期光学红外测光数据，确认其为高吸积率白矮星通过洛希瓣溢流吸积的共生系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦哲伦桥恒星族是大小麦哲伦云潮汐相互作用的遗迹，全面理解其演化需要探测致密天体残骸（在光学波段不可见）。eROSITA全天巡天发现了桥区大量致密天体驱动系统，但需要更深入的后续研究来表征这些源的物理性质。

Method: 结合eROSITA发现的候选超软X射线源J0431-71，使用XMM-Newton进行X射线光谱分析，SALT进行光学光谱观测，并利用OGLE、ATLAS、ASAS-SN、WISE和GAIA数据进行长期光学红外测光监测。

Result: J0431-71是一个高度变化的超软X射线源，具有：kT~30eV的热X射线光谱、亮态光度3.2×10^37 erg/s；巴尔默发射线、[Fe X]日冕线、HeII发射和Bowen荧光；~500-560天的光学红外周期性并与X射线紫外发射相位一致；~520天周期的"越红越亮"趋势表明供体星脉动。观测性质与高吸积率白矮星经洛希瓣溢流吸积一致，使其成为桥区首个共生源。

Conclusion: J0431-71的观测证据支持其为高吸积率白矮星系统，通过洛希瓣溢流吸积，被认定为麦哲伦桥中发现的第一个共生源，为理解该区域恒星演化和致密天体种群提供了重要线索。

Abstract: The Magellanic Bridge stellar population is a relic of the tidal interaction between the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds. A comprehensive view of the evolution of the Bridge stellar population requires probing the compact remnants of stellar evolution, otherwise hidden at optical wavelengths. The all-sky survey conducted by the eROSITA instrument on-board the Spectrum Roentgen Gamma observatory has discovered a significant population of compact-object-powered systems in the Bridge using X-ray. The candidate super-soft source eRASSU J043115.8-711730 (hereafter J0431-71) was discovered as a part of this campaign, and we present here a deeper study of this source using XMM-Newton and SALT spectroscopy, long-term optical-infrared photometry using OGLE, ATLAS, ASAS-SN, WISE, and GAIA data. J0431-71 is a highly variable super-soft X-ray source, classified as a red giant with the GAIA color-magnitude diagram. The source exhibits: (a) a thermal X-ray spectrum with a temperature of kT$\sim$30 eV and a bright state luminosity of $3.2\times10^37$~erg~s$^{-1}$ in the 0.15-1 keV band, (b) Balmer emission lines, [Fe X] coronal line, HeII emission, and the Bowen fluorescence blend, (c) an optical and infrared periodicity of $\sim$500-560 days in phase with the X-ray-UV emission, (d) a 'redder-when-brighter' trend in the stellar emission with a $\sim$520 day period indicating a pulsating donor star. We argue that the observed spectral and temporal properties in J0431-71 are consistent with a high-accretion rate onto a white-dwarf via Roche-lobe overflow, making J0431-71 the first symbiotic source discovered in the Bridge.

</details>


### [145] [Comprehensive X-ray Study of Giant Radio Galaxies with XMM-Newton and Chandra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27754)
*Niraj Maurya,Satish S. Sonkamble,Suraj Dhiwar,M. B. Pandge,S. Ilani Loubser,Avinash Kale*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用XMM-Newton和Chandra的归档数据对27个巨射电星系（GRGs）进行了系统的X射线光谱分析，发现约44%的样本存在内禀吸收，多个源呈现窄Fe Kα发射线，光子指数中位数约为1.6。核X射线光度与延展射电功率的比较表明许多源拥有较强的X射线核，暗示可能存在重启的核活动；对10个有黑洞质量数据的源初步探索了黑洞质量、X射线光度、爱丁顿比与光子指数之间的关系，发现M_BH与L_2-10keV之间存在正相关趋势，而爱丁顿比与质量无明显依赖，λ_Edd与Γ之间呈现初步负相关，暗示低效率吸积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨射电星系（GRGs）是射电星系中尺度最大的一类，其核活动状态、吸积模式与长期演化机制尚未被充分理解。本研究旨在通过系统性的X射线光谱分析，揭示GRGs的核环境、吸收性质、活动状态以及核-瓣连接，从而深化对这类极端射电星系理解。

Method: 对27个GRGs的XMM-Newton和Chandra归档数据进行统一的X射线光谱拟合，分析内禀吸收、Fe Kα发射线、软X射线特征（热等离子体和离化吸收体），测量光子指数和核X射线光度，并结合已有射电功率和黑洞质量数据开展相关性与比例关系分析。

Result: 约44%的样本存在内禀吸收（N_H,int ≳ 10^21 cm^-2），多个源呈现等效宽度高达~570 eV的窄Fe Kα发射线。软X射线特征普遍存在，包括热等离子体发射（kT≈0.2-0.8 keV）和高速度外流的离化吸收体。光子指数中位数Γ≈1.6，与射电强AGN一致。核X射线光度与延展射电功率比较显示许多源拥有相对强的X射线核，暗示重启的核活动。在10个有黑洞质量数据的源中，M_BH与L_2-10keV呈正相关趋势，爱丁顿比与质量无明确依赖，λ_Edd与Γ之间存在初步负相关。

Conclusion: GRGs呈现出多样化的核条件和吸积状态，支持遮蔽、间歇性或重启活动以及低效率吸积共同塑造其长期演化的图景。现有结果为进一步开展更大样本的统计研究提供了基础。

Abstract: We present a systematic X-ray spectral analysis of 27 giant radio galaxies (GRGs) using archival \textit{XMM-Newton} and \textit{Chandra} data. Roughly 44\% of the sample show intrinsic absorption ($N_{\mathrm{H,int}} \gtrsim 10^{21}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$), while several exhibit narrow Fe K$α$ emission lines with equivalent widths up to $\sim$570 eV. Soft X-ray features are also common, including thermal plasma emission (kT$\sim 0.2$--0.8 keV) and ionized absorbers, in some cases consistent with high-velocity outflows. The photon indices are typically hard (median $ Γ\sim 1.6$), in line with radio-loud AGN. A comparison between nuclear X-ray luminosity and extended radio power shows that many sources host relatively strong X-ray cores compared to their lobes, pointing toward possible restarted nuclear activity. For 10 GRGs with published black hole masses, we explore preliminary relations with X-ray luminosity, Eddington ratio, and photon index. We find a positive trend between $M_{\rm BH}$ and $L_{2-10\,{\rm keV}}$, while Eddington ratios show no clear dependence on mass. A tentative negative relation between $λ_{\rm Edd}$ and $Γ$ hints at inefficient accretion, although confirmation will require larger samples. Overall, GRGs display diverse nuclear conditions and accretion states, supporting scenarios where obscuration, episodic or restarted activity, and low-efficiency accretion shape their long-term evolution.

</details>

Kulkarni,J. Kurpas,E. Kyritsis,R. Laktionov,A. Liu,M. Lorenz,N. Malavasi,M. G. F. Mayer,N. Meidinger,T. Mistele,S. Mueller,D. Muñoz-Giraldo,N. T. Nguyen-Dang,Q. Ni,S. Ok,N. Ota,G. Puehlhofer,F. Pacaud,A. Pandya,E. Perinati,C. Pommranz,G. Ponti,K. Poppenhaeger,K. G. Pradeep,A. Rau,W. Roster,S. Rukdee,S. Saeedi,A. Santangelo,M. Sasaki,S. Sheth,S. Shreeram,M. Sommer,A. Srivastava,V. Suleimanov,J. Truemper,N. Vasilas,A. Veronica,N. Webb,P. Weber,J. Wilms,M. C. H. Yeung,F. Zangrandi,S. Zelmer,X. Zhang,Y. Zhang,X. Zheng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于SRG/eROSITA任务前三次全天巡天（eRASS:3）获得的西银半球X射线源目录，包含近200万个0.2-2.3 keV能段的点源和延展源，以及约1.5万个2.3-5.0 keV硬能段源，并提供了对应的光学/红外对应体分类目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eROSITA于2019年12月启动全天巡天计划，此前eRASS1已发布目录。为了提供更全面的X射线源普查、扩大源样本规模、识别稀有天体种群，并支持后续科学研究，需要利用前三次巡天累积的数据生成更大规模、更高质量的源目录。

Method: 基于SRG/eROSITA望远镜在约六个月周期的三次全天巡天数据，在0.2-2.3 keV主能段和2.3-5.0 keV硬能段分别进行源检测，生成点源和延展源目录。同时通过光学和红外波段的光学/红外对应体识别与分类，生成主样本和硬样本各三个对应体分类目录，并结合Legacy Survey Imaging for DESI等巡天数据进行源属性表征。

Result: eRASS:3主目录包含1,911,744个点源和63,796个延展源（0.2-2.3 keV波段），较eRASS1翻倍；硬目录包含近15,000个源（2.3-5.0 keV波段）。在DESI Legacy Survey覆盖范围内识别的140万个对应体中约88%为河外源。此为DR2目录发布，并更新了eROSITA上限流量服务器。

Conclusion: eRASS:3目录成功将X射线源数量翻倍，提供了覆盖多种天体物理类型的全面X射线源普查。通过统一的光学/红外对应体分类流程，实现了对以河外源为主的高质量、同质性识别，并生成了兼顾完整性和纯净度的优化样本，为稀有种群发现和后续科学研究奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: The eROSITA telescope array on board the Spektrum-Roentgen-Gamma (SRG) mission began its all-sky survey program in December 2019, scanning the sky at an approximately six-month cadence. Here, we present new catalogues of point-like and extended X-ray sources derived from the first three completed eROSITA all-sky surveys (eRASS:3), covering the western Galactic hemisphere, which Germany's eROSITA consortium holds proprietary rights for. We describe the observational strategy, data processing, and analysis pipelines. We also characterise the resulting X-ray source populations. The eRASS:3 main catalogue contains nearly two million sources, including 1,911,744 point-like and 63,796 extended sources, detected in the 0.2-2.3 keV energy band, eROSITA's most sensitive energy window. This volume has doubled the X-ray source content of eRASS1 and provides a comprehensive census of X-ray-emitting objects across diverse astrophysical classes. We also released a secondary hard catalogue of nearly 15,000 sources detected in the harder 2.3-5.0 keV energy band. In addition, we released six catalogues, three for the main sample and three for the hard sample, in which we identify and classify the optical and infrared counterparts of eRASS:3 point-like sources. This approach delivers a homogeneous, high-quality identification of mostly extragalactic X-ray emitters; for example, we estimate that approximately 88% of the 1.4 million counterparts identified within the footprints of the Legacy Survey Imaging for DESI are extragalactic sources. This work has enabled the generation of samples optimised for completeness and purity, while expanding the discovery space for rare populations. The second data release (DR2) of the SRG/eROSITA all-sky survey is a catalogue-only release, comprising the catalogues presented in this work together with an updated version of the eROSITA upper flux limit server.

</details>


### [147] [Thermal or Non-thermal? Diffuse emission in the infall region of stacked galaxy groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27804)
*E. Bulbul,X. Zhang,Z. Ding,T. Mistele,M. Kluge,E. Artis,Y. E. Bahar,K. Dennerl,D. Eckert,L. Fiorino,P. F. Hopkins,N. Malavasi,A. Merloni,K. Nandra,E. Quataert,M. E. Ramos-Ceja,J. S. Sanders,J. Strunk,S. Zelmer*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用SRG/eROSITA巡天观测的827个低质量星系群的光谱堆叠技术，首次在星系群内介质（IGrM）的入流区域（延伸到约2 R200m）探测到弥散发射并测量了热力学性质，发现温度分布、金属丰度随半径变化，并在光谱残差中发现了可能是非热逆康普顿发射的额外成分，推算磁场强度为亚微高斯量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系群维里半径周围的微弱入流区域由于X射线表面亮度极低而长期未被探索，本研究利用eROSITA巡天的大统计功率填补了这一观测空白。

Method: 对来自首个eROSITA全天巡天目录的827个邻近低质量星系群（Mtol<1×10^14 M太阳）进行光谱堆叠，将光谱延伸到~2 R200m（2.2 Mpc），用高斯差分发射测量模型拟合堆叠光谱。

Result: 测得平均温度0.96 keV、温度宽度0.28 keV、金属丰度0.21 A太阳；电子密度从(4.8±1.3)×10^-5 cm^-3（0.7-2 R500c）下降到(5.5±2.0)×10^-6 cm^-3（2-4 R500c）。光谱残差表明存在额外成分，非热逆康普顿模型合理，贡献约30%热通量，推算磁场强度为亚微高斯量级。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了eROSITA探测星系群低密度外围区域的能力，为理解星系群外缘气体热力学状态、非热成分及磁场环境提供了新的观测约束。

Abstract: The faint infall regions surrounding the virial radius of galaxy groups remain largely unexplored due to their low X-ray surface brightness. Using the large statistical power of SRG/eROSITA survey observations, we present the first spectroscopic measurement of the intragroup medium (IGrM) in the infall regions of a large sample of low-mass galaxy groups ($M_{\rm tot}<1\times10^{14}\,M_{sun}$), extending to $\sim2\,R_{200m}$ (2.2 Mpc). Through spectral stacking of 827 nearby groups from the first eROSITA All-Sky Survey catalog, we detect diffuse emission and measure the thermodynamic properties of gas at densities previously inaccessible to X-ray observations. The stacked spectra are well described by a Gaussian differential emission measure model, yielding a temperature distribution with a mean temperature of $0.96_{-0.04}^{+0.05}$ keV and width of $0.28_{-0.10}^{+0.10}$ keV, and a metal abundance of $0.21_{-0.04}^{+0.06}$ A$_{sun}$, consistent with expectations for group outskirts. The inferred electron densities decrease from $(4.8\pm1.3)\times10^{-5}$cm$^{-3}$ at $(0.7-2)\,R_{500c}$ to $(5.5\pm2.0)\times10^{-6}$ cm$^{-3}$ at $(2-4)\,R_{500c}$, demonstrating eROSITA's ability to probe the low-density outskirts of galaxy groups. Residual emission in the spectra suggests the presence of an additional spectral component. While a secondary thermal interpretation requires an unexpectedly hot, metal-poor plasma, a non-thermal inverse Compton model provides an equally plausible explanation, contributing $\sim30\%$ of the thermal flux. Assuming that the additional component is produced by inverse Compton emission from a common population of relativistic electrons, the inferred magnetic field strength would be in the sub-$μ$G regime.

</details>


### [148] [Tidal Disruption Events with the SKA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27827)
*T. An,A. J. Goodwin,J. C. A. Miller-Jones,M. Pérez-Torres,L. Rhodes,X. Shu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文论述了SKA（平方公里阵列）望远镜将通过其微Jy灵敏度、宽带覆盖、长基线干涉测量和快速响应能力，将TDE（潮汐瓦解事件）的射电研究从个例研究转变为群体规模的统计推断，从而推动对相对论性喷流、非相对论性外流、环核介质以及黑洞种群特征的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对TDE及相关核瞬变的研究面临多重局限：仅有少数事件能发射相对论性喷流，大量热TDE的射电辐射微弱或延迟，且对星系核中黑洞群的统计特征了解不足。现有射电望远镜的灵敏度和覆盖率无法支撑群体规模的系统性研究。

Method: 利用SKA的独特能力（50-350 MHz的SKA-Low和0.35-15.4 GHz的SKA-Mid频段覆盖、微Jy级灵敏度、长基线干涉测量、快速触发及并行巡天），对TDE的射电余辉进行宽频段、长时间监测，追踪低频同步辐射转折的演化以刻画CNM密度剖面，并通过高精度定位识别离核瞬变，结合喷流能量学、几何和磁化参数建模进行群体推断。

Result: SKA将把射电量热法从少量近邻事件扩展到体积限样本的非相对论性外流；使离轴和弱相对论性喷流成为常规探测目标；通过低频同步辐射转折追踪CNM密度剖面；以亚角秒精度定位微弱离核瞬变。这些观测将约束喷流发生率、能量学、几何和磁化程度，并测试与变脸AGN及高能中微子的关联。

Conclusion: SKA将把TDE射电研究从逐一详查转变为群体尺度的统计推断，为喷流物理和黑洞种群研究开辟新纪元。作者同时讨论了并合巡天、快速触发和SKA-VLBI的优势，并基于AA*和AA4的预期性能给出预测。

Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) and related nuclear transients probe jet launching, disk formation and circularization, particle acceleration, and the circumnuclear medium (CNM). However, the small fraction of events launching relativistic jets, the weak or delayed radio emission of many thermal TDEs, and the black-hole demographics of galactic nuclei remain poorly understood. SKA, with microJy sensitivity, wide bandwidth, long baselines, rapid response, and commensal surveys across 50-350 MHz (SKA-Low) and 0.35-15.4 GHz (SKA-Mid), will transform this field. Its sensitivity, frequency coverage, and very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) capability will extend radio calorimetry from a few well-studied nearby events to volume-limited samples of non-relativistic outflows. The SKA will bring off-axis and mildly relativistic jets into routine reach, trace CNM density profiles through the evolution of the low-frequency synchrotron turnover, and localize faint off-nuclear transients with sub-arcsecond precision. These data will constrain jet incidence, energetics, geometry, and magnetization, map the CNM through shock interactions, test links to changing-look active galactic nuclei (AGN) and high-energy neutrinos, and identify off-nuclear events associated with recoiling supermassive or intermediate-mass black holes. We discuss the benefits of commensal surveys, rapid triggering, and SKA-VLBI, and provide predictions based on the projected performance of AA* and AA4. SKA will shift TDE radio studies from detailed case-by-case studies to population-scale inference, advancing studies of jet physics and black-hole demographics.

</details>


### [149] [Thermal Breaking of the I-Love Universality for Hot White Dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27873)
*Jinyi Lv,Jing-Yi Wu,Hongji Chen,Kexin Jia,Weihan Sun,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究使用MESA恒星演化代码构建不同温度下0.15太阳质量氦核和0.6太阳质量碳氧核白矮星模型，利用Clairaut-Radau方程提取等密度面偏心率，发现高温会增强偏心率变化并导致I-Love关系偏离零温Chandrasekhar模型，降温后恢复，证明温度引起的普适关系破坏源于等密度面自相似性丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑天体的I-Love-Q普适关系在引力波天文学中有重要应用，但热效应可能破坏低质量白矮星中的这些关系，需要定量研究温度对普适关系适用性的影响。

Method: 采用MESA恒星演化代码构建0.15太阳质量氦核和0.6太阳质量碳氧核白矮星在不同温度下的真实模型，通过Clairaut-Radau方程定量提取内部等密度面偏心率的径向变化。

Result: 数值结果表明更高的中心温度会放大偏心率变化，使I-Love关系偏离零温Chandrasekhar模型；随后的冷却过程则使这些关系恢复。温度诱导的普适关系违反本质上源于等密度面自相似性的丧失。

Conclusion: 该研究确认了温度诱导的I-Love-Q关系破坏的内在物理机制是等密度面失去自相似性，为紧凑天体普适关系的适用条件提供了关键见解。

Abstract: The universal I-Love-Q relations for compact stars have significant applications in gravitational-wave astronomy, but thermal effects can break these relations in low-mass white dwarfs. In this work, we employ the stellar evolution code MESA to construct realistic models of $0.15 \, M_{\odot}$ helium-core and $0.6 \, M_{\odot}$ carbon-oxygen core white dwarfs at various temperatures. By utilizing the Clairaut-Radau equation, we quantitatively extract the radial variation of the eccentricity of internal isodensity surfaces. Our numerical results demonstrate that higher central temperatures amplify the eccentricity variation, causing the I-Love relations to deviate from the zero-temperature Chandrasekhar model, whereas subsequent cooling restores them. This confirms that the temperature-induced violation of the universal relations is fundamentally driven by the loss of self-similarity in isodensity surfaces, providing key insights into the applicability conditions of I-Love-Q relations in compact objects.

</details>


### [150] [High-mass binary black hole mergers from detailed binary evolution models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27962)
*Max M. Briel,Olcay Bıyıklı,Tassos Fragos,Anarya Ray,Zepei Xing,Monica Gallegos-Garcia,Abhishek Chattaraj,Jeff J. Andrews,Michael Zevin,Vicky Kalogera,Seth Gossage,Philipp M. Srivastava,Elizabeth Teng*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用POSYDON双星演化代码模拟了主黑洞质量>40太阳质量的双黑洞（BBH）并合群总体，系统比较了三种吸积效率（Eddington限制、GRRMHD-informed、完全保守）和不同的natal kick强度对并合率、质量比和有效自旋χ_eff分布的影响。结果显示完全保守吸积会产生与观测不符的χ_eff=0.6尖峰和q~0.5的质量比峰，而Eddington限制和GRRMHD-informed吸积配合中等强度的kick可以解释部分高质BBH群，但需要额外的形成通道来解释负χ_eff系统和次级黑洞高自旋的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波观测发现主黑洞质量>40太阳质量的BBH并合事件群，这些事件延伸至甚至超越对不稳定质量间隙，且可能具有平坦的质量比和更宽的χ_eff分布。已有理论难以同时解释这些观测特征，因此需要检验超Eddington吸积（在稳定质量转移中）是否能产生与观测相符的BBH并合群总体。

Method: 使用POSYDON双星演化代码，模拟主黑洞质量>40太阳质量的BBH并合群总体。参数设置包含三种黑洞吸积效率模型：Eddington限制、GRRMHD-informed（基于广义相对论磁流体力学模拟）、完全保守吸积；同时变化natal kick强度，包括在高黑洞质量下施加强kick。

Result: 发现超Eddington吸积不会抑制高质量BBH并合。完全保守吸积在POSYDON中会增加BBH并合数，但产生与观测不符的χ_eff=0.6强峰和q~0.5的尖锐质量比峰（观测优选χ_eff=0.0和更平坦的质量比分布）。GRRMHD-informed和Eddington限制吸积与观测的主黑洞质量和质量比分布相容，但需要natal kicks来填充负χ_eff区域。联合分析强约束了双星演化物理，且不倾向于完全保守黑洞吸积作为高质量BBH并合的主导形成机制。

Conclusion: Eddington限制和GRRMHD-informed吸积配合适度kicks可以解释部分高质量BBH群，但完全保守吸积被联合分析排除作为主导通道。观测中高比例的负χ_eff系统和次级黑洞高自旋仍然需要额外的形成通道来解释，表明仅靠稳定质量转移中的超Eddington吸积不足以完整解释现有引力波观测数据。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations reveal a population of binary black hole (BBH) mergers with primary masses above ${\sim}40\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$, extending into and potentially beyond the pair-instability mass gap, with a possibly flat mass-ratio and broader χ_\mathrm{eff} distribution. We investigate whether super-Eddington accretion during stable mass transfer in isolated binary evolution can produce BBH mergers consistent with these properties across primary BH mass, mass-ratio, and χ_\mathrm{eff} distributions. Using POSYDON, we simulate BBH merger populations with primary BH masses above ${\sim}40\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$, under three BH accretion efficiencies: Eddington-limited, GRRMHD-informed, and fully conservative. We additionally vary the natal kick strength, including strong kicks at high BH masses.
  We find that super-Eddington accretion does not suppress BBH mergers in the high-mass regime. Fully-conservative accretion leads to an increase of BBH mergers in POSYDON with a strong kick-independent peak at $χ_\mathrm{eff}=0.6$ and a sharp mass-ratio peak at $q\sim0.5$, whereas observations favor $χ_\mathrm{eff}=0.0$ and a flatter mass-ratio distribution. The GRRMHD-informed and Eddington-limited accretion are compatible with the observed primary BH mass and mass ratio distribution, but require natal kicks to populate negative χ_\mathrm{eff}.
  A joint analysis of the primary BH mass, mass ratio, and χ_\mathrm{eff} distributions provides strong constraints on binary evolution physics, and disfavor fully-conservative BH accretion as the dominant formation mechanism for high-mass BBH mergers. The Eddington-limited and GRRMHD-informed prescriptions with modest kicks can explain part of the high-mass population, but an additional formation channel is still needed to account for the high fraction of negative χ_\mathrm{eff} systems and high secondary BH spins.

</details>


### [151] [Comparative Periodogram Analysis of 22 Years of Super-Kamiokande Solar $^{8}\mathrm{B}$ Neutrino Data: Classical, Phase-Based, and Information Theoretic Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27979)
*Liangliang Ren,Ze-Lin Zhang,Bing Xu,Tian-Cheng Huang,Ran Wang,Jia-Xin Dong,Yan-Ping Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对Super-Kamiokande 22年太阳中微子数据（1996-2018）进行系统性的多方法周期分析，比较九种算法，并通过层次时间分割分离天体物理信号与探测器系统效应。发现~38.8天周期信号仅存在于早期低统计量数据中，而~24.3天信号被贝叶斯框架确认为季节系统来源，且未发现~11年太阳活动周期调制（上限<0.2%）。GLS方法在异方差不确定度处理上最优，经典Lomb-Scargle系统低估显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳8B中微子是探测太阳内部动力学和中微子电磁性质的重要探针。对22年Super-Kamiokande数据集进行稳健的周期性搜索，需要克服探测器系统效应与低信噪比下频繁主义与贝叶斯统计差异带来的挑战，从而为下一代实验（如Hyper-K和JUNO）提供方法论蓝图。

Method: 采用九种周期分析算法的系统性比较，包括广义Lomb-Scargle（GLS）、经典Lomb-Scargle、Lafler-Kinman、MHAOV、PDM1等；通过层次时间分割将数据分段以区分天体物理信号与探测器系统效应；使用贝叶斯因子（ln B）进行模型选择，并与频繁主义显著性对比。

Result: 1) GLS方法最稳健，经典Lomb-Scargle系统低估显著性，Lafler-Kinman方法普遍失效；2) 在SK-I和pre-2001数据中，七种算法提供~38.8天周期的弱证据，但在高统计量的SK-IV修正通量数据中完全不成立（ln B << -5），判定为早期低统计量的瞬态特征；3) ~24.3天周期信号在贝叶斯框架下被决定性拒绝，确认为季节系统源；4) 未发现~11年太阳周调制，幅度上限<0.2%。

Conclusion: 本文建立了低信噪比周期搜索的多度量最佳实践框架，强调频繁主义显著性与贝叶斯模型选择（ln B）之间的显著差异，为Hyper-Kamiokande和JUNO等下一代观测站提供了直接的方法论蓝图。

Abstract: Solar $^8\mathrm{B}$ neutrinos offer a unique probe of solar interior dynamics and neutrino electromagnetic properties. We present a systematic, multi-method periodogram analysis of the 22-year Super-Kamiokande solar neutrino dataset (1996--2018), comparing nine algorithms. Through hierarchical temporal segmentation, we disentangle astrophysical signals from detector systematics. The Generalized Lomb-Scargle (GLS) method provides the most statistically robust detections by correctly handling heteroscedastic uncertainties, whereas classical Lomb-Scargle systematically underestimates significance. The Lafler--Kinman method generally fails, whereas independent algorithms like MHAOV and PDM1 recover consistent periodicities, providing vital cross-validation. In pre-2001 and SK-I data, seven algorithms provide \textit{weak evidence} ($\ln B > 0$) for a $\sim 38.8$ d periodicity. However, this signal is entirely absent in the highest-statistics SK-IV modified flux data, where the Bayes factor decisively favors the null model ($\ln B \ll -5$), indicating it is a transient feature of the early low-statistics era. Conversely, a $\sim 24.3$ d signal in post-2001 raw flux is decisively rejected by the Bayesian framework and vanishes in modified flux, confirming its seasonal systematic origin. Furthermore, no evidence is found for an $\sim 11$-year solar cycle modulation, yielding a stringent amplitude upper limit of $<0.2\%$ of the mean flux. By highlighting the stark contrast between frequentist significance and Bayesian model selection ($\ln B$) in low signal-to-noise regimes, we establish a rigorous, multi-metric best-practice framework for periodicity searches. This work provides a direct methodological blueprint for next-generation observatories like Hyper-Kamiokande and JUNO.

</details>


### [152] [Impact of Kaon Condensation on the Thermal Evolution of the CCO in HESS J1731--347 Supernova Remnant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28107)
*D. G. Nanopoulos,P. S. Koliogiannis,V. Petousis,M. Veselsky,Ch. C. Moustakidis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了HESS J1731–347超新星遗迹中心致密天体，发现负电K介子凝聚虽然能软化状态方程并复现其低质量和小半径的结构特征，但会显著加速热演化，导致表面温度远低于观测推断值，因此无法在该冷却框架内同时解释该天体的结构与热性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HESS J1731–347超新星遗迹的中心致密天体表现出低质量、小半径与相对高表面温度的异常组合，这种看似矛盾的性质若能在统一理论框架内调和，将为致密物质的物态方程和组成提供重要约束。作者希望检验K介子凝聚这一软化物态方程的奇异相是否能在复现结构性质的同时满足热演化约束。

Method: 在统一的理论框架下，作者构建了包含负电K介子凝聚的奇异夸克物质模型，结合冷却机制（如中微子发射过程）对该致密天体进行热演化模拟，系统考察K介子凝聚的起始对表面温度随时间的演化影响，并与HESS J1731–347的观测温度推断值进行比较。

Result: 研究发现，K介子凝聚的启动会显著加速致密天体的热演化进程，使表面温度降至远低于观测推断范围的数值。这表明显存冷却框架下，K介子凝聚无法同时满足该天体的结构和热演化约束。

Conclusion: 在所采用的冷却框架下，K介子凝聚不能同时解释HESS J1731–347的结构与热性质，这对该天体中是否存在K介子凝聚奇异相及其物态方程选择提出了明确限制，可能需要考虑其他排他机制或更精细的冷却过程。

Abstract: Recent analyses of the central compact object in the HESS J1731--347 supernova remnant suggest an unusual combination of a low mass and small radius, while its thermal emission indicates a relatively high surface temperature at its estimated age. Reconciling these structural and thermal properties within a unified theoretical framework may provide important constraints on the equation of state and composition of dense matter. In this work, we investigate the thermal consequences of negatively charged kaon condensation, an exotic phase that softens the equation of state and facilitate the reproduction of the inferred bulk properties of HESS J1731--347. We find that the onset of kaon condensation strongly accelerates the thermal evolution, leading to surface temperature substantially below the observationally inferred range. Within the adopted cooling framework, kaon condensation therefore cannot simultaneously account for the structural and thermal properties of HESS J1731--347.

</details>


### [153] [The First Measurement of Jet Collimation Profiles in an X-ray Binary: the Case of SS 433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28122)
*Xi Yan,Lang Cui,Zsolt Paragi,Sándor Frey*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用VLBI数据首次直接测量了X射线双星（XRB）SS 433中的喷流准直轮廓。发现接近喷流在1995和1998年数据中呈现准抛物线轮廓；2000年数据虽存在振荡，但表明喷流固有张角从~20°（8×10^14 cm）逐渐减小至~3°（9×10^15 cm），证实了喷流的逐步准直。还测得四个频段的核位置偏移并推导出核心位移关系，使SS 433成为首个喷流准直、张角演化和核位移关系均被约束的XRB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管活动星系核（AGN）中喷流准直研究已取得重要进展，但X射线双星（XRBs）中的喷流准直轮廓此前从未被直接测量过，这一空白限制了我们对不同尺度喷流物理机制统一性的理解。

Method: 使用甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）数据对SS 433的喷流展开分析：分别利用1995、1998和2000年的喷流宽度数据构建接近喷流的准直轮廓；通过频率依赖的核位置测量推导核心位移关系；同时考虑自由-自由吸收效应对退行喷流的宽度和张角进行约束。

Result: 1995和1998年的接近喷流表现出良好的准抛物线准直轮廓；2000年数据中存在局部振荡，无法稳健表征准直轮廓，但暗示固有喷流张角随距离增大而逐渐减小的趋势。同时获得四个频段的核位置及核心位移关系，并给出退行喷流的宽度与张角测量结果（受自由-自由吸收限制）。

Conclusion: SS 433成为首个同时约束喷流准直轮廓、张角演化和核心位移关系的X射线双星，为喷流准直物理机制的研究提供了关键的观测基础。

Abstract: While significant progress has been achieved in active galactic nuclei (AGN), jet collimation profiles in X-ray binaries (XRBs) have not been directly measured. Here we report the first measurement of jet collimation profiles in an XRB using very long baseline interferometry data from SS 433. The approaching jet exhibits a well-constrained quasi-parabolic profile in the 1995 and 1998 data, whereas the prominent local oscillations present in the 2000 jet-width measurements preclude a robust characterization of the jet collimation profile. Nevertheless, the 2000 data suggest that the intrinsic jet opening angle decreases gradually from $\sim 20^\circ$ at 8$\times 10^{14}$ cm to $\sim 3^\circ$ at $9\times 10^{15}$ cm (deprojected), providing evidence for progressive jet collimation. The width and opening angle of the receding jet are also presented, although their interpretation is limited by the free--free absorption effect. In addition, we measure the frequency-dependent core positions at four bands and derive a core-shift relation. These results establish SS 433 as the first XRB in which jet collimation, opening-angle evolution, and core-shift relation are constrained.

</details>


### [154] [Multiwavelength study of supernova remnants in the Circinus constellation with SRG/eROSITA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28188)
*A. Khokhriakova,W. Becker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过在Circinus星座区域结合光学成像、射电连续谱数据和SRG/eROSITA全天巡天X射线观测，进行首次多波长超新星遗迹候选体搜寻，识别出一个新的高置信候选体G320.2-3.6，并提出三个后续验证目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星遗迹是恒星反馈及星际介质化学和动力学演化的关键示踪物，但在复杂区域中仅依赖档案多波长数据识别超新星遗迹仍然具有挑战性。作者希望在Circinus星座区域寻找此前未被认识的超新星遗迹候选体，并为后续确认提供优先目标列表。

Method: 结合光学成像、射电连续谱数据以及SRG/eROSITA全天巡天的X射线观测，对Circinus星座区域的候选超新星遗迹进行多波长联合分析，并根据组合诊断信号的强度对候选体进行分类评估。

Result: 联合分析改进了已知遗迹（如MSH 15-52和G320.6-1.6）的形态和光谱特性约束。新识别出一个高置信超新星遗迹候选体G320.2-3.6，另有三个天体（G319.8-2.0、G321.8-1.1和G320.5-1.8）被确定为具有前景的后续验证目标。

Conclusion: 这是Circinus区域首次多波长搜寻超新星遗迹候选体的研究，识别出G320.2-3.6作为最有希望的候选体，并建议对三个目标开展射电、光谱光学及更深、空间分辨的X射线后续观测以确认其性质。

Abstract: Supernova remnants (SNRs) are key tracers of stellar feedback and the chemical and dynamical evolution of the interstellar medium. However, identifying SNRs in complex regions remains challenging, particularly when only archival multi-wavelength data are available. We aim to search for previously unrecognized SNR candidates in the Circinus constellation by combining optical imaging, radio continuum data, and available SRG/eROSITA all-sky survey X-ray observations, to provide a prioritized list of targets for future confirmation. Candidate remnants were classified according to the strength of the combined diagnostics. This joint analysis yields improved constraints on the morphology and spectral properties of known remnants in the region, including MSH 15-52 and G320.6- 1.6. In addition, we identify a new promising SNR candidate, G320.2-3.6. Our study provides a first multi-wavelength search of SNR candidates in the Circinus region. In addition to G320.2-3.6, three objects (G319.8-2.0, G321.8-1.1, and G320.5-1.8) emerge as promising targets for follow-up radio, spectroscopic optical, and deeper, spatially resolved X-ray observations to confirm their nature.

</details>


### [155] [Estimating amplitude of matter density fluctuations in solar and supernova models using neutrino flavor evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28398)
*Caroline Laber-Smith,Hansen Torres,Lily Newkirk,Dhriti Rathod,A. Baha Balantekin,Amol V. Patwardhan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用统计数据同化（SDA）方法从探测端中微子信号中提取中微子在太阳和核心坍缩超新星中传播路径上物质密度波动信息的能力。结果表明，SDA方法在太阳中微子场景下能可靠提取密度波动幅度，在CCSN场景下在较高波动幅度时也有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子味演化受环境强烈影响，使其成为携带源性质信息的天体物理信使。作者希望探究探测器处的中微子信号能在多大程度上包含传播路径上物质密度波动的信息，从而反向推断源环境的物理性质。

Method: 采用统计数据同化（SDA）框架，针对太阳和核心坍缩超新星中的简化中微子味演化与传播模型，分析探测信号对路径上物质密度波动幅度的信息提取能力。

Result: SDA方法在太阳和CCSN两种场景下均能提取密度波动幅度信息，其中在太阳中微子情形下可靠性更高；在CCSN模型中，当波动幅度较高时该方法同样表现有效。

Conclusion: SDA方法可作为从探测端中微子信号反向推断传播路径物质密度波动信息的有效工具，尤其在太阳中微子场景下可靠性更好，对CCSN场景在高波动幅度下也具备可行性。

Abstract: Neutrinos can undergo substantial flavor evolution between their production in astrophysical sources-such as the Sun and core-collapse supernovae-and their subsequent detection in terrestrial detectors. This flavor evolution is strongly influenced by the environment that the neutrinos interact with, making them useful astrophysical messengers capable of potentially carrying information about the properties of the source wherein they are produced. In this work, we apply the framework of statistical data assimilation (SDA) in order to ascertain the extent to which a neutrino signal at detection may contain information about matter density fluctuations along their path from the source. Using simplified models of neutrino flavor evolution and propagation in the sun and in a core-collapse supernova (CCSN), we find that the SDA method is able to extract information about the amplitude of density fluctuations in both the solar and CCSN scenarios, with the method showing relatively greater reliability in the solar neutrino case. Nonetheless, even in the CCSN neutrino model, the method proved effective at high fluctuation amplitudes

</details>


### [156] [Binary neutron stars in the next-generation era: Multi-messenger detection prospects and constraints on the equation of state, mass distribution, and cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28438)
*Hauke Koehn,Thibeau Wouters,Gilad Sadeh,Peter T. H. Pang,Mattia Bulla,Chris Van Den Broeck,Michael W. Coughlin,Tim Dietrich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文评估了下一代引力波探测器（爱因斯坦望远镜ET和宇宙探险者CE）对双中子星（BNS）多信使探测的预期数量，并通过联合分层贝叶斯推断来约束中子星状态方程（EOS）、质量分布和宇宙学参数。研究发现ET每年可识别约40-100个电磁对应体，联合CE网络可达200-500个；在理想情况下，引力波信号、千新星（KN）和宿主星系红移可将典型中子星半径R1.4约束到~0.2 km，哈勃常数H0约束到~1 km/s/Mpc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代引力波观测站将揭示中子星物质性质和宇宙膨胀历史的关键信息，但目前对多信使探测的预期数量和联合约束能力缺乏系统评估，需要量化ET和CE的探测潜力及其对EOS、质量分布和宇宙学参数的约束精度。

Method: 作者假设局部并合率为106.6 Gpc^-3 yr^-1，考虑两种质量函数（窄分布以1.4 M_sun为中心，宽分布覆盖1.1-2 M_sun），通过模拟的后续观测算法估计ET和CE的多信使探测数量；随后基于识别的事件执行注入模拟，在完全贝叶斯框架中分层约束EOS、质量分布和宇宙学参数，并对比仅用引力波数据与结合光变曲线信息的推断结果。

Result: ET单独每年可成功识别约40至100个电磁对应体（取决于探测布局和质量分布），与CE联合时每年可达200至500个多信使探测；晚时观测还能发现更多伽马射线暴或千新星的余辉。理想场景下可将R1.4约束在~0.2 km内，H0约束在~1 km/s/Mpc内，并能恢复质量分布的关键特征。千新星光变曲线后验对EOS约束影响可忽略，但有助于宇宙学参数推断。

Conclusion: 下一代引力波探测器具备高数量的多信使探测能力和高精度的联合约束潜力，KN光变曲线数据对宇宙学参数推断具有额外价值，尽管其并不显著改善EOS约束。这一结果表明多信使观测在揭示中子星物理和宇宙学方面具有重要前景。

Abstract: Next-generation gravitational-wave (GW) observatories will provide crucial insights into the nature of neutron star (NS) matter and the cosmological expansion history. We estimate the number of multi-messenger detections from binary neutron stars (BNS) with the Einstein Telescope (ET) and Cosmic Explorer (CE), and project the resulting constraints on the equation of state (EOS), BNS mass distribution, and cosmology via joint hierarchical Bayesian inference. Assuming a local merger rate of 106.6 Gpc$^{-3}$ yr$^{-1}$ and considering two different mass functions, a narrow one centred around 1.4 $M_\odot$ and a wide one ranging between 1.1--2 $M_\odot$, we find that for ET, our mock follow-up algorithm results in at least $\sim40$ and up to $\sim100$ successfully identified electromagnetic counterparts per year, depending on the detector layout and mass distribution. In a joint network with CE, the number of multi-messenger detections can range from $\sim 200$ to $\sim500$. Additionally, several more afterglows from gamma-ray bursts or KNe could be found with dedicated late-time observations. Based on the identified multi-messenger events, we perform an injection campaign to hierarchically constrain the EOS, mass distribution, and cosmology in a fully Bayesian framework. Focussing on ET alone, we show how in an ideal scenario, GW signals, KNe, and host galaxy redshifts can constrain the canonical NS radius $R_{1.4}$ within $\sim 0.2$ km and the Hubble constant $H_0$ within $\sim 1$ km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$, while recovering the essential features of the mass distribution. By comparing inference results that rely solely on GW data and those that incorporate light curve information, we find that while KN light-curve posteriors have a negligible impact on the EOS constraints, they can benefit the inference of cosmological parameters.

</details>


### [157] [Uncertainty-Aware Tidal Disruption Event Classification : A Host-Agnostic Probabilistic Random Forest Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28510)
*Vysakh Anilkumar,Sjoert van Velzen,Marek Kowalski,Simeon Reusch*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率随机森林（PRF）的不确定性感知分类框架，用于在大规模光度巡天中识别潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）。该框架仅依赖光变曲线特征（不依赖宿主星系信息），在ZTF警报流数据上提取11个特征，通过留一法交叉验证与XGBoost对比，展现了更高的分类稳定性和鲁棒性，并发现了11个新的TDE候选体及3个被错误分类的潜在TDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模光度巡天中TDE分类面临挑战：确定性机器学习模型在数据质量变化和低信噪比条件下会产生过度自信的错误分类；现有基于光变曲线的方法未纳入测量不确定性，导致输出不稳定且缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出基于概率随机森林（PRF）的宿主无关、不确定性感知的TDE分类框架。流程包括：从ZTF警报流中的核瞬变提取11个特征（包括上升/衰减时标、黑体温度演化、瞬变前变异性指标）；将特征测量视为分布而非点估计输入PRF；通过留一法交叉验证与XGBoost进行基准对比评估。

Result: PRF在模糊候选体的分类稳定性和鲁棒性上优于XGBoost。两种分类器具有互补性：XGBoost在平衡和高精度场景下召回率更高，能有效隔离类TDE源；PRF通过惩罚大特征不确定性的源来拒绝更多假阳性。将该框架应用于存档数据，识别出11个未分类群体中的新TDE候选体以及3个在已有训练标签中被错误分类为超新星或活动星系核的潜在TDE。

Conclusion: TDE可以通过光度光变曲线特征被可靠识别，提供了一种宿主无关的框架。不确定性感知的概率分类器对于Rubin时代至关重要，可以防止确定性模型在低信噪比下固有的过度自信错误分类。

Abstract: The classification of Tidal Disruption Events in large-scale photometric surveys is challenging because deterministic machine learning models produce overconfident misclassifications under varying data quality and low signal-to-noise conditions. Existing lightcurve-based approaches fail to incorporate measurement uncertainties, consequently generating brittle outputs. We present a host-agnostic, uncertainty-aware classification framework using a Probabilistic Random Forest (PRF). Our pipeline extracts 11 characteristic features from nuclear transients in the ZTF alert stream, including rise and decay timescales, blackbody temperature evolution, and pre-transient variability metrics. Relying exclusively on photometric data without host galaxy information ensures effectiveness for the faint transient population expected from the Rubin Observatory. The PRF, which treats feature measurements as distributions, was evaluated against XGBoost through Leave-One-Out Cross-Validation. It yields higher stability and robustness for ambiguous candidates than XGBoost. The two classifiers occupy complementary regimes. XGBoost achieves higher recall in balanced and high-precision scenarios, where its rigid decision boundaries efficiently isolate TDE-like sources, while PRF rejects more false positives by penalizing sources with large feature uncertainties. Applying this framework to archival data identified 11 new candidate TDEs from the unclassified population and 3 potential photometric TDEs within existing training labels previously misclassified as supernovae or active galactic nuclei. This work demonstrates that TDEs can be reliably identified using photometric lightcurve features, providing a host-independent framework. Uncertainty-aware, probabilistic classifiers are essential for the Rubin era to prevent the overconfident misclassifications inherent in deterministic models at low signal-to-noise.

</details>


### [158] [Same explosion, many faces: numerical modeling reveals viewing angle as a driver of diversity for core-collapse SNe in binary systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28519)
*Soham Mandal,Raphael Baer-way,Shazrene Mohamed,Irene Salmaso,Poonam Chandra,Maryam Modjaz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过构建双星系统中的核塌缩超新星（CCSNe）模型套件，研究稳定洛希瓣溢流导致的非球对称星周物质（CSM）对超新星观测性质的影响，发现视角效应会导致光变曲线峰值亮度差异达约5倍，且用一维孤立恒星星风模型解释观测会引入高达50%的爆炸参数偏差和>200%的质量损失率偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量CCSNe的前身星位于双星系统中，双星相互作用会显著影响前身星结构和其周围的CSM，但这一影响对观测到的超新星景观（landscape）的约束仍然不足，理解双星驱动的非球对称CSM如何影响超新星光变曲线具有重要意义。

Method: 使用MESA构建双星演化模型套件，固定主星初始质量16太阳质量，探索伴星质量12-15太阳质量和初始轨道周期>500天的参数空间；从质量损失历史和轨道动力学生成三维CSM结构，沿三条视线方向提取角度依赖的密度剖面，并用辐射流体力学代码Stella计算多波段光变曲线。

Result: 双星驱动的CSM呈现高度非球对称形态，由轨道周期和质量比决定；超新星抛射物与结构化介质的相互作用产生显著视角依赖性，峰值亮度差异达约5倍，晚期B-V颜色变化约1.5星等；用孤立前身星和球对称风的二维框架解释这类事件会导致爆炸性质推断偏差达50%，质量损失率偏差超过200%。

Conclusion: 观测到的相互作用II型超新星多样性中，相当大一部分可能源于双星塑造的非对称CSM和视角效应，这为采用多维方法解释此类暂现源提供了强有力的动机。

Abstract: Observable properties of core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe) depend sensitively on the circumstellar material (CSM) formed by pre-explosion mass loss from the progenitor star. Since a large fraction of CCSN progenitors reside in binaries, both the progenitor structure and surrounding CSM can be significantly impacted by binary interaction. Yet, its impact on the observed CCSN landscape remains poorly constrained. In this work, we investigate CCSNe from binary systems undergoing stable Roche lobe overflow. We construct a suite of binary evolution models in \texttt{MESA} with a fixed initial primary mass ($16M_{\odot}$), exploring secondary masses in the range $12-15M_{\odot}$ and initial orbital periods $>500$ days. We generate three-dimensional (3D) CSM structures from the resulting mass-loss histories and orbital dynamics, extract angle-dependent density profiles along three lines of sight, and compute multi-band light curves with the radiation-hydrodynamics code \texttt{Stella}. We find that binary-driven CSM develops highly aspherical morphologies, governed by the orbital period and the mass ratio. Interaction between SN ejecta and this structured medium produces pronounced viewing-angle dependence in the light curves, with peak luminosities differing by factors of $\sim5$ and late-time $B-V$ colors varying by $\sim1.5$ mag depending on observer orientation. We further show that interpreting such events with one-dimensional frameworks assuming isolated progenitors and spherical winds can introduce biases up to $50\%$ for inferred explosion properties and $>200\%$ for inferred mass-loss rates. Our results are consistent with a substantial fraction of interacting Type II SN diversity arising from binary-shaped asymmetric CSM and viewing-angle effects, motivating multidimensional approaches to interpreting these transients.

</details>


### [159] [Search for gravitational waves associated with high-energy neutrinos detected by IceCube during the third observing run of LIGO-Virgo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28539)
*Matthias Vereecken,Matteo Pracchia,Kara Merfeld,Iara Tosta e Melo,Wasim Javed,Patrick Sutton*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文针对IceCube在高能中微子明亮期(O3)检测到的事件，使用非模型化、定向搜索方法寻找与其相关联的引力波瞬变信号，未发现显著统计信号，并对不同辐射模型设置了下限距离约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能中微子与引力波的联合探测可用于识别其共同的宇宙源（如致密天体合并），但常规实时或目录搜索可能遗漏较弱引力波信号，因此需要更灵敏的非模型化定向搜索来捕捉中微子与引力波的潜在关联。

Method: 采用非模型化(unmodeled)、定向搜索方法，对Advanced LIGO、Advanced Virgo和KAGRA在第三观测运行期(O3)的数据进行分析，针对IceCube检测到的高能中微子事件进行聚焦搜索，该搜索对弱于实时报告或引力波瞬变目录中的引力波信号更为敏感。

Result: 未发现统计上显著的引力波信号；针对不同引力波发射模型，给出了可能引力波源距离的下限（即距离约束）。

Conclusion: O3期间未找到与IceCube高能中微子相关联的引力波瞬变证据，但通过距离下限约束对不同发射模型下的引力波源给出了排除范围。

Abstract: We search for generic gravitational-wave transients associated with high-energy neutrinos detected by the IceCube Neutrino Observatory during the third Observing Run (O3) of Advanced LIGO, Advanced Virgo, and KAGRA. We perform an unmodeled, targeted search, which is sensitive to gravitational-wave signals weaker than those reported in real time or in the gravitional-wave transient catalog, and can thus uncover coincidences missed by typical neutrino follow-up searches. We find no statistically significant gravitational-wave signal and set lower bounds on the distance of possible gravitational-wave sources for different emission models.

</details>


### [160] [Spinning down neutron-star merger remnants with the Tayler-Spruit dynamo: Global simulations reveal the formation of massive disks and neutron-rich ejecta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28556)
*Harry Ho-Yin Ng,Elias R. Most*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次通过全局、长期的广义相对论中微子辐射磁流体动力学（GR-MHD）模拟，在双中子星并合残余物中引入未解析的Tayler-Spruit发电机亚网格模型。结果表明该发电机主要活跃于残余核心的高纬度区域，通过麦克斯韦应力在数百毫秒时间内重新分配角动量，使核心旋转轮廓平坦化，并将角动量转移到盘上，形成更庞大、更磁化、更富含中子的外流物，从而定性改变残余物的旋转演化、坍缩前景、盘形成及多信使特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合残余物的磁场放大和角动量输运对残余物的长期演化、寿命和电磁信号至关重要。磁转动不稳定性（MRI）在外层负剪切区起作用，但正剪切、稳定分层的核心可能易受Tayler-Spruit发电机的影响，而此前模型中尚未纳入这一机制。

Method: 开发了一种新的平均场发电机亚网格模型来描述未解析的Tayler-Spruit发电机，并将其纳入全局、长时标的广义相对论中微子辐射磁流体动力学（GR-MHD）模拟中；采用轴对称设置，从真实的并合残余物初始条件出发进行模拟。

Result: Tayler-Spruit发电机主要活跃于残余核心高纬度区域；麦克斯韦应力在数百毫秒的自旋减慢时间尺度内重新分配角动量，显著使核心旋转轮廓变平，并将质量和角动量从外部残余物转移到盘中；这产生了更庞大、更扩展、更强磁化且低电子分数的盘，导致更富含中子的外流物。

Conclusion: 目前未被建模的Tayler-Spruit发电机活动能够定性改变长寿命双中子星并合残余物的旋转演化、坍缩前景、盘形成以及多信使（引力波+电磁）观测特征，表明该效应必须在未来的残余物模拟中予以考虑。

Abstract: Magnetic-field amplification and angular momentum (AM) transport critically shape the secular evolution, lifetime, and electromagnetic signatures of binary neutron-star merger remnants. While the magnetorotational instability can operate in the outer negative-shear regions of the neutron-star remnant and accretion disk, the positive-shear, stably stratified core may instead be susceptible to the Tayler-Spruit dynamo. We present the first global, long-term general-relativistic neutrino-radiation magnetohydrodynamics simulations of a neutron-star merger remnant incorporating the unresolved Tayler-Spruit dynamo through a new mean-field dynamo subgrid prescription. Our axisymmetric simulations starting from a realistic merger remnant show that the Tayler-Spruit dynamo is primarily active in high-latitude regions of the remnant core. The resulting Maxwell stresses redistribute AM on a spin-down timescale of a few hundred milliseconds, substantially flattening the core rotation profile and transferring mass and AM from the outer remnant into the disk. This produces a more massive, extended, and strongly magnetized disk with a low electron fraction, leading to substantially more neutron-rich ejecta. Our results demonstrate that currently unmodelled Tayler-Spruit dynamo action can qualitatively alter the rotational evolution, collapse prospects, disk formation, and multi-messenger signatures of long-lived neutron-star merger remnants.

</details>


### [161] [A Four-dimensional Model-agnostic Probe into the Astrophysical Origins of Binary Black Hole Subpopulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28622)
*Anarya Ray,Vicky Kalogera*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了对双黑洞（BBH）主质量、质量比、有效旋进和非进动自旋参数四维联合分布的数据驱动重建，识别出四个不同子群并发现了质量区间内的新相关性，为BBH子群的起源提供了模型无关的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波目录显示存在多个BBH子群，它们可能来自不同的演化通道，但强建模方法因理论不确定性易产生先验主导的结论，而灵活方法在高维度下难以扩展，导致丢失天体力学的相关性信息。

Method: 采用数据驱动方法（non-parametric/模型无关方法）重建BBH的四维联合分布，涵盖主质量、质量比、有效旋进参数和有效进动自旋参数，以可扩展的方式在高维空间中捕获子群结构。

Result: 识别出四个覆盖不同BBH质量范围的独立子群，并在这些特定质量区间发现了超越强建模参数化和低维数据驱动框架的新相关性，揭示了孤立双星演化、动力学组装和层级并合等特定子通道在不同质量范围内的丰度。

Conclusion: 研究结果对BBH子群的天文物理起源（包括孤立双星演化、动力学组装和层级并合）提供了新的模型无关见解，弥补了强建模与灵活方法之间的空白。

Abstract: There is strong evidence of multiple binary black hole~(BBH) subpopulations in the cumulative gravitational wave catalog by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration that likely originate through distinct evolutionary channels. The astrophysical interpretation of this complex underlying population is subject to theoretical uncertainties in treatments of binary stellar evolution, core collapse, and host environments. Due to a lack of robust predictions and the sheer diversity of plausible features, strongly modelled population analyses often lead to prior-driven conclusions. On the other hand, flexible alternatives are often difficult to scale in higher dimensions, which can lead to a loss of critical information on astrophysically meaningful correlations. In this \textit{Letter}, we present the first data-driven reconstruction of the joint four-dimensional distribution of BBH primary masses, mass ratios, effective inspiral and effective precessing spin parameters, which yields novel model-agnostic constraints on the astrophysical origins of BBH subpopulations. We characterize four distinct subpopulations spanning different ranges of BBH masses and report new correlations in these specific mass ranges that are beyond the reach of strongly modeled parametrizations and lower-dimensional data-driven frameworks. Our results unveil novel insights into the abundances of specific subchannels of isolated binary evolution, dynamical assembly, and hierarchical mergers across various mass ranges in the astrophysical BBH population.

</details>
